# S7a · Trạng thái từng kỹ năng Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** Implement this plan task-by-task.
> Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Every skill in a child's current skill set gets one level on the 5-step scale ("Chưa đủ bài" → "Vận dụng được"). The level is recomputed from approved evidence only. Levels 1–2 update by themselves. Levels 3–4 and the ⚑ flag ("Cần thầy/cô xem lại") become proposals that the child's tutor confirms in Hộp duyệt → tab **Kỹ năng**. The app never lowers a level by itself. "Hồ sơ cấp" gets real numbers, and the child sees nothing new.

**Architecture:** The rules live in one pure TypeScript function, `computeTargetState` (`lib/engine/`). It takes the evidence of one skill (validated episodes, auto-checked closed items, exposures, earlier mastery decisions, the policy, `now`) and returns `{proposedState, flag, conditions, route}`, plus what to write.

The database has two migrations:
- `0022_seed_ap_0_3.sql` is generated by `npm run gen:config -- --new` from the new versioned policy file `content/assessment_policy_AP-0.3.json`.
- `0023_skill_states.sql` is written by hand and is expand-only. It adds:
  - the policy mapping `engine_policy_map` (catalogue `0.2.1-draft` → `AP-0.3`, without touching the locked catalogue);
  - the append-only book `mastery_decisions`;
  - the derived table `competency_state`;
  - security-definer functions that hand the evidence to the engine (`eng_engine_inputs`) and write its results with every guard repeated in SQL (`eng_apply_engine_results`);
  - the tutor's decisions, the rebuild and the staff reads;
  - the narrowed score-word rule (amendment A);
  - the purge.

A server runner (`lib/engine/runner.ts`) loads the inputs, runs the engine for each skill and applies the results. It runs synchronously, and fail-soft, after every write that can change evidence. A "Tính lại từ đầu" (rebuild) re-projects `competency_state` from the decisions and runs the engine again. The SQL guards make that idempotent.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js App Router + TypeScript strict + Tailwind; Supabase Postgres (schema `english`); zod 4; Vitest (unit tests, plus DB tests on local Postgres 16); Playwright (CI only).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states-design.md` (approved 22–23/10/2026):
- §1 holds the parent's decisions Q1–Q4, 1–4 and A. Item 1 (levels 1–2 update by themselves) is approved under DESIGN §17.
- §2 holds the level rules, §3 the flow, §4 the data, §5 the screens, §6 what is out of scope, and §7 the risks.

Background:
- DESIGN §6 L104–121 (designed tables);
- DESIGN §8 L139–177 (engine interface and required tests);
- skill-map spec `2026-10-21-skill-map-levels-design.md` P3-a / P3-b;
- UX_COPY_GUIDE §3.2.

The branch `feature/s7a-skill-states` is `main` (SM-1, SM-2 and S6 merged; 0001–0021 applied on the real DB) plus the spec.

## Global Constraints

- Everything lives in schema `english`. Never create or change anything in schema `public`. Never run `supabase db push` or `db reset --linked`.
- **Two migrations, in this order:**
  1. `db/migrations/0022_seed_ap_0_3.sql`: written ONLY by `npm run gen:config -- --new --name=ap_0_3`, never edited by hand.
  2. `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql`: hand-written, idempotent and expand-only. Its LAST line is `insert into english.schema_migrations(version) values ('0023') on conflict do nothing;`.
  
  Never edit 0001–0021. Never edit `content/catalogue_v0.2.json`, `content/assessment_policy_AP-0.2-pilot.json`, `content/target_sets_v1.json`, or any locked entry of `content/config.lock.json`. After this slice the next free migration number is **0024**.
- **`english.mastery_decisions` is append-only.**
  - Triggers: `english.eng_forbid_mutation('learner_id')` for update and delete, and `english.eng_forbid_mutation()` for truncate.
  - RLS: on. SELECT only through the policy `english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id)`.
  - No direct INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE / TRUNCATE by anyone, `service_role` included.
- **`english.competency_state` is derived** (DESIGN §6 "projection"). The same RLS and the same "no direct writes" apply. It is written only by the security-definer functions of 0023 and can be deleted and rebuilt.
- **`english.engine_policy_map` is insert-only** (`english.eng_forbid_row_change()`). It is not readable by any app role. It is read only through `english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner)`.
- Every callable new function is `security definer` with `set search_path = ''`. It is `revoke`d from `public, anon, authenticated` and granted to `service_role` only. Routes and pages call it only after `requireAccess` / `requireLearnerPage` / `requireStaff`. Internal helpers are revoked from everyone (`eng_skill_level` stays executable by `authenticated, service_role` because CHECK constraints use it). No `grant … on all tables`.
- **Who decides (D15-style, checked again in SQL):**
  - Confirm, "Chưa" and manual levels: only a **tutor with an active relationship to the child** (`role = 'tutor'` and `english.eng_actor_may_manage`). Any other caller gets `42501 (tutor_only)`.
  - Rebuild: that tutor or the family's parent.
  - The parent reads everything.
  - Students have no function and read no row.
- **Never downgrade by itself.**
  - `AUTO` only raises a level, and only to levels 1–2.
  - Levels 3–4 change only through `CONFIRMED`.
  - A weak result after a level of 2 or more becomes ⚑ (`FLAG`), and the level stays.
  - AI never proposes or confirms a level.
- **Evidence:**
  - Route 1 uses only validated scores, i.e. the current `AGREE` / `MODIFY` decision of (episode, skill).
  - Route 2 uses only auto-checked closed items done in the app, for skills whose skill-map domain is listed in `receptiveAuto.domains`.
  - Never `results`, `tag_marks`, `reading_reports` or `recheck_events` (signals, D32).
- **Thresholds come from the policy row** that `eng_engine_policy` returns (`AP-0.3`). Never write a threshold as a literal in `lib/` (tests may).
- All copy lives in `lib/copy/vi.ts` (rules in `docs/UX_COPY_GUIDE.md`).
  - Text that names the person teaching uses `tutorHonorific` / `tutorHonorificCap`, never a standalone "cô" or "thầy".
  - No level copy contains "đạt" (the SM-2 test scans `levelCopy`).
  - The app is always light (no `dark:`), and never shows the word "TOMO".
- Track is configuration. Never write `=== "A"` / `=== "B"` anywhere, tests included (ESLint `no-restricted-syntax`).
- The student sees no level, no scale, no ⚑ and no score. No student function is added. `tests/e2e/student-no-scores.spec.ts` and the student block of `tests/e2e/review.spec.ts` are extended.
- `lib/engine/*` uses **relative imports only** and never imports `server-only`. Node scripts and DB tests import it (`scripts/lib/enginePg.ts`), the same as `lib/config/*`. Only `lib/engine/hook.ts` and `lib/engine/supabaseStore.ts` are server-only.
- Before the PR, this must be green: `npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm test && npm run gen:config`. Then the Opus `reviewer` runs. The PR description is in Vietnamese and says "**SQL cần áp: 0022 rồi 0023** (áp TRƯỚC khi thử preview hoặc gộp; cần 0019–0021 đã áp)".
- Every commit message ends with exactly:

```
Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_015ozhb22xjdX1tG2koXkZk1
```

## Decisions taken in this plan (for the parent)

None of these decisions changes approved behaviour. They fill points the spec leaves open, and the PR description repeats them. Rows marked **§17** are counting details. DESIGN §17 says Claude does not decide those alone, so the parent confirms them before the PR is merged (the same way S6's G1–G4 were confirmed).

| # | Spec says | This plan does | Why | §17 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | §4 "migration `0022`" for everything; AP-0.3 "seed qua `gen:config -- --new`" | **Two files.** `0022_seed_ap_0_3.sql` is generated (the lock compares the whole file with the generator output, so nothing else can sit in it). `0023_skill_states.sql` holds every other change, including amendment A. The next free number becomes **0024**, not 0023. | `scripts/lib/configLock.ts` refuses a seed file that differs from `buildSeedSql`; `scripts/lib/migrate.ts` refuses two files with one number. | |
| D2 | §4 "app dùng `AP-0.3` cho engine qua bảng cấu hình (không sửa danh mục đã khóa)" | New insert-only table `english.engine_policy_map(catalogue_version, policy_version, note, set_at)`. 0023 seeds `('0.2.1-draft', 'AP-0.3')`. `english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner)` = the newest mapping of the catalogue of the child's current set, otherwise that catalogue's own policy. A later change is a new row, never an update. `eng_recheck_days` (0014) and `eng_record_level` (0020, `level_decisions.policy_version`) keep reading the catalogue's `AP-0.2-pilot`. Their numbers are identical (14 days), and S7b moves level decisions to the engine policy. | Expand-only, versioned, no edit to a locked row. | |
| D3 | §4 "Gỡ chặn `validation_decisions_first_once` … nếu engine cần" | **Left unchanged.** S7a writes no `INSUFFICIENT` / `REQUEST_MORE` decision. The engine reads only the current `AGREE` / `MODIFY` of each (episode, skill) and ignores the other kinds. "Chưa đủ bài" means "no validated score", not a decision kind. | The caveat at `0010_review.sql` L117–122 only bites when those kinds are written. | |
| D4 | §2 bậc 1 "có hỗ trợ ảnh hưởng kỹ năng (khai báo, quan sát, bài sửa S6, chế độ PRACTICE có khung)" | A piece of work counts as **helped** for a skill when any of these holds: (a) an `assistance_events` row lists that skill; (b) `attempts.declared_assistance` contains `unknown` (UAT-06); (c) it is a revision (`mode = REVISION` or `facets.revision`); or (d) its mode is not in `independent.allowedModes` (`INDEPENDENT_CHECK`). PRACTICE is "Luyện tập (có gợi ý)", so every PRACTICE step is helped. | One rule for "khung", declared help and revisions. | §17 |
| D5 | §2 bậc 1–2 | Level 1 = some validated score ≥ `supported.minScore` that does not reach level 2, helped or not (an unaided 2/4 is level 1). Level 2 = an unaided first attempt ≥ `developing.minScore`. A score of 0 alone stays "Chưa đủ bài". "Không có cơ hội thể hiện" (NULL) never counts. Baseline work counts for levels 1–2, never for level 3 (DESIGN §8). | The spec table is silent on an unaided score below level 2. | §17 |
| D6 | §2 "≥ 1 bài kiểm tra trực tiếp" | Direct check = `facets.checkpoint` (in-session test) or `episodes.observed_directly`. This is the same set D15 restricts to tutors. It has to be one of the counted level-3 episodes. | PRD D22 "checkpoint quan sát trực tiếp". | §17 |
| D7 | §2 "ngày khác nhau", "trải ≥ 7 ngày", "≥ 14 ngày", "21 ngày" | Every day count uses **Vietnamese calendar days** (UTC+7). Exactly 7 / 14 / 21 days counts. | The parent thinks in days, not hours (DESIGN §1). | §17 |
| D8 | §2 bậc 4 "bài `delayed_check` (≥ 14 ngày)"; DESIGN §8 "cùng chủ đề/nguồn" | The gap is counted from the latest exposure of that skill **before** the episode, from another attempt, on the same topic (`context_id` = template id). Lesson exposures (no attempt) count for every topic. If the topic has no exposure, any topic is used. With no exposure at all, it is not a delayed check. | DESIGN §8 "luyện xen giữa → cập nhật khoảng cách". | §17 |
| D9 | §2 cách 2 (P3-b) | Items counted: auto-check results `correct` / `incorrect` / `blank` (blank = wrong; `needs_person` is never counted), only from first attempts with no help, in modes PRACTICE or INDEPENDENT_CHECK. These are config in `receptiveAuto`: `firstAttemptOnly`, `allowedModes`, `maxAssistanceAffectingTarget`. The review attempt is part of the 8-item / 2-activity / 75 % base. A review = an attempt on a step with `delayed_check`, or an attempt ≥ `reviewMinDays` after the latest exposure of the skill on any topic, scoring ≥ `reviewMinAccuracyPct`. | P3-b says nothing about retries or help; counting retries would let a child repeat a quiz until 75 %. | §17 |
| D10 | §1 item 4, §2 bậc 4 | The delayed and transfer checks must be **two different episodes** (`minDistinctEpisodes = 2`). Each must be an unaided first attempt ≥ `minScoreEach`. `transferNeedsTutorDescribedDifference` is satisfied by the template's `transfer_check` mark (§1 item 4). | One piece of work cannot prove both memory and transfer. | §17 |
| D11 | §0 "mọi kỹ năng của 2 em" | The engine scope is the **current target set** (A-v1 / B-v1, 12 skills each). Tracking-only skills such as A3 "Sửa bài" get no state. Explicit keys passed to the runner are intersected with the set, so an incremental run equals a rebuild. | One denominator for "n/N", and deterministic rebuilds. | |
| D12 | §2 ⚑ | ⚑ is raised when all of these hold: (a) the current level ≥ 2; (b) there is evidence dated after the decision that set that level, within `conflictWindowDays` (VN days, up to today); (c) that evidence is an unaided first attempt below `minScoreEach`, or a counted route-2 attempt below `minAccuracyPct`. Its key is the ids of that evidence, and the same conflict never flags twice. ⚑ is a flag, not a state (DESIGN §8 `NEEDS_CONFIRMATION` = UX "Cần thầy/cô xem lại (giữ bậc cũ)"). | Spec §2 and UX §3.2. | §17 |
| D13 | §3 step 4, §7 "Đề xuất trùng lặp" | One open proposal per (child, skill). The evidence key of a skill = the ids of every validated decision and auto-checked attempt the engine read for it. A new proposal is written only when no proposal with the same key exists, so after "Chưa" there is no new proposal until that key changes. An open proposal is never withdrawn automatically. The tab shows the conditions as they were when it was made. | Unique index `(learner_id, target_key, kind, evidence_key)` + one-open rule in SQL. | |
| D14 | §3 steps 2–3 | When evidence reaches level 3 on a skill still below 2, the app writes **both** `AUTO` (up to level 2) and `PROPOSED` (level 3). | Levels 1–2 are automatic (§1 item 1); 3–4 need a person. | |
| D15 | §4 `competency_state` | `competency_state` holds one row per (child, skill): the state from the latest `AUTO` / `CONFIRMED` / `MANUAL` (by `seq`), the open ⚑ and the open proposal (not answered), and the latest engine conditions and route. Rebuild = delete the child's rows → re-project from the decisions → run the engine again. `mastery_decisions` gets two columns the spec table does not list: `seq` (insert order, used for "latest") and `evidence_key`. It also gets `route`. | Expand-only; ordering by `created_at` alone ties within a millisecond. | |
| D16 | §3 step 1 "chạy ở server sau khi ghi" | The runner runs **synchronously after the write has succeeded and was not a replay**, inside the same request. It is fail-soft: an error is logged to `app_errors` (`engine_recompute`), the user's write still answers 2xx, and before 0023 is applied the run is skipped silently. Triggers: review decisions (S5), "sửa quyết định", observed support, recording work outside the app, any student submission, and S6 "Xong / Mở vòng 2". | The result is on screen right after the save (e2e-checkable); no job queue exists yet (DESIGN §9 not built). | |
| D17 | §3 step 4, §5 buttons | ⚑ cards get two actions: **"Giữ bậc"** (`CONFIRMED` with the level unchanged; closes ⚑) and **"Sửa bậc"** (link to the manual form). "Xác nhận tất cả" posts the ids shown on screen and accepts proposals only. A ⚑ id is refused with `(flag_not_bulk)`. See Spec gap G3. | §3 step 4 "không cho cờ ⚑". | |
| D18 | §1 item 2 | New actions in `requireAccess`: `mastery:read` (tutor, parent), `mastery:decide` (tutor), `mastery:rebuild` (tutor, parent). The SQL checks the actor again. | Same shape as `levels:decide` (SM-2). | |
| D19 | §5 "Hộp duyệt → tab Kỹ năng" | Tab bar on `/review` ("Bài làm" · "Kỹ năng (n)") and a new page `/review/skills` (a static segment wins over `/review/[episodeId]`). The count is what the person can decide (tutor) or what is open (parent). | Keeps the S5 inbox untouched. | |
| D20 | §5 Hồ sơ cấp | Per domain, over the current set's skills of that domain: **Đã vững** = level 3–4 with no ⚑. **Đang luyện** = level 1–2, or any ⚑ (marked ⚑). **Còn cần** = for each skill below 3 with no open proposal, the nearest unmet condition of its next level (the route with more conditions met; then the highest have/need). A skill with an open proposal shows "Đang chờ thầy/cô xác nhận". **"n/N điểm đã vững"** = Đã vững / skills of that domain in the set, shown on each bar and in the detail. | Spec §5 + P3-a "không điểm cốt lõi nào Cần cô xem lại". | |
| D21 | UX §3.2 "Cần cô xem lại" | `levelLabels` and the UX guide use `tutorHonorific`: "Cần thầy/cô xem lại", "… hoặc thầy/cô nhắc", "… thầy/cô đã xác nhận". The 5 level names do not change. | Spec §0 item 4 already writes "thầy/cô". | |
| D22 | §5 "Kỹ năng đang theo dõi … thang 4 ô" | Each row shows 4 labelled cells (filled up to the level; none for "Chưa đủ bài" plus that text), ⚑, "Đang chờ xác nhận: …", "Cập nhật …", and "App tự cập nhật" when the level came from `AUTO`. The child's tutor gets "Sửa tay bậc 1–2" (reason required). Tutor and parent get "Tính lại từ đầu". | UX §3.2 "thang 4 ô có chữ, không dùng màu đơn thuần". | |
| D23 | Brief "e2e additions to existing specs" | The S7a e2e suite lives in `tests/e2e/purge.spec.ts` (the Playwright project that runs last and already makes throwaway learners). It seeds a throwaway TEST learner with three backdated validated checkpoints (`scripts/lib/skillEvidence.ts`) and purges it afterwards. The other additions go into `targets.spec.ts`, `review.spec.ts` and `student-no-scores.spec.ts`. | Level 3 needs work spread over ≥ 7 days; it cannot be produced live in one run. | |

## Spec gaps (asked in the PR, not decided silently)

1. **G1 — threshold of level 2.** Spec §2 says level 2 is a first attempt "≥ `minScoreEach`" (3/4). AP-0.2 has a separate `developing.minScore = 2`. The plan writes **`developing.minScore = 3`** into AP-0.3, so the engine reads one number that matches the spec. If the parent wants 2/4, change that one value in `content/assessment_policy_AP-0.3.json` **before** 0022 is applied: after 0022 is applied AP-0.3 is locked, and a change means AP-0.4.
2. **G2 — levels 1–2 for skills that only have auto-checked questions.** Spec §2 defines route 2 (P3-b) only for level 3. A reading or listening skill that has only closed questions in the app stays "Chưa đủ bài" until it meets all of P3-b. The plan follows the spec literally. One option the parent could choose instead: route-2 attempts ≥ `minAccuracyPct` count as level 2.
3. **G3 — what the tutor can do with ⚑, and whether a person may lower a level.** Spec §3 gives "Xác nhận / Chưa" for proposals and says nothing for ⚑. The plan offers "Giữ bậc" and "Sửa bậc" (D17). "Sửa bậc" uses the manual form (levels 1–2, reason required), which lets the tutor lower a 3–4 skill to 1–2 with a reason. This is a human decision, never automatic, the same way SM-2 P2-c lets a tutor adjust a domain level either way. Confirm.
4. **G4 — level 4 cannot happen yet.** No published template marks a step `transfer_check` (A-W0–A-W4, B-W0–B-W4). Only A-W3 / A-W4 step 9 and B-W4 steps 4 and 9 are `delayed_check`. §1 item 4 says level 4 counts only when the template marks the step, so "Vận dụng được" appears only once a new template version marks a transfer step (content v0.3 / S8). There is nothing to change in this PR. The parent should know before reading "0 kỹ năng Vận dụng được".

- [ ] **Step 4: Extend the schema.** In `lib/config/schemas.ts`, add `import { DOMAIN_KEYS } from "../skillmap/types";` next to the zod import. Then replace the block from `// ---------- Assessment policy (DESIGN §8)` down to `export type AssessmentPolicy = z.infer<typeof assessmentPolicySchema>;` with:

```ts
// ---------- Assessment policy (DESIGN §8) --------------------------------------------------
const count = z.number().int().nonnegative();
const pct = z.number().int().min(0).max(100);
const policyMode = z.enum(["PRACTICE", "INDEPENDENT_CHECK", "REVISION"]);

/**
 * P3-b (skill-map spec 2026-10-21; S7a route 2): "Tự làm được" from auto-checked closed items done in the app, for skills whose
 * skill-map domain is listed. First seen in AP-0.3; older policies have no such block (route 2 is then off).
 */
export const receptiveAutoSchema = z.strictObject({
  domains: z.array(z.enum(DOMAIN_KEYS)).min(1),
  minItems: count,
  minActivities: count,
  minAccuracyPct: pct,
  reviewMinDays: count,
  reviewMinAccuracyPct: pct,
  firstAttemptOnly: z.boolean(),
  allowedModes: z.array(policyMode).min(1),
  maxAssistanceAffectingTarget: count,
});
export type ReceptiveAuto = z.infer<typeof receptiveAutoSchema>;

export const assessmentPolicySchema = z.strictObject({
  independent: z.strictObject({
    minFirstAttemptEpisodes: count,
    distinctDays: z.boolean(),
    minContexts: count,
    minSpanDays: count,
    minScoreEach: z.number().int().min(0).max(4),
    requireValidated: z.boolean(),
    minDirectCheckpoint: count,
    allowedModes: z.array(policyMode).min(1),
    maxAssistanceAffectingTarget: count,
  }),
  transferable: z.strictObject({
    requires: z.literal("INDEPENDENT"),
    minDistinctEpisodes: count,
    needs: z.array(z.enum(["delayed_check", "transfer_check"])).min(1),
    delayedMinDaysFromLastExposure: count,
    transferNeedsTutorDescribedDifference: z.boolean(),
  }),
  supported: z.strictObject({ minScore: z.number().int().min(0).max(4), assistanceAllowed: z.boolean() }),
  developing: z.strictObject({ minScore: z.number().int().min(0).max(4) }),
  conflictWindowDays: count,
  revisionNeverFirstAttempt: z.boolean(),
  receptiveAuto: receptiveAutoSchema.optional(),
  /** Reserved for S7b (domain level-up gate, P3-a). Must stay null until a policy version defines it. */
  levelGate: z.null().optional(),
});
export type AssessmentPolicy = z.infer<typeof assessmentPolicySchema>;
```

`lib/skillmap/types.ts` imports nothing, so this adds no cycle.

### Task 2: Engine building blocks — types, evidence key, Vietnamese days, decision projection

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/engine/types.ts`, `lib/engine/key.ts`, `lib/engine/days.ts`, `lib/engine/project.ts`, `tests/unit/fixtures/engine.ts`, `tests/unit/engine.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AssessmentPolicy` (Task 1), `DomainKey` (`lib/skillmap/types.ts`), `loadContentItems` (tests only).
- Produces (later tasks use exactly these names):
  - `ENGINE_VERSION = "S7a-1"`, `SKILL_STATES`, `type SkillState`, `type Level`, `STATE_LEVEL`, `stateAt(level: number): SkillState`, `DECISION_KINDS`, `type DecisionKind`, `type Mode`, `type Route = 1 | 2`, `type Facets`, `type AssistanceMark`, `type ScoredEpisode`, `type AutoItemResult`, `type AutoAttempt`, `type Exposure`, `type MasteryDecision`, `type EnginePolicy`, `type EngineInput`, `CONDITION_IDS`, `type ConditionId`, `type Condition`, `type EngineResult`.
  - `evidenceKeyOf(ids: readonly string[]): string`. Format `<count>-<16 hex>`, the same as the SQL CHECK `^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$`.
  - `vnDay(at: string | Date): number`; `daysSinceLastPractice(exposures: readonly Exposure[], at: string, attemptId: string, contextId: string | null): number | null`.
  - `projectDecisions(decisions: readonly MasteryDecision[]): DecisionProjection`, with `type DecisionProjection = { state: SkillState; stateSince: string | null; stateDecisionId: string | null; openProposal: MasteryDecision | null; openFlag: MasteryDecision | null; proposalKeys: ReadonlySet<string>; flagKeys: ReadonlySet<string> }`.
  - Test fixtures: `AP03`, `AP02`, `NOW`, `daysAgo(n)`, `ep(opts)`, `auto(opts)`, `exposure(daysAgo, contextId?, attemptId?)`, `decision(kind, toState, opts)`, `input(opts)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fixtures** — `tests/unit/fixtures/engine.ts`

```ts
/** Builders for the pure engine tests (S7a). Every id is unique per call; `seq` follows the call order like english.mastery_decisions.seq. */
import { loadContentItems } from "@/lib/config/versions";
import type { AutoAttempt, DecisionKind, EngineInput, EnginePolicy, Exposure, Facets, MasteryDecision, ScoredEpisode, SkillState } from "@/lib/engine/types";

const items = loadContentItems();
export const AP03: EnginePolicy = { version: "AP-0.3", config: items.policies.find((p) => p.id === "AP-0.3")!.data };
export const AP02: EnginePolicy = { version: "AP-0.2-pilot", config: items.policies.find((p) => p.id === "AP-0.2-pilot")!.data };

/** 23/10/2026, 12:00 in Vietnam. */
export const NOW = new Date("2026-10-23T05:00:00Z");
/** Noon (Vietnam) `n` days before NOW. */
export const daysAgo = (n: number): string => new Date(NOW.getTime() - n * 86_400_000).toISOString();

const NO_FACETS: Facets = { baseline: false, checkpoint: false, delayedCheck: false, transferCheck: false, revision: false };
let counter = 0;
const nextId = () => (counter += 1);

export type EpOpts = Partial<Omit<ScoredEpisode, "facets" | "at">> & { daysAgo?: number; facets?: Partial<Facets> };
/** One validated episode of the skill under test: by default an unaided first attempt (INDEPENDENT_CHECK), 3/4, 1 day ago, A-W1. */
export function ep(o: EpOpts = {}): ScoredEpisode {
  const i = nextId();
  const { daysAgo: d = 1, facets, ...rest } = o;
  return {
    episodeId: `ep-${i}`, attemptId: `at-${i}`, activityId: `act-${i}`, at: daysAgo(d), contextId: "A-W1-VOLCANOES",
    isFirstAttempt: true, mode: "INDEPENDENT_CHECK", observedDirectly: false, assistance: [], declaredUnknown: false,
    decisionId: `vd-${i}`, score: 3, ...rest, facets: { ...NO_FACETS, ...facets },
  };
}

export type AutoOpts = Partial<Omit<AutoAttempt, "facets" | "items" | "at">> & {
  daysAgo?: number; facets?: Partial<Facets>; correct?: number; wrong?: number; blank?: number;
};
/** One auto-checked attempt with `correct` / `wrong` / `blank` closed items for the skill under test. */
export function auto(o: AutoOpts = {}): AutoAttempt {
  const i = nextId();
  const { daysAgo: d = 1, facets, correct = 0, wrong = 0, blank = 0, ...rest } = o;
  const mk = (n: number, result: "correct" | "incorrect" | "blank") => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, k) => ({ itemId: `q${i}-${result}-${k}`, result }));
  return {
    attemptId: `aa-${i}`, episodeId: `ae-${i}`, activityId: `act-${i}`, at: daysAgo(d), isFirstAttempt: true, mode: "INDEPENDENT_CHECK",
    assistance: [], declaredUnknown: false, ...rest, facets: { ...NO_FACETS, ...facets },
    items: [...mk(correct, "correct"), ...mk(wrong, "incorrect"), ...mk(blank, "blank")],
  };
}

/** One practice of the skill `d` days ago on `contextId` (null = a lesson, no topic), from attempt `attemptId` (null = no attempt). */
export const exposure = (d: number, contextId: string | null = "A-W1-VOLCANOES", attemptId: string | null = null): Exposure => ({
  at: daysAgo(d), contextId, attemptId,
});

export type DecisionOpts = Partial<Omit<MasteryDecision, "createdAt">> & { daysAgo?: number };
export function decision(kind: DecisionKind, toState: SkillState, o: DecisionOpts = {}): MasteryDecision {
  const i = nextId();
  const { daysAgo: d = 30, ...rest } = o;
  return {
    id: `md-${i}`, seq: i, kind, fromState: "NOT_OBSERVED", toState,
    evidenceKey: kind === "PROPOSED" || kind === "FLAG" ? `${i}-${"0".repeat(16)}` : null, respondsTo: null, createdAt: daysAgo(d), ...rest,
  };
}

/** Engine input for a writing skill under AP-0.3 at NOW; override any part. */
export const input = (o: Partial<EngineInput> = {}): EngineInput => ({
  targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", domain: "writing", episodes: [], autoAttempts: [], exposures: [], decisions: [], policy: AP03, now: NOW, ...o,
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests** — `tests/unit/engine.test.ts` (Task 3 appends the engine tests below the marker)

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { daysSinceLastPractice, vnDay } from "@/lib/engine/days";
import { evidenceKeyOf } from "@/lib/engine/key";
import { projectDecisions } from "@/lib/engine/project";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, SKILL_STATES, STATE_LEVEL, stateAt } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { decision, exposure } from "./fixtures/engine";

describe("engine types", () => {
  it("5 states in order, levels 0..4, stateAt clamps", () => {
    expect(SKILL_STATES).toEqual(["NOT_OBSERVED", "SUPPORTED", "DEVELOPING", "INDEPENDENT", "TRANSFERABLE"]);
    expect(SKILL_STATES.map((s) => STATE_LEVEL[s])).toEqual([0, 1, 2, 3, 4]);
    expect([stateAt(-1), stateAt(2), stateAt(9)]).toEqual(["NOT_OBSERVED", "DEVELOPING", "TRANSFERABLE"]);
    expect(ENGINE_VERSION).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9.-]{0,19}$/); // same CHECK as mastery_decisions.engine_version
  });
});

describe("evidenceKeyOf (D13)", () => {
  it("is order-free and duplicate-free, has the SQL format, and changes when one id is added", () => {
    expect(evidenceKeyOf([])).toBe("0-cbf29ce484222325"); // FNV-1a 64 offset basis
    expect(evidenceKeyOf(["a"])).toBe("1-af63dc4c8601ec8c"); // FNV-1a 64 test vector of "a"
    expect(evidenceKeyOf(["b", "a", "a"])).toBe(evidenceKeyOf(["a", "b"]));
    expect(evidenceKeyOf(["a", "b", "c"])).not.toBe(evidenceKeyOf(["a", "b"]));
    expect(evidenceKeyOf(["x".repeat(500)])).toMatch(/^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
  });
});

describe("Vietnamese days (D7) and the delayed-check gap (D8)", () => {
  it("vnDay changes at midnight in Vietnam, not in UTC", () => {
    expect(vnDay("2026-10-22T16:59:59Z")).toBe(vnDay("2026-10-22T00:00:00Z")); // 23:59:59 on 22/10 in Vietnam
    expect(vnDay("2026-10-22T17:00:00Z")).toBe(vnDay("2026-10-22T00:00:00Z") + 1); // 00:00 on 23/10 in Vietnam
  });

  it("counts from the latest practice of the same topic before the work, ignoring the work's own exposure", () => {
    const at = exposure(3).at;
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(20), exposure(17), exposure(3, "A-W1-VOLCANOES", "self")], at, "self", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBe(14);
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(20), exposure(1)], at, "self", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBe(17); // later practice is not "before"
  });

  it("a lesson (no topic) counts for every topic; another topic counts only when the topic itself has no practice; none → null", () => {
    const at = exposure(3).at;
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(20), exposure(10, null)], at, "x", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBe(7);
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(20), exposure(5, "A-W2-WATERCYCLE")], at, "x", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBe(17);
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(9, "A-W2-WATERCYCLE")], at, "x", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBe(6);
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([exposure(9, "A-W2-WATERCYCLE")], at, "x", null)).toBe(6); // route 2: any topic
    expect(daysSinceLastPractice([], at, "x", "A-W1-VOLCANOES")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("projectDecisions (mirror of english.eng_project_skill_state)", () => {
  it("no decision → Chưa đủ bài, nothing open", () => {
    expect(projectDecisions([])).toMatchObject({ state: "NOT_OBSERVED", stateSince: null, stateDecisionId: null, openProposal: null, openFlag: null });
  });

  it("the state is the latest AUTO / CONFIRMED / MANUAL by seq (not by array order); REJECTED and PROPOSED never set it", () => {
    const a1 = decision("AUTO", "SUPPORTED", { daysAgo: 20 });
    const a2 = decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 15 });
    const p = decision("PROPOSED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 10 });
    const r = decision("REJECTED", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 9, respondsTo: p.id });
    const proj = projectDecisions([r, p, a2, a1]);
    expect(proj).toMatchObject({ state: "DEVELOPING", stateSince: a2.createdAt, stateDecisionId: a2.id, openProposal: null });
    expect([...proj.proposalKeys]).toEqual([p.evidenceKey]);
  });

  it("an unanswered PROPOSED / FLAG is open; CONFIRMED of a proposal moves the state; CONFIRMED of a ⚑ keeps it but restarts stateSince", () => {
    const a = decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 20 });
    const p = decision("PROPOSED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 10 });
    const f = decision("FLAG", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 8, fromState: "DEVELOPING" });
    expect(projectDecisions([a, p, f])).toMatchObject({ state: "DEVELOPING", openProposal: { id: p.id }, openFlag: { id: f.id } });
    const keep = decision("CONFIRMED", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 7, respondsTo: f.id, fromState: "DEVELOPING" });
    const yes = decision("CONFIRMED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 6, respondsTo: p.id, fromState: "DEVELOPING" });
    const after = projectDecisions([a, p, f, keep, yes]);
    expect(after).toMatchObject({ state: "INDEPENDENT", stateSince: yes.createdAt, openProposal: null, openFlag: null });
    expect([...after.flagKeys]).toEqual([f.evidenceKey]);
    expect(projectDecisions([a, f, keep]).stateSince).toBe(keep.createdAt);
  });

  it("a MANUAL after a confirmed level wins (a person may correct it, G3)", () => {
    const c = decision("CONFIRMED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 10, respondsTo: "md-x" });
    const m = decision("MANUAL", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 2, fromState: "INDEPENDENT" });
    expect(projectDecisions([m, c]).state).toBe("DEVELOPING");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `lib/engine/types.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * S7a skill-state engine: shared types (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states-design.md §2; DESIGN §8).
 * Relative imports only and no server-only: Node scripts and DB tests import this folder (scripts/lib/enginePg.ts).
 */
import type { AssessmentPolicy } from "../config/schemas";
import type { DomainKey } from "../skillmap/types";

/** Written on every engine decision (mastery_decisions.engine_version). Bump it whenever a rule in computeTargetState changes. */
export const ENGINE_VERSION = "S7a-1";

/** UX_COPY_GUIDE §3.2. ⚑ "Cần thầy/cô xem lại" is a flag on top of a state, never a state (D12). */
export const SKILL_STATES = ["NOT_OBSERVED", "SUPPORTED", "DEVELOPING", "INDEPENDENT", "TRANSFERABLE"] as const;
export type SkillState = (typeof SKILL_STATES)[number];
export type Level = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
export const STATE_LEVEL: Record<SkillState, Level> = { NOT_OBSERVED: 0, SUPPORTED: 1, DEVELOPING: 2, INDEPENDENT: 3, TRANSFERABLE: 4 };
export const stateAt = (level: number): SkillState => SKILL_STATES[Math.max(0, Math.min(4, Math.trunc(level)))] as SkillState;

export const DECISION_KINDS = ["AUTO", "PROPOSED", "CONFIRMED", "REJECTED", "MANUAL", "FLAG"] as const;
export type DecisionKind = (typeof DECISION_KINDS)[number];

export type Mode = "PRACTICE" | "INDEPENDENT_CHECK" | "REVISION";
/** 1 = scored by a person (D22); 2 = auto-checked closed items in the app (P3-b). */
export type Route = 1 | 2;
export type Facets = { baseline: boolean; checkpoint: boolean; delayedCheck: boolean; transferCheck: boolean; revision: boolean };
/** One assistance_events row that lists this skill. */
export type AssistanceMark = { kind: string; verification: "observed" | "declared" | "unknown" };

/** One validated episode, as seen for ONE skill: its current AGREE / MODIFY decision for that skill. */
export type ScoredEpisode = {
  episodeId: string;
  attemptId: string;
  activityId: string;
  /** attempts.submitted_at (ISO). */
  at: string;
  /** episodes.context_id = template id (topic / source family). */
  contextId: string;
  isFirstAttempt: boolean;
  mode: Mode;
  observedDirectly: boolean;
  facets: Facets;
  assistance: AssistanceMark[];
  /** attempts.declared_assistance contains 'unknown' (work recorded outside the app; UAT-06). */
  declaredUnknown: boolean;
  /** validation_decisions.id of the current decision for this skill. */
  decisionId: string;
  /** final_score 0..4, or null = "không có cơ hội thể hiện" (never 0). */
  score: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | null;
};

export type AutoItemResult = "correct" | "incorrect" | "blank";
/** One attempt with auto-checked closed items of this skill (attempt_auto_checks joined to the snapshot's items). */
export type AutoAttempt = {
  attemptId: string;
  episodeId: string;
  activityId: string;
  at: string;
  isFirstAttempt: boolean;
  mode: Mode;
  facets: Facets;
  assistance: AssistanceMark[];
  declaredUnknown: boolean;
  items: { itemId: string; result: AutoItemResult }[];
};

/** exposure_log row of this skill; contextId null = a lesson (or no attempt). */
export type Exposure = { at: string; contextId: string | null; attemptId: string | null };

export type MasteryDecision = {
  id: string;
  /** mastery_decisions.seq: insert order, the only order used for "latest". */
  seq: number;
  kind: DecisionKind;
  fromState: SkillState;
  toState: SkillState;
  evidenceKey: string | null;
  respondsTo: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type EnginePolicy = { version: string; config: AssessmentPolicy };

export type EngineInput = {
  targetKey: string;
  /** Skill-map domain of the skill (route 2 needs it); null = not in the map. */
  domain: DomainKey | null;
  episodes: ScoredEpisode[];
  autoAttempts: AutoAttempt[];
  exposures: Exposure[];
  decisions: MasteryDecision[];
  policy: EnginePolicy;
  now: Date;
};

export const CONDITION_IDS = [
  "l1_evidence",
  "l2_unaided_first",
  "r1_first_attempts",
  "r1_contexts",
  "r1_span_days",
  "r1_direct_checkpoint",
  "r2_items",
  "r2_activities",
  "r2_accuracy",
  "r2_review",
  "l4_independent",
  "l4_delayed",
  "l4_transfer",
] as const;
export type ConditionId = (typeof CONDITION_IDS)[number];

/** One condition of one level ("điều kiện đạt/chưa"): stored as is in mastery_decisions.conditions and competency_state.conditions. */
export type Condition = {
  id: ConditionId;
  level: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
  route: Route;
  have: number;
  need: number;
  met: boolean;
  /** Episode ids that support it (links in the inbox). */
  evidence: string[];
};

export type EngineResult = {
  targetKey: string;
  engineVersion: string;
  policyVersion: string;
  /** From the decisions (projection). */
  currentState: SkillState;
  /** The highest level the evidence alone supports (level 4 also needs currentState ≥ Tự làm được). */
  evidenceState: SkillState;
  /** What the skill would show if every proposal were confirmed: never below currentState (never downgrade). */
  proposedState: SkillState;
  /** Route that reached evidenceState (1 below level 3 and at level 4); null = nothing yet. */
  route: Route | null;
  conditions: Condition[];
  flag: { conflict: boolean; key: string | null; evidence: string[] };
  /** AUTO to write (levels 1–2, only upward). */
  auto: SkillState | null;
  /** PROPOSED to write (levels 3–4). */
  propose: SkillState | null;
  /** FLAG to write (⚑, level kept). */
  raiseFlag: boolean;
  /** Key of every validated decision + auto-checked attempt read for this skill (D13). */
  evidenceKey: string;
  evidence: { auto: string[]; propose: string[]; flag: string[] };
};
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `lib/engine/key.ts`**

```ts
const OFFSET = 0xcbf29ce484222325n;
const PRIME = 0x100000001b3n;
const MASK = 0xffffffffffffffffn;

/**
 * Order-free key of a set of evidence ids (D13): "<count>-<FNV-1a 64 hex>". The same set gives the same key; one id more gives another key.
 * Stored in mastery_decisions.evidence_key (CHECK ^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$). Not a security hash: it only tells "the same evidence".
 */
export function evidenceKeyOf(ids: readonly string[]): string {
  const sorted = [...new Set(ids)].sort();
  let h = OFFSET;
  for (const byte of new TextEncoder().encode(sorted.join("|"))) h = ((h ^ BigInt(byte)) * PRIME) & MASK;
  return `${sorted.length}-${h.toString(16).padStart(16, "0")}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `lib/engine/days.ts`**

```ts
import type { Exposure } from "./types";

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const VN_OFFSET_MS = 7 * 3_600_000;

/** D7: day number of a moment in Vietnam (UTC+7, no daylight saving). Two moments of the same Vietnamese calendar day give the same number. */
export function vnDay(at: string | Date): number {
  const t = typeof at === "string" ? Date.parse(at) : at.getTime();
  return Math.floor((t + VN_OFFSET_MS) / DAY_MS);
}

/**
 * D8: whole Vietnamese days between `at` and the latest practice of the skill BEFORE it, from another attempt. With a topic: practice on that
 * topic or in a lesson (no topic); if the topic has none, any practice. Without a topic (route 2): any practice. No practice at all → null.
 */
export function daysSinceLastPractice(exposures: readonly Exposure[], at: string, attemptId: string, contextId: string | null): number | null {
  const t = Date.parse(at);
  const before = exposures.filter((x) => x.attemptId !== attemptId && Date.parse(x.at) < t);
  const sameTopic = contextId === null ? before : before.filter((x) => x.contextId === contextId || x.contextId === null);
  const pool = sameTopic.length > 0 ? sameTopic : before;
  if (pool.length === 0) return null;
  const last = pool.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, Date.parse(x.at)), Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
  return vnDay(at) - vnDay(new Date(last));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `lib/engine/project.ts`**

```ts
import type { MasteryDecision, SkillState } from "./types";

export type DecisionProjection = {
  state: SkillState;
  stateSince: string | null;
  stateDecisionId: string | null;
  openProposal: MasteryDecision | null;
  openFlag: MasteryDecision | null;
  /** Evidence keys of every PROPOSED / FLAG ever written for this skill (D12, D13: the same evidence is never proposed / flagged twice). */
  proposalKeys: ReadonlySet<string>;
  flagKeys: ReadonlySet<string>;
};

/**
 * competency_state from the book (D15) - the same rule as english.eng_project_skill_state: the state is the latest AUTO / CONFIRMED / MANUAL
 * by seq; a PROPOSED / FLAG is open while no row responds to it.
 */
export function projectDecisions(decisions: readonly MasteryDecision[]): DecisionProjection {
  const ds = [...decisions].sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
  const answered = new Set(ds.flatMap((d) => (d.respondsTo ? [d.respondsTo] : [])));
  let state: SkillState = "NOT_OBSERVED";
  let stateSince: string | null = null;
  let stateDecisionId: string | null = null;
  let openProposal: MasteryDecision | null = null;
  let openFlag: MasteryDecision | null = null;
  const proposalKeys = new Set<string>();
  const flagKeys = new Set<string>();
  for (const d of ds) {
    if (d.kind === "AUTO" || d.kind === "CONFIRMED" || d.kind === "MANUAL") {
      state = d.toState;
      stateSince = d.createdAt;
      stateDecisionId = d.id;
    } else if (d.kind === "PROPOSED") {
      if (d.evidenceKey) proposalKeys.add(d.evidenceKey);
      if (!answered.has(d.id)) openProposal = d;
    } else if (d.kind === "FLAG") {
      if (d.evidenceKey) flagKeys.add(d.evidenceKey);
      if (!answered.has(d.id)) openFlag = d;
    }
  }
  return { state, stateSince, stateDecisionId, openProposal, openFlag, proposalKeys, flagKeys };
}
```

```ts
const run = computeTargetState;
const ids = (xs: { episodeId: string }[]) => xs.map((x) => x.episodeId);
const conditionIds = (r: ReturnType<typeof run>) => r.conditions.map((c) => c.id);
const condition = (r: ReturnType<typeof run>, id: string) => r.conditions.find((c) => c.id === id)!;

/** Three unaided first attempts: Vietnamese days 10, 6, 2 (span 8), topics A-W1 + A-W2, the first one an in-session test. */
const three = (d: [number, number, number] = [10, 6, 2], o: [EpOpts?, EpOpts?, EpOpts?] = []) => [
  ep({ daysAgo: d[0], contextId: "A-W1-VOLCANOES", facets: { checkpoint: true }, ...o[0] }),
  ep({ daysAgo: d[1], contextId: "A-W2-WATERCYCLE", score: 4, ...o[1] }),
  ep({ daysAgo: d[2], contextId: "A-W2-WATERCYCLE", ...o[2] }),
];
const NOTES = { kind: "notes", verification: "declared" as const };
const decided = (...ds: ReturnType<typeof decision>[]) => ds;
/** Current state Tự làm được (AUTO 2 → PROPOSED 3 → CONFIRMED). */
function confirmedIndependent() {
  const a = decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 45 });
  const p = decision("PROPOSED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 40, fromState: "DEVELOPING" });
  const c = decision("CONFIRMED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 39, respondsTo: p.id, fromState: "DEVELOPING" });
  return decided(a, p, c);
}

describe("levels 1–2 and what counts as help (D4, D5)", () => {
  it("no evidence: Chưa đủ bài and nothing to write", () => {
    const r = run(input());
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ currentState: "NOT_OBSERVED", evidenceState: "NOT_OBSERVED", proposedState: "NOT_OBSERVED", route: null, auto: null, propose: null, raiseFlag: false });
    expect(r.conditions.every((c) => !c.met || c.need === 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("'không có cơ hội thể hiện' (NULL) and a lone 0/4 never count (NULL ≠ 0)", () => {
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ score: null }), ep({ score: 0 })] })).evidenceState).toBe("NOT_OBSERVED");
  });

  it.each([
    ["declared help (notes)", { assistance: [NOTES] }],
    ["help the tutor saw (frame)", { assistance: [{ kind: "frame", verification: "observed" as const }] }],
    ["paper work with unknown help (UAT-06)", { declaredUnknown: true }],
    ["a PRACTICE step ('Luyện tập (có gợi ý)')", { mode: "PRACTICE" as const }],
    ["an S6 revision: never a first attempt (FR-14a)", { mode: "REVISION" as const, isFirstAttempt: false, facets: { revision: true }, assistance: [{ kind: "tutor_prompt", verification: "observed" as const }] }],
    ["a retry in the same learning family", { isFirstAttempt: false }],
  ])("%s at 4/4 is only 'Làm được khi có gợi ý' (AUTO, no proposal)", (_name, o) => {
    const e = ep({ score: 4, ...o });
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [e] }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "SUPPORTED", auto: "SUPPORTED", propose: null, route: 1 });
    expect(r.evidence.auto).toEqual([e.episodeId]);
  });

  it("an unaided first attempt at 3/4 is 'Đang tiến bộ' (AP-0.3 developing.minScore = minScoreEach, G1); at 2/4 it is level 1", () => {
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ score: 3 })] }))).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "DEVELOPING", auto: "DEVELOPING" });
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ score: 2 })] }))).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "SUPPORTED", auto: "SUPPORTED" });
  });

  it("hadHelp: unaided INDEPENDENT_CHECK is not help; every D4 case is", () => {
    const base = { mode: "INDEPENDENT_CHECK" as const, facets: ep().facets, assistance: [], declaredUnknown: false };
    expect(hadHelp(base, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(false);
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, assistance: [NOTES] }, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(true);
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, assistance: [NOTES] }, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 1, true)).toBe(false); // maxAssistance is config
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, declaredUnknown: true }, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(true);
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, mode: "PRACTICE" }, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(true);
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, mode: "PRACTICE" }, ["PRACTICE", "INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(false);
    expect(hadHelp({ ...base, facets: { ...base.facets, revision: true } }, ["INDEPENDENT_CHECK"], 0, true)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("level 3 · route 1 (D22, DESIGN §8 counting rules)", () => {
  it("3 unaided first attempts on 3 days, 2 topics, span ≥ 7, one in-session test → AUTO 'Đang tiến bộ' + PROPOSED 'Tự làm được' (D14)", () => {
    const eps = three();
    const r = run(input({ episodes: eps }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "INDEPENDENT", proposedState: "INDEPENDENT", route: 1, auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT" });
    expect(r.evidence.propose).toEqual(ids(eps));
    expect(["r1_first_attempts", "r1_contexts", "r1_span_days", "r1_direct_checkpoint"].map((id) => [condition(r, id).have, condition(r, id).need, condition(r, id).met])).toEqual([
      [3, 3, true], [2, 2, true], [8, 7, true], [1, 1, true],
    ]);
  });

  it.each([
    ["span exactly 7 days counts", () => three([9, 5, 2]), "INDEPENDENT"],
    ["span 6 days does not", () => three([8, 5, 2]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["two of them on the same Vietnamese day: only 2 distinct days", () => three([10, 2, 2]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["one topic only (2 vs 1 bối cảnh)", () => three(undefined, [{}, { contextId: "A-W1-VOLCANOES" }, { contextId: "A-W1-VOLCANOES" }]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["no in-session test and nothing watched live", () => three(undefined, [{ facets: {} }]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["work the tutor watched live is a direct check (D6)", () => three(undefined, [{ facets: {}, observedDirectly: true }]), "INDEPENDENT"],
    ["one of the three had help (UAT-10)", () => three(undefined, [{}, {}, { assistance: [NOTES] }]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["one of the three is 2/4", () => three(undefined, [{}, {}, { score: 2 }]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["a baseline step never counts for level 3 (DESIGN §8)", () => three(undefined, [{ facets: { checkpoint: true, baseline: true } }]), "DEVELOPING"],
    ["a revision never counts (FR-14a)", () => three(undefined, [{}, {}, { mode: "REVISION", isFirstAttempt: false, facets: { revision: true } }]), "DEVELOPING"],
  ])("%s", (_name, episodes, expected) => {
    expect(run(input({ episodes: episodes() })).evidenceState).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe("level 3 · route 2 (P3-b, receptiveAuto of AP-0.3)", () => {
  /** Quiz attempts 30 / 20 / 2 days ago (each 3 right + 1 wrong = 75 %), on 3 activities; exposures as S4 writes them. */
  function readingSet(o: { a1Ago?: number; lastPracticeAgo?: number; a1?: AutoOpts; a2?: AutoOpts; review?: AutoOpts } = {}) {
    const a1Ago = o.a1Ago ?? 30;
    const a2Ago = o.lastPracticeAgo ?? 20;
    const a1 = auto({ daysAgo: a1Ago, correct: 3, wrong: 1, activityId: "quiz-1", ...o.a1 });
    const a2 = auto({ daysAgo: a2Ago, correct: 3, wrong: 1, activityId: "quiz-2", ...o.a2 });
    const r = auto({ daysAgo: 2, correct: 3, wrong: 1, activityId: "quiz-3", ...o.review });
    const exposures: Exposure[] = [
      exposure(a1Ago, "A-W1-VOLCANOES", a1.attemptId),
      exposure(a2Ago, "A-W2-WATERCYCLE", a2.attemptId),
      exposure(2, "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES", r.attemptId),
    ];
    return input({ targetKey: "A-T1-TEXT-R", domain: "reading", autoAttempts: [a1, a2, r], exposures });
  }
  /** Exactly 8 items, all right (to show that dropping one attempt breaks "≥ 8 câu"). */
  const eight = (a2: AutoOpts = {}) => readingSet({ a1: { correct: 3, wrong: 0 }, a2: { correct: 3, wrong: 0, ...a2 }, review: { correct: 2, wrong: 0 } });

  it("12 items on 3 activities, exactly 75 %, a review 18 days after the last practice at 75 % → PROPOSED 'Tự làm được' by route 2", () => {
    const r = run(readingSet());
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "INDEPENDENT", route: 2, auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT" });
    expect(["r2_items", "r2_activities", "r2_accuracy", "r2_review"].map((id) => [condition(r, id).have, condition(r, id).need, condition(r, id).met])).toEqual([
      [12, 8, true], [3, 2, true], [75, 75, true], [1, 1, true],
    ]);
    expect(r.evidence.auto).toEqual(r.evidence.propose); // the AUTO to level 2 cites the route-2 evidence (D14)
  });

  it.each([
    ["7 items is not enough", () => readingSet({ a1: { correct: 2, wrong: 0 }, a2: { correct: 2, wrong: 0 }, review: { correct: 3, wrong: 0 } }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["exactly 8 items is enough", () => eight(), "INDEPENDENT"],
    ["one activity only", () => readingSet({ a1: { activityId: "quiz-1" }, a2: { activityId: "quiz-1" }, review: { activityId: "quiz-1" } }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a blank answer counts as wrong: 9/13 = 69 %", () => readingSet({ a2: { blank: 1 } }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a review exactly 14 days after the last practice counts", () => readingSet({ a1Ago: 20, lastPracticeAgo: 16 }), "INDEPENDENT"],
    ["13 days does not", () => readingSet({ a1Ago: 20, lastPracticeAgo: 15 }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a delayed_check step is a review without the 14-day gap", () => readingSet({ a1Ago: 20, lastPracticeAgo: 3, review: { facets: { delayedCheck: true } } }), "INDEPENDENT"],
    ["a review below 70 % is not a review", () => readingSet({ a1: { correct: 4, wrong: 0 }, a2: { correct: 4, wrong: 0 }, review: { correct: 2, wrong: 1 } }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a retry is not counted", () => eight({ isFirstAttempt: false }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["help on the attempt is not counted", () => eight({ assistance: [{ kind: "external_declared", verification: "declared" }] }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["unknown help is not counted", () => eight({ declaredUnknown: true }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a revision is not counted", () => eight({ mode: "REVISION" }), "NOT_OBSERVED"],
    ["a PRACTICE step is counted (receptiveAuto.allowedModes)", () => eight({ mode: "PRACTICE" }), "INDEPENDENT"],
  ])("%s", (_name, make, expected) => {
    expect(run(make()).evidenceState).toBe(expected);
  });

  it("a writing skill never uses route 2, and neither does AP-0.2 (no receptiveAuto)", () => {
    const writing = run({ ...readingSet(), domain: "writing" });
    expect(writing.evidenceState).toBe("NOT_OBSERVED");
    expect(conditionIds(writing).some((id) => id.startsWith("r2_"))).toBe(false);
    const old = run({ ...readingSet(), policy: AP02 });
    expect(old.evidenceState).toBe("NOT_OBSERVED");
    expect(conditionIds(old).some((id) => id.startsWith("r2_"))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("when both routes are met, route 1 is reported and cited", () => {
    const eps = three();
    const r = run({ ...readingSet(), episodes: eps });
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ route: 1, propose: "INDEPENDENT" });
    expect(r.evidence.propose).toEqual(ids(eps));
  });
});

describe("level 4 'Vận dụng được' (§1 item 4, D8, D10)", () => {
  /** Current Tự làm được; a delayed check on A-W3 (3 days ago) and a transfer check on a new topic (2 days ago), both 3/4 unaided. */
  function level4(o: { practiceAgo?: number; extra?: Exposure[]; decisions?: ReturnType<typeof confirmedIndependent> } = {}) {
    const d = ep({ daysAgo: 3, contextId: "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES", facets: { delayedCheck: true, checkpoint: true } });
    const t = ep({ daysAgo: 2, contextId: "A-W5-NEWTOPIC", facets: { transferCheck: true } });
    const exposures = [exposure(o.practiceAgo ?? 17, "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES"), exposure(3, "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES", d.attemptId), exposure(2, "A-W5-NEWTOPIC", t.attemptId), ...(o.extra ?? [])];
    return { d, t, inp: input({ episodes: [d, t], decisions: o.decisions ?? confirmedIndependent(), exposures }) };
  }

  it("Tự làm được + a delayed check exactly 14 days after the last practice on that topic + a transfer check → PROPOSED 'Vận dụng được'", () => {
    const { d, t, inp } = level4();
    const r = run(inp);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ currentState: "INDEPENDENT", evidenceState: "TRANSFERABLE", proposedState: "TRANSFERABLE", route: 1, auto: null, propose: "TRANSFERABLE" });
    expect(r.evidence.propose).toEqual([d.episodeId, t.episodeId]);
  });

  it.each([
    ["13 days after the last practice on that topic is not a delayed check", { practiceAgo: 16 }],
    ["a lesson in between (no topic) restarts the gap", { extra: [exposure(10, null)] }],
  ])("%s", (_name, o) => {
    const r = run(level4(o).inp);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "DEVELOPING", proposedState: "INDEPENDENT", propose: null });
  });

  it("practice on ANOTHER topic does not restart the gap when the topic itself has practice", () => {
    expect(run(level4({ extra: [exposure(5, "A-W1-VOLCANOES")] }).inp).propose).toBe("TRANSFERABLE");
  });

  it("one episode marked both delayed and transfer is not enough: two different episodes (minDistinctEpisodes)", () => {
    const both = ep({ daysAgo: 3, contextId: "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES", facets: { delayedCheck: true, transferCheck: true } });
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [both], decisions: confirmedIndependent(), exposures: [exposure(17, "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES")] }));
    expect(condition(r, "l4_delayed").met).toBe(true);
    expect(condition(r, "l4_transfer").met).toBe(false);
    expect(r.propose).toBeNull();
  });

  it("without a confirmed 'Tự làm được' the same evidence never proposes level 4", () => {
    const r = run(level4({ decisions: decided(decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 45 })) }).inp);
    expect(condition(r, "l4_independent").met).toBe(false);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ evidenceState: "DEVELOPING", propose: null, auto: null });
  });
});

describe("never downgrade by itself (§0 item 4)", () => {
  it("a confirmed 'Tự làm được' stays when later evidence only shows help", () => {
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [ep({ mode: "PRACTICE", score: 4 })], decisions: confirmedIndependent() }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ currentState: "INDEPENDENT", evidenceState: "SUPPORTED", proposedState: "INDEPENDENT", auto: null, propose: null, raiseFlag: false });
  });

  it("a level a person set by hand is not lowered or raised by AUTO below it", () => {
    const manual = decided(decision("MANUAL", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 5, fromState: "INDEPENDENT" }));
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ assistance: [NOTES] })], decisions: manual }))).toMatchObject({ proposedState: "DEVELOPING", auto: null, propose: null });
  });
});

describe("⚑ conflict window (D12)", () => {
  const developingSince = (d: number) => decided(decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: d }));

  it("an unaided first attempt below 3/4 after the level, within 21 days → ⚑, the level stays", () => {
    const low = ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2 });
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [low], decisions: developingSince(10) }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ proposedState: "DEVELOPING", raiseFlag: true, auto: null, propose: null });
    expect(r.flag).toMatchObject({ conflict: true, evidence: [low.episodeId] });
    expect(r.flag.key).toBe(evidenceKeyOf([low.decisionId]));
  });

  it("exactly 21 Vietnamese days counts, 22 does not", () => {
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 21, score: 2 })], decisions: developingSince(30) })).raiseFlag).toBe(true);
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 22, score: 2 })], decisions: developingSince(30) })).raiseFlag).toBe(false);
  });

  it.each([
    ["the low result is older than the level", () => input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 12, score: 2 })], decisions: developingSince(10) })],
    ["the low result had help", () => input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2, mode: "PRACTICE" })], decisions: developingSince(10) })],
    ["a baseline step", () => input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2, facets: { baseline: true } })], decisions: developingSince(10) })],
    ["the skill is only at level 1", () => input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2 })], decisions: decided(decision("AUTO", "SUPPORTED", { daysAgo: 10 })) })],
  ])("no ⚑ when %s", (_name, make) => {
    expect(run(make()).raiseFlag).toBe(false);
  });

  // Decisions are built oldest first: `seq` follows creation order, and the projection reads only `seq`.
  it("an open ⚑ blocks a second one", () => {
    const base = developingSince(10);
    const ds = decided(...base, decision("FLAG", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 5, fromState: "DEVELOPING" }));
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2 })], decisions: ds }));
    expect(r.flag.conflict).toBe(true);
    expect(r.raiseFlag).toBe(false);
  });

  it("after 'Giữ bậc' the old low result no longer counts; a NEW low result flags again", () => {
    const base = developingSince(10);
    const low1 = ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2 });
    const f = decision("FLAG", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 3, fromState: "DEVELOPING", evidenceKey: evidenceKeyOf([low1.decisionId]) });
    const keep = decision("CONFIRMED", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 2, fromState: "DEVELOPING", respondsTo: f.id });
    const ds = decided(...base, f, keep);
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [low1], decisions: ds })).flag.conflict).toBe(false);
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [low1, ep({ daysAgo: 1, score: 1 })], decisions: ds })).raiseFlag).toBe(true);
  });

  it("the same conflict is never flagged twice (key guard, also when the ⚑ was closed without restarting the level)", () => {
    const base = developingSince(10);
    const low1 = ep({ daysAgo: 3, score: 2 });
    const f = decision("FLAG", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 3, fromState: "DEVELOPING", evidenceKey: evidenceKeyOf([low1.decisionId]) });
    const closed = decision("REJECTED", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 2, fromState: "DEVELOPING", respondsTo: f.id, evidenceKey: null });
    const r = run(input({ episodes: [low1], decisions: decided(...base, f, closed) }));
    expect(r.flag.conflict).toBe(true);
    expect(r.raiseFlag).toBe(false);
  });

  it("route 2: a counted quiz below 75 % after 'Tự làm được' flags a reading skill", () => {
    const r = run(input({ targetKey: "A-T1-TEXT-R", domain: "reading", autoAttempts: [auto({ daysAgo: 2, correct: 1, wrong: 3 })], decisions: confirmedIndependent() }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ currentState: "INDEPENDENT", proposedState: "INDEPENDENT", raiseFlag: true });
  });
});

describe("one open proposal; 'Chưa' blocks the same evidence (D13)", () => {
  it("an open proposal blocks another one (AUTO still moves level 1–2)", () => {
    const r = run(input({ episodes: three(), decisions: decided(decision("PROPOSED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 5 })) }));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: null });
  });

  it("after 'Chưa' the same evidence is never proposed again; one more piece of evidence is", () => {
    const eps = three();
    const a = decision("AUTO", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 8 });
    const p = decision("PROPOSED", "INDEPENDENT", { daysAgo: 7, fromState: "DEVELOPING", evidenceKey: evidenceKeyOf(eps.map((e) => e.decisionId)) });
    const no = decision("REJECTED", "DEVELOPING", { daysAgo: 6, fromState: "DEVELOPING", respondsTo: p.id, evidenceKey: null });
    expect(run(input({ episodes: eps, decisions: decided(a, p, no) })).propose).toBeNull();
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [...eps, ep({ daysAgo: 1, contextId: "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES" })], decisions: decided(a, p, no) })).propose).toBe("INDEPENDENT");
  });

  it("after 'Xác nhận' nothing is left to propose", () => {
    expect(run(input({ episodes: three(), decisions: confirmedIndependent() }))).toMatchObject({ currentState: "INDEPENDENT", proposedState: "INDEPENDENT", propose: null, auto: null });
  });
});

describe("result contract", () => {
  it("carries the engine and policy versions; lists route-2 conditions only where they apply", () => {
    const r = run(input({ episodes: three() }));
    expect([r.engineVersion, r.policyVersion]).toEqual([ENGINE_VERSION, "AP-0.3"]);
    expect(conditionIds(r)).toEqual(["l1_evidence", "l2_unaided_first", "r1_first_attempts", "r1_contexts", "r1_span_days", "r1_direct_checkpoint", "l4_independent", "l4_delayed", "l4_transfer"]);
    expect(conditionIds(run(input({ domain: "reading" })))).toContain("r2_review");
  });

  it("is pure (same input, same output) and its evidence key ignores order but sees new evidence (UAT-16)", () => {
    const eps = three();
    const inp = input({ episodes: eps });
    expect(run(inp)).toEqual(run(inp));
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [...eps].reverse() })).evidenceKey).toBe(run(inp).evidenceKey);
    expect(run(input({ episodes: [...eps, ep()] })).evidenceKey).not.toBe(run(inp).evidenceKey);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `lib/engine/computeTargetState.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * S7a engine (spec §2, DESIGN §8): the level of ONE skill of ONE child from its evidence. Pure: no I/O, no clock (input.now).
 * The SQL (english.eng_apply_engine_results) repeats every guard before it writes, so a wrong caller can never downgrade a level,
 * write a second open proposal or propose the same evidence twice.
 */
import { daysSinceLastPractice, vnDay } from "./days";
import { evidenceKeyOf } from "./key";
import { projectDecisions } from "./project";
import {
  ENGINE_VERSION,
  STATE_LEVEL,
  stateAt,
  type AssistanceMark,
  type AutoAttempt,
  type Condition,
  type ConditionId,
  type EngineInput,
  type EngineResult,
  type Facets,
  type Mode,
  type Route,
  type ScoredEpisode,
} from "./types";

type Helpable = { mode: Mode; facets: Facets; assistance: readonly AssistanceMark[]; declaredUnknown: boolean };
type Scored = ScoredEpisode & { score: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 };

/** D4: help that touches the skill - a help row, an "unknown" declaration (UAT-06), a revision (FR-14a), or a mode outside `allowedModes`. */
export function hadHelp(e: Helpable, allowedModes: readonly Mode[], maxAssistance: number, revisionNeverFirst: boolean): boolean {
  if (revisionNeverFirst && (e.mode === "REVISION" || e.facets.revision)) return true;
  if (e.declaredUnknown) return true;
  if (e.assistance.length > maxAssistance) return true;
  return !allowedModes.includes(e.mode);
}

const byTime = <T extends { at: string }>(xs: readonly T[]): T[] => [...xs].sort((a, b) => Date.parse(a.at) - Date.parse(b.at));
const uniq = <T>(xs: readonly T[]): T[] => [...new Set(xs)];
const episodeIds = (xs: readonly { episodeId: string }[]): string[] => uniq(xs.map((x) => x.episodeId));
const correctOf = (a: AutoAttempt): number => a.items.filter((i) => i.result === "correct").length;
/** Integer-safe "accuracy ≥ pct %": correct × 100 ≥ pct × total (and at least one item). */
const atLeastPct = (correct: number, total: number, pct: number): boolean => total > 0 && correct * 100 >= pct * total;

function cond(id: ConditionId, level: Condition["level"], route: Route, have: number, need: number, evidence: string[] = []): Condition {
  return { id, level, route, have, need, met: have >= need, evidence };
}

export function computeTargetState(input: EngineInput): EngineResult {
  const p = input.policy.config;
  const proj = projectDecisions(input.decisions);
  const curLevel = STATE_LEVEL[proj.state];
  const helped = (e: Helpable) => hadHelp(e, p.independent.allowedModes, p.independent.maxAssistanceAffectingTarget, p.revisionNeverFirstAttempt);

  const scored = byTime(input.episodes).filter((e): e is Scored => e.score !== null);
  const unaided = scored.filter((e) => e.isFirstAttempt && !helped(e));

  // Levels 1–2 (D5)
  const l1 = scored.filter((e) => e.score >= p.supported.minScore);
  const l2 = unaided.filter((e) => e.score >= p.developing.minScore);

  // Level 3 · route 1 (D22): unaided first attempts, never baseline, each ≥ minScoreEach
  const pool = unaided.filter((e) => !e.facets.baseline && e.score >= p.independent.minScoreEach);
  const poolIds = episodeIds(pool);
  const days = uniq(pool.map((e) => vnDay(e.at)));
  const span = days.length > 0 ? Math.max(...days) - Math.min(...days) : 0;
  const direct = pool.filter((e) => e.facets.checkpoint || e.observedDirectly);
  const conditions: Condition[] = [
    cond("l1_evidence", 1, 1, l1.length, 1, episodeIds(l1)),
    cond("l2_unaided_first", 2, 1, l2.length, 1, episodeIds(l2)),
    cond("r1_first_attempts", 3, 1, p.independent.distinctDays ? days.length : pool.length, p.independent.minFirstAttemptEpisodes, poolIds),
    cond("r1_contexts", 3, 1, uniq(pool.map((e) => e.contextId)).length, p.independent.minContexts, poolIds),
    cond("r1_span_days", 3, 1, span, p.independent.minSpanDays, poolIds),
    cond("r1_direct_checkpoint", 3, 1, direct.length, p.independent.minDirectCheckpoint, episodeIds(direct)),
  ];
  const route1 = conditions.filter((c) => c.level === 3).every((c) => c.met);

  // Level 3 · route 2 (P3-b, D9): only skills of the listed domains, only when the policy has receptiveAuto
  const ra = p.receptiveAuto;
  let counted: AutoAttempt[] = [];
  let route2 = false;
  if (ra && input.domain !== null && ra.domains.includes(input.domain)) {
    counted = byTime(input.autoAttempts).filter(
      (a) => a.items.length > 0 && (!ra.firstAttemptOnly || a.isFirstAttempt) && !hadHelp(a, ra.allowedModes, ra.maxAssistanceAffectingTarget, p.revisionNeverFirstAttempt),
    );
    const total = counted.reduce((s, a) => s + a.items.length, 0);
    const correct = counted.reduce((s, a) => s + correctOf(a), 0);
    const ids = episodeIds(counted);
    const reviews = counted.filter((a) => {
      if (!atLeastPct(correctOf(a), a.items.length, ra.reviewMinAccuracyPct)) return false;
      if (a.facets.delayedCheck) return true;
      const gap = daysSinceLastPractice(input.exposures, a.at, a.attemptId, null);
      return gap !== null && gap >= ra.reviewMinDays;
    });
    const accuracy = cond("r2_accuracy", 3, 2, total > 0 ? Math.floor((correct * 100) / total) : 0, ra.minAccuracyPct, ids);
    accuracy.met = atLeastPct(correct, total, ra.minAccuracyPct);
    const r2 = [
      cond("r2_items", 3, 2, total, ra.minItems, ids),
      cond("r2_activities", 3, 2, uniq(counted.map((a) => a.activityId)).length, ra.minActivities, ids),
      accuracy,
      cond("r2_review", 3, 2, reviews.length > 0 ? 1 : 0, 1, episodeIds(reviews)),
    ];
    conditions.push(...r2);
    route2 = r2.every((c) => c.met);
  }

  // Level 4 (§1 item 4, D8, D10): current ≥ Tự làm được + a delayed check + a transfer check, two different episodes
  const t = p.transferable;
  const passing = unaided.filter((e) => e.score >= p.independent.minScoreEach);
  const delayed = passing.filter((e) => {
    if (!e.facets.delayedCheck) return false;
    const gap = daysSinceLastPractice(input.exposures, e.at, e.attemptId, e.contextId);
    return gap !== null && gap >= t.delayedMinDaysFromLastExposure;
  });
  // transferNeedsTutorDescribedDifference: the template's transfer_check mark is that description (§1 item 4).
  const transfers = passing.filter((e) => e.facets.transferCheck);
  const usable = transfers.filter((x) => t.minDistinctEpisodes < 2 || delayed.length === 0 || delayed.some((d) => d.episodeId !== x.episodeId));
  const l4 = [
    cond("l4_independent", 4, 1, curLevel >= 3 ? 1 : 0, 1),
    cond("l4_delayed", 4, 1, delayed.length, t.needs.includes("delayed_check") ? 1 : 0, episodeIds(delayed)),
    cond("l4_transfer", 4, 1, usable.length, t.needs.includes("transfer_check") ? 1 : 0, episodeIds(usable)),
  ];
  conditions.push(...l4);

  // What the evidence supports
  const met = (id: ConditionId) => conditions.find((c) => c.id === id)?.met ?? false;
  let level = 0;
  if (met("l1_evidence")) level = 1;
  if (met("l2_unaided_first")) level = 2;
  if (route1 || route2) level = 3;
  if (l4.every((c) => c.met)) level = 4;
  const route: Route | null = level === 0 ? null : level === 3 ? (route1 ? 1 : 2) : 1;
  const level3Ids = route1 ? poolIds : episodeIds(counted);

  // What to write (D13, D14): AUTO only upward and only to 1–2; PROPOSED for 3–4 when none is open and this evidence was never proposed
  const autoLevel = Math.min(level, 2);
  const auto = autoLevel > curLevel ? stateAt(autoLevel) : null;
  const afterAuto = Math.max(curLevel, autoLevel);
  const evidenceKey = evidenceKeyOf([...input.episodes.map((e) => e.decisionId), ...input.autoAttempts.map((a) => a.attemptId)]);
  const propose = level >= 3 && level > afterAuto && proj.openProposal === null && !proj.proposalKeys.has(evidenceKey) ? stateAt(level) : null;

  // ⚑ (D12): level ≥ 2, low unaided evidence after the decision that set it, within conflictWindowDays
  const today = vnDay(input.now);
  const since = proj.stateSince === null ? null : Date.parse(proj.stateSince);
  const recent = (at: string) => since !== null && Date.parse(at) > since && today - vnDay(at) <= p.conflictWindowDays;
  const lowEpisodes = curLevel >= 2 ? unaided.filter((e) => !e.facets.baseline && e.score < p.independent.minScoreEach && recent(e.at)) : [];
  const lowAuto = curLevel >= 2 && ra ? counted.filter((a) => recent(a.at) && !atLeastPct(correctOf(a), a.items.length, ra.minAccuracyPct)) : [];
  const conflict = lowEpisodes.length + lowAuto.length > 0;
  const flagKey = conflict ? evidenceKeyOf([...lowEpisodes.map((e) => e.decisionId), ...lowAuto.map((a) => a.attemptId)]) : null;
  const raiseFlag = flagKey !== null && proj.openFlag === null && !proj.flagKeys.has(flagKey);
  const flagIds = uniq([...episodeIds(lowEpisodes), ...episodeIds(lowAuto)]);

  const autoEvidence = auto === null ? [] : auto === "SUPPORTED" ? episodeIds(l1) : l2.length > 0 ? episodeIds(l2) : level3Ids;
  const proposeEvidence = propose === null ? [] : propose === "TRANSFERABLE" ? uniq([...episodeIds(delayed), ...episodeIds(usable)]) : level3Ids;

  return {
    targetKey: input.targetKey,
    engineVersion: ENGINE_VERSION,
    policyVersion: input.policy.version,
    currentState: proj.state,
    evidenceState: stateAt(level),
    proposedState: stateAt(Math.max(level, curLevel)),
    route,
    conditions,
    flag: { conflict, key: flagKey, evidence: flagIds },
    auto,
    propose,
    raiseFlag,
    evidenceKey,
    evidence: { auto: autoEvidence, propose: proposeEvidence, flag: flagIds },
  };
}
```

**Interfaces:**

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from "../../scripts/lib/migrate";

const sql = readFileSync(path.join(MIGRATIONS_DIR, "0023_skill_states.sql"), "utf8");
const code = sql.split("\n").filter((l) => !l.trim().startsWith("--")).join("\n");
const fnText = (src: string, name: string) => {
  const start = src.indexOf(`create or replace function english.${name}(`);
  expect(start, name).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  return src.slice(start, src.indexOf("\n$$;", start));
};
const lines = (s: string) => s.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith("--"));
/** Every callable function of 0023 is for the server only (service_role after requireAccess); internal ones for nobody. */
const grantsOf = (name: string) => [...code.matchAll(new RegExp(`grant execute on function english\\.${name}\\([^)]*\\) to ([a-z_, ]+);`, "g"))].map((m) => m[1]);

describe("0023_skill_states.sql", () => {
  it("ends by recording its own version, is expand-only and never touches schema public", () => {
    expect(sql.trim().endsWith("insert into english.schema_migrations(version) values ('0023') on conflict do nothing;")).toBe(true);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/drop\s+table|drop\s+column|rename\s+(to|column)|drop\s+function/i);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/\bpublic\./);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/grant\s+[a-z, ]+on all tables/i);
  });

  it("the engine reads AP-0.3 through an insert-only map; the locked catalogue is not touched (D2)", () => {
    expect(code).toContain("values ('0.2.1-draft', 'AP-0.3',");
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/update\s+english\.catalogue_versions/i);
    expect(code).toMatch(/create trigger engine_policy_map_immutable\s+before update or delete on english\.engine_policy_map\s+for each row execute function english\.eng_forbid_row_change\(\);/);
    expect(code).toContain("revoke all on english.engine_policy_map from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;");
    expect(fnText(sql, "eng_engine_policy")).toContain("english.engine_policy_map");
  });

  it("the decision book is append-only, staff-read only, and nobody writes it or the derived state directly", () => {
    expect(code).toMatch(/create trigger mastery_decisions_forbid_mutation\s+before update or delete on english\.mastery_decisions\s+for each row execute function english\.eng_forbid_mutation\('learner_id'\);/);
    expect(code).toMatch(/create trigger mastery_decisions_forbid_truncate\s+before truncate on english\.mastery_decisions\s+for each statement execute function english\.eng_forbid_mutation\(\);/);
    for (const t of ["mastery_decisions", "competency_state"]) {
      expect(code).toContain(`alter table english.${t} enable row level security;`);
      expect(code).toMatch(new RegExp(`create policy ${t}_select on english\\.${t}\\s+for select to authenticated\\s+using \\(english\\.eng_is_staff_of\\(learner_id\\)\\);`));
    }
    expect(code).toContain("revoke insert, update, delete, truncate on english.mastery_decisions, english.competency_state from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;");
    expect(code).toContain("check (kind in ('AUTO', 'PROPOSED', 'CONFIRMED', 'REJECTED', 'MANUAL', 'FLAG'))");
    expect(code).toContain("create unique index if not exists mastery_decisions_answered_once on english.mastery_decisions(responds_to) where responds_to is not null;");
    expect(code).toContain("create unique index if not exists mastery_decisions_evidence_once on english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, evidence_key) where kind in ('PROPOSED', 'FLAG');");
  });

  it("never downgrade is in the table: AUTO only up to 1–2, PROPOSED only up to 3–4, ⚑ keeps the level, MANUAL only to 1–2 with a reason", () => {
    expect(code).toContain("(kind = 'AUTO' and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) between 1 and 2 and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) > english.eng_skill_level(from_state))");
    expect(code).toContain("(kind = 'PROPOSED' and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) between 3 and 4 and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) > english.eng_skill_level(from_state))");
    expect(code).toContain("(kind = 'FLAG' and to_state = from_state and english.eng_skill_level(from_state) >= 2)");
    expect(code).toContain("(kind = 'MANUAL' and to_state in ('SUPPORTED', 'DEVELOPING') and to_state <> from_state and reason is not null)");

  it("internal helpers are executable by nobody", () => {

  });

  it("score words (amendment A): only 'Đạt yêu cầu' and 'Vững, rõ ràng' of the 0–4 labels are still refused", () => {
    const fn = fnText(sql, "eng_has_score_words");

    expect(fn).not.toContain("[cC][hH][ưƯ][aA],?");

  it("the purge keeps EVERY line of the 0021 version and its preview; the state and the book go before the S5 tables", () => {
    const old21 = readFileSync(path.join(MIGRATIONS_DIR, "0021_feedback_revision.sql"), "utf8");
    for (const fn of ["eng_purge_learner", "eng_purge_preview"]) {

    const purge = fnText(sql, "eng_purge_learner");
    const at = (s: string) => purge.indexOf(s);
    expect(at("delete from english.competency_state cs where cs.learner_id = p_learner_id;")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(at("delete from english.mastery_decisions md where md.learner_id = p_learner_id;")).toBeGreaterThan(at("delete from english.competency_state cs"));
    expect(at("delete from english.mastery_decisions md")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.validation_decisions vd"));
    for (const k of ["mastery_decisions", "competency_state"]) {
      expect(purge).toContain(`'${k}', n_${k},`);
      expect(fnText(sql, "eng_purge_preview")).toContain(`'${k}', (select pg_catalog.count(*) from english.${k} x where x.learner_id = p_learner),`);
    }
  });

  // (Tasks 5 and 6 add their tests here)
});
```
