- [ ] **Step 6: Create the migration (part 1).** Create `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql` with exactly this content (header, sections 1–3, section 7, and the marker line of section 8):

```sql
-- 0023_skill_states.sql
-- S7a · Trạng thái từng kỹ năng (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states-design.md §1–§5;
-- kế hoạch docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states.md):
--   1. english.engine_policy_map: danh mục → chính sách engine dùng (0.2.1-draft → AP-0.3, seed ở 0022). Danh mục đã khóa không đổi.
--   2. english.mastery_decisions (append-only): AUTO (bậc 1–2, app tự cập nhật) · PROPOSED (bậc 3–4) · FLAG (⚑ giữ bậc) · CONFIRMED /
--      REJECTED (gia sư của em trả lời) · MANUAL (gia sư sửa tay bậc 1–2, có lý do). english.competency_state: bảng dẫn xuất, dựng lại được.
--   3. Engine chạy bằng TypeScript (lib/engine): đọc bằng chứng qua eng_engine_inputs, ghi kết quả qua eng_apply_engine_results — hàm SQL
--      kiểm lại mọi luật: không bao giờ hạ bậc, một đề xuất mở mỗi (em, kỹ năng), cùng bằng chứng không đề xuất / gắn cờ lại.
--   4. Gia sư của em: eng_decide_skill (Xác nhận / Chưa), eng_confirm_skill_proposals (Xác nhận tất cả — chỉ đề xuất lên bậc),
--      eng_set_skill_state (sửa tay bậc 1–2). Gia sư / phụ huynh: eng_reset_skill_states (tính lại từ đầu). Đọc: eng_skill_states, eng_skill_inbox.
--   5. eng_has_score_words bản mới (sửa đổi A): chỉ còn chặn 2 nhãn "Đạt yêu cầu" và "Vững, rõ ràng".
--   6. eng_purge_learner + eng_purge_preview thêm 2 bảng mới.
-- Học sinh không đọc được bảng nào mới và không có hàm nào mới. Không đụng schema public. Idempotent; chỉ thêm (expand).
-- Cần 0019 (bản đồ kỹ năng), 0021 (bài sửa) và 0022 (AP-0.3) đã áp.

-- 1. Chính sách engine dùng (D2) ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Dòng mới nhất của một danh mục thắng; đổi = thêm dòng (không sửa, không xóa). Không ai đọc thẳng: chỉ qua eng_engine_policy.
create table if not exists english.engine_policy_map (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  catalogue_version text not null references english.catalogue_versions(version),
  policy_version text not null references english.assessment_policies(version),
  note text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(note)) between 1 and 300),
  set_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp()
);
create unique index if not exists engine_policy_map_once on english.engine_policy_map(catalogue_version, policy_version);
insert into english.engine_policy_map(catalogue_version, policy_version, note)
values ('0.2.1-draft', 'AP-0.3', 'S7a: engine dùng AP-0.3 (thêm P3-b) cho bộ A-v1 / B-v1; danh mục 0.2.1-draft đã khóa vẫn ghi AP-0.2-pilot')
on conflict (catalogue_version, policy_version) do nothing;
drop trigger if exists engine_policy_map_immutable on english.engine_policy_map;
create trigger engine_policy_map_immutable
  before update or delete on english.engine_policy_map
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_row_change();
drop trigger if exists engine_policy_map_immutable_truncate on english.engine_policy_map;
create trigger engine_policy_map_immutable_truncate
  before truncate on english.engine_policy_map
  for each statement execute function english.eng_forbid_row_change();
alter table english.engine_policy_map enable row level security;
revoke all on english.engine_policy_map from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- Chính sách engine của một em: dòng map mới nhất của danh mục của bộ kỹ năng hiện tại; không có thì chính sách của danh mục. NULL = chưa có bộ.
create or replace function english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner uuid) returns text
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select coalesce(
           (select m.policy_version from english.engine_policy_map m where m.catalogue_version = d.catalogue_version order by m.id desc limit 1),
           c.assessment_policy_version)
  from english.target_sets ts
  join english.target_set_definitions d on d.id = ts.definition_id
  join english.catalogue_versions c on c.version = d.catalogue_version
  where ts.learner_id = p_learner and ts.effective_from <= pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()
  order by ts.effective_from desc, ts.seq desc
  limit 1
$$;

-- Phạm vi của engine = các kỹ năng của bộ hiện tại (D11). NULL = chưa có bộ.
create or replace function english.eng_engine_scope(p_learner uuid) returns text[]
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select d.target_ids
  from english.target_sets ts
  join english.target_set_definitions d on d.id = ts.definition_id
  where ts.learner_id = p_learner and ts.effective_from <= pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()
  order by ts.effective_from desc, ts.seq desc
  limit 1
$$;
revoke execute on function english.eng_engine_policy(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_engine_scope(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 2. Sổ quyết định kỹ năng + trạng thái dẫn xuất --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Bậc của một trạng thái (UX_COPY_GUIDE §3.2): 0..4; NULL = không phải trạng thái. Dùng trong CHECK.
create or replace function english.eng_skill_level(p_state text) returns int
language sql immutable set search_path = ''
as $$
  select case p_state
    when 'NOT_OBSERVED' then 0 when 'SUPPORTED' then 1 when 'DEVELOPING' then 2
    when 'INDEPENDENT' then 3 when 'TRANSFERABLE' then 4 end
$$;
revoke execute on function english.eng_skill_level(text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function english.eng_skill_level(text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- AUTO / PROPOSED / FLAG: do app ghi (không có người); CONFIRMED / REJECTED: gia sư trả lời đúng một đề xuất (responds_to); MANUAL: gia sư sửa tay
-- bậc 1–2 (có thể đóng một ⚑ đang mở). seq = thứ tự ghi (dùng cho "mới nhất"). evidence_key = khóa bằng chứng (D13).
create table if not exists english.mastery_decisions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  seq bigint generated always as identity,
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  target_key text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(target_key)) between 1 and 100),
  kind text not null check (kind in ('AUTO', 'PROPOSED', 'CONFIRMED', 'REJECTED', 'MANUAL', 'FLAG')),
  from_state text not null check (english.eng_skill_level(from_state) is not null),
  to_state text not null check (english.eng_skill_level(to_state) is not null),
  route smallint check (route in (1, 2)),
  conditions jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb check (pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(conditions) = 'array'),
  evidence_episode_ids uuid[] not null default '{}' check (cardinality(evidence_episode_ids) <= 200),
  evidence_key text check (evidence_key is null or evidence_key ~ '^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$'),
  policy_version text not null references english.assessment_policies(version),
  engine_version text check (engine_version is null or engine_version ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9.-]{0,19}$'),
  -- id của english.members, KHÔNG có khóa ngoại (như level_decisions.decided_by_member).
  decided_by_member uuid,
  decided_role text check (decided_role is null or decided_role = 'tutor'),
  reason text check (reason is null or pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(reason)) between 3 and 300),
  responds_to uuid references english.mastery_decisions(id),
  client_id text check (client_id is null or pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(client_id)) between 8 and 100),
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  constraint mastery_decisions_actor check (
    (kind in ('AUTO', 'PROPOSED', 'FLAG') and decided_by_member is null and decided_role is null and client_id is null
       and responds_to is null and engine_version is not null and evidence_key is not null)
    or (kind in ('CONFIRMED', 'REJECTED', 'MANUAL') and decided_by_member is not null and decided_role = 'tutor' and client_id is not null)),
  constraint mastery_decisions_levels check (
    (kind = 'AUTO' and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) between 1 and 2 and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) > english.eng_skill_level(from_state))
    or (kind = 'PROPOSED' and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) between 3 and 4 and english.eng_skill_level(to_state) > english.eng_skill_level(from_state))
    or (kind = 'FLAG' and to_state = from_state and english.eng_skill_level(from_state) >= 2)
    or (kind = 'CONFIRMED' and responds_to is not null)
    or (kind = 'REJECTED' and responds_to is not null and to_state = from_state and reason is not null)
    or (kind = 'MANUAL' and to_state in ('SUPPORTED', 'DEVELOPING') and to_state <> from_state and reason is not null))
);
create unique index if not exists mastery_decisions_answered_once on english.mastery_decisions(responds_to) where responds_to is not null;
create unique index if not exists mastery_decisions_evidence_once on english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, evidence_key) where kind in ('PROPOSED', 'FLAG');
create unique index if not exists mastery_decisions_client_once on english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, client_id, target_key) where client_id is not null;
create index if not exists mastery_decisions_learner_idx on english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, seq desc);

drop trigger if exists mastery_decisions_forbid_mutation on english.mastery_decisions;
create trigger mastery_decisions_forbid_mutation
  before update or delete on english.mastery_decisions
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_mutation('learner_id');
drop trigger if exists mastery_decisions_forbid_truncate on english.mastery_decisions;
create trigger mastery_decisions_forbid_truncate
  before truncate on english.mastery_decisions
  for each statement execute function english.eng_forbid_mutation();

-- Một dòng / (em, kỹ năng): bậc hiện hành, ⚑ đang mở, đề xuất đang mở (đều suy ra từ sổ) + điều kiện mới nhất engine tính (cho "Còn cần").
-- Bảng dẫn xuất (DESIGN §6 "projection"): xóa đi tính lại được (eng_reset_skill_states). Chỉ các hàm của file này ghi.
create table if not exists english.competency_state (
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  target_key text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(target_key)) between 1 and 100),
  state text not null default 'NOT_OBSERVED' check (english.eng_skill_level(state) is not null),
  state_since timestamptz,
  state_decision_id uuid references english.mastery_decisions(id),
  flag_decision_id uuid references english.mastery_decisions(id),
  proposal_decision_id uuid references english.mastery_decisions(id),
  route smallint check (route in (1, 2)),
  conditions jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb check (pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(conditions) = 'array'),
  evaluated_at timestamptz,
  policy_version text,
  engine_version text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  primary key (learner_id, target_key)
);

-- RLS: phụ huynh / gia sư trong phạm vi đọc; học sinh không bao giờ. Không ai (kể cả service_role) ghi trực tiếp.
alter table english.mastery_decisions enable row level security;
alter table english.competency_state enable row level security;
revoke all on english.mastery_decisions, english.competency_state from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke insert, update, delete, truncate on english.mastery_decisions, english.competency_state from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on english.mastery_decisions, english.competency_state to authenticated;
drop policy if exists mastery_decisions_select on english.mastery_decisions;
create policy mastery_decisions_select on english.mastery_decisions
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));
drop policy if exists competency_state_select on english.competency_state;
create policy competency_state_select on english.competency_state
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));

-- 3. Hàm nhỏ dùng chung (nội bộ) ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Mảng id (jsonb) → uuid[] không trùng, có thứ tự.
create or replace function english.eng_uuid_array(p jsonb) returns uuid[]
language sql immutable set search_path = ''
as $$
  select coalesce(array(select distinct x::uuid from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(p, '[]'::jsonb)) as t(x) order by 1), '{}'::uuid[])
$$;

-- Các dòng hỗ trợ của một lần làm (D4), kèm kỹ năng bị ảnh hưởng.
create or replace function english.eng_attempt_assistance(p_attempt uuid) returns jsonb
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select coalesce(pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
           'kind', s.kind, 'verification', s.verification, 'targets', pg_catalog.to_jsonb(s.affects_target_keys)) order by s.id), '[]'::jsonb)
  from english.assistance_events s
  where s.attempt_id = p_attempt
$$;

-- competency_state của một (em, kỹ năng) từ sổ (D15) - cùng luật với lib/engine/project.ts: bậc = AUTO / CONFIRMED / MANUAL mới nhất theo seq;
-- ⚑ / đề xuất đang mở = FLAG / PROPOSED chưa có dòng nào trả lời. Giữ nguyên điều kiện / đường / lúc tính của engine.
create or replace function english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner uuid, p_target text) returns void
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_state english.mastery_decisions%rowtype;
  v_flag uuid;
  v_prop uuid;
begin
  select d.* into v_state from english.mastery_decisions d
  where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = p_target and d.kind in ('AUTO', 'CONFIRMED', 'MANUAL')
  order by d.seq desc limit 1;
  select d.id into v_flag from english.mastery_decisions d
  where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = p_target and d.kind = 'FLAG'
    and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = d.id)
  order by d.seq desc limit 1;
  select d.id into v_prop from english.mastery_decisions d
  where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = p_target and d.kind = 'PROPOSED'
    and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = d.id)
  order by d.seq desc limit 1;
  insert into english.competency_state(learner_id, target_key, state, state_since, state_decision_id, flag_decision_id, proposal_decision_id, updated_at)
  values (p_learner, p_target, coalesce(v_state.to_state, 'NOT_OBSERVED'), v_state.created_at, v_state.id, v_flag, v_prop, pg_catalog.clock_timestamp())
  on conflict (learner_id, target_key) do update
    set state = excluded.state, state_since = excluded.state_since, state_decision_id = excluded.state_decision_id,
        flag_decision_id = excluded.flag_decision_id, proposal_decision_id = excluded.proposal_decision_id, updated_at = excluded.updated_at;
end
$$;

revoke execute on function english.eng_uuid_array(jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_attempt_assistance(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_project_skill_state(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- Danh sách mẫu chung với lib/revision/scoreWords.ts: tests/unit/fixtures/scoreWords.ts.
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the evidence helper** — `scripts/lib/skillEvidence.ts`

```ts
/**
 * Evidence for skill-state tests (S7a): one VALIDATED piece of work written straight into the evidence tables, dated `daysAgo` Vietnamese
 * days back at 12:00 - what weeks of real use would leave behind (level 3 needs work spread over ≥ 7 days). TEST learners only: it refuses
 * any other learner. The connection must be the database owner (DB tests, CI e2e), because the tables refuse direct inserts from app roles.
 * Never used by the app.
 */
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { ClientBase } from "pg";

export type EvidenceFacet = "baseline" | "checkpoint" | "delayed_check" | "transfer_check" | "revision";
export type EvidenceSpec = {
  /** activity_instances.id of this learner; the topic (episodes.context_id) is its inquiry's template id. */
  activityId: string;
  daysAgo: number;
  /** Skill → 0..4, or null = "không có cơ hội thể hiện". One tutor observation + one AGREE decision per skill. */
  scores: Record<string, number | null>;
  mode?: "PRACTICE" | "INDEPENDENT_CHECK" | "REVISION";
  /** Default: true when this is the first attempt on the step. */
  firstAttempt?: boolean;
  observedDirectly?: boolean;
  facets?: EvidenceFacet[];
  /** attempts.declared_assistance; default ['none']. */
  declared?: string[];
  /** assistance_events rows; each lists every scored skill. */
  help?: { kind: string; verification: "observed" | "declared" | "unknown" }[];
};
export type InsertedEvidence = { attemptId: string; episodeId: string; decisionIds: Record<string, string> };

export async function insertValidatedEpisode(client: ClientBase, learnerId: string, tutorMemberId: string, spec: EvidenceSpec): Promise<InsertedEvidence> {
  const ctx = await client.query<{ is_test: boolean; context: string }>(
    `select l.is_test, i.template_id as context
     from english.activity_instances a
     join english.learners l on l.id = a.learner_id
     join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id
     where a.id = $1 and a.learner_id = $2`,
    [spec.activityId, learnerId],
  );
  const row = ctx.rows[0];
  if (!row) throw new Error("insertValidatedEpisode: this step does not belong to this learner");
  if (!row.is_test) throw new Error("insertValidatedEpisode: refusing a learner that is not a test learner");

  const at = (
    await client.query<{ at: Date }>(
      "select ((((now() at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh')::date - $1::int) + time '12:00') at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh') as at",
      [spec.daysAgo],
    )
  ).rows[0]!.at;
  const last = (
    await client.query<{ id: string; no: number; fam: string }>(
      "select id, attempt_no as no, learning_family_id as fam from english.attempts where activity_instance_id = $1 order by attempt_no desc limit 1",
      [spec.activityId],
    )
  ).rows[0];
  const attemptId = randomUUID();
  const no = (last?.no ?? 0) + 1;
  const keys = Object.keys(spec.scores);

  await client.query(
    `insert into english.attempts(id, activity_instance_id, learner_id, attempt_no, parent_attempt_id, learning_family_id, mode, provenance,
                                  declared_assistance, submitted_at, content_hash, client_submission_id)
     values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, 'manual_import', $8::text[], $9, $10, $11)`,
    [attemptId, spec.activityId, learnerId, no, last?.id ?? null, last?.fam ?? attemptId, spec.mode ?? "INDEPENDENT_CHECK", spec.declared ?? ["none"], at, "e".repeat(64), randomUUID()],
  );
  await client.query(
    "insert into english.artifact_versions(attempt_id, learner_id, kind, version, content, is_raw, created_by) values ($1, $2, 'text', 1, $3::jsonb, true, $4)",
    [attemptId, learnerId, JSON.stringify({ text: "Bài thử cho trạng thái kỹ năng (dữ liệu thử)." }), tutorMemberId],
  );
  const f = new Set(spec.facets ?? []);
  const facets = {
    baseline: f.has("baseline"), checkpoint: f.has("checkpoint"), delayed_check: f.has("delayed_check"), transfer_check: f.has("transfer_check"),
    ...(f.has("revision") ? { revision: true } : {}),
  };
  const episodeId = (
    await client.query<{ id: string }>(
      `insert into english.episodes(attempt_id, learner_id, is_first_attempt, context_id, observed_directly, facets, created_at)
       values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::jsonb, $7) returning id`,
      [attemptId, learnerId, spec.firstAttempt ?? no === 1, row.context, spec.observedDirectly ?? false, JSON.stringify(facets), at],
    )
  ).rows[0]!.id;
  await client.query(
    "insert into english.exposure_log(learner_id, target_key, at, source, attempt_id) select $1, k, $2, $3, $4 from unnest($5::text[]) as k",
    [learnerId, at, no > 1 ? "retry" : "practice", attemptId, keys],
  );
  for (const h of spec.help ?? []) {
    await client.query(
      "insert into english.assistance_events(learner_id, attempt_id, kind, affects_target_keys, verification, at) values ($1, $2, $3, $4::text[], $5, $6)",
      [learnerId, attemptId, h.kind, keys, h.verification, at],
    );
  }
  const decisionIds: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const k of keys) {
    const score = spec.scores[k] ?? null;
    const obs = (
      await client.query<{ id: string }>(
        `insert into english.observations(episode_id, learner_id, target_key, scorer, scorer_member_id, status, score, not_observed, blind, rubric_version, created_at)
         values ($1, $2, $3, 'tutor', $4, 'PROPOSED', $5, $6, false, 'test', $7) returning id`,
        [episodeId, learnerId, k, tutorMemberId, score, score === null, at],
      )
    ).rows[0]!.id;
    decisionIds[k] = (
      await client.query<{ id: string }>(
        `insert into english.validation_decisions(episode_id, learner_id, target_key, observation_id, decision, final_score, final_not_observed,
                                                 validator_member_id, validator_role, created_at)
         values ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'AGREE', $5, $6, $7, 'tutor', $8) returning id`,
        [episodeId, learnerId, k, obs, score, score === null, tutorMemberId, at],
      )
    ).rows[0]!.id;
  }
  return { attemptId, episodeId, decisionIds };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write the failing DB test** — `tests/db/skillStates.test.ts`. Tasks 5–7 replace the marker comment at the end with their sections.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CONTENT_DIR } from "../../lib/config/content";
import type { InquiryTemplate } from "../../lib/config/schemas";
import { addDays, daysBetween, todayVn } from "../../lib/inquiries/dates";
import { seedThrowawayLearner } from "../../scripts/lib/seed";
import { insertValidatedEpisode, type EvidenceSpec } from "../../scripts/lib/skillEvidence";
import { SCORE_WORD_SAMPLES } from "../unit/fixtures/scoreWords";
import { asCommit, createTestDb, migrate, rowsAs, seed, type Seeded, type TestDb } from "./helpers";

const TODAY = todayVn();
const template = (id: string): InquiryTemplate =>
  JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(CONTENT_DIR, "inquiries", `${id}.json`), "utf8")) as InquiryTemplate;
/** The due date that releases every step of the template today. */
function dueReleasingAll(id: string): string {
  const t = template(id);
  const last = t.steps.map((s) => s.planned_date ?? "").sort().at(-1) ?? "";
  return addDays(TODAY, daysBetween(last, t.dates.to));
}
const SKILL = "A-L3-CAUSE-W"; // An's writing skill on the checkpoint writing steps A-W1 11, A-W2 12, A-W3 13

/** S7a (migrations 0022 + 0023): policy map, decision book, derived state, engine in/out, decisions, reads, purge. One database for the file. */
describe("S7a skill states (migrations 0022 + 0023)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let s: Seeded;
  const q1 = async <R extends Record<string, unknown>>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) => (await db.client.query<R>(sql, params)).rows;
  const svc = <T = unknown>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) =>
    asCommit(db.client, "service_role", null, async (q) => ((await q(sql, params)).rows[0] as { r: T }).r);
  const fail = async (p: Promise<unknown>, code: string, re?: RegExp) => {
    const err = await p.then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e as { code?: string; message?: string });
    expect(err, "expected an error").not.toBeNull();
    expect(err?.code, err?.message).toBe(code);
    if (re) expect(err?.message).toMatch(re);
  };
  let reqSeq = 0;
  const assign = async (who: "an" | "nguyen", tpl: string) =>
    (await q1<{ id: string }>("select english.eng_assign_inquiry($1, $2, 1, $3, $4, $5) as id", [s.learners[who], tpl, dueReleasingAll(tpl), s.members.parent, `s7a-${(reqSeq += 1)}-${tpl}`]))[0]!.id;
  const step = async (instance: string, stepNo: number) =>
    (await q1<{ id: string }>("select id from english.activity_instances where inquiry_instance_id = $1 and step_no = $2", [instance, stepNo]))[0]!.id;
  const evidence = (who: "an" | "nguyen", spec: EvidenceSpec) =>
    insertValidatedEpisode(db.client, s.learners[who], who === "an" ? s.members.tutorAn : s.members.tutorNguyen, spec);
  /** A row written straight into the book as the table owner (tests only: the app writes through the functions). */
  const book = async (learnerId: string, o: { kind?: string; from?: string; to?: string; key?: string; target?: string } = {}) =>
    (await q1<{ id: string }>(
      `insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version)
       values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'AP-0.3', 'S7a-1') returning id`,
      [learnerId, o.target ?? SKILL, o.kind ?? "AUTO", o.from ?? "NOT_OBSERVED", o.to ?? "SUPPORTED", o.key ?? `1-${"a".repeat(16)}`],
    ))[0]!.id;

  let w1: string; // An, A-W1 (step 11: checkpoint writing)
  let w2: string; // An, A-W2 (step 12: checkpoint writing)
  let w3: string; // An, A-W3 (step 13: checkpoint writing)
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.client);
    s = await seed(db.client);
    w1 = await assign("an", "A-W1-VOLCANOES");
    w2 = await assign("an", "A-W2-WATERCYCLE");
    w3 = await assign("an", "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES");
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db?.drop();
  });

  describe("1. policy map, books, RLS, score words, purge", () => {
    it("policy map: catalogue 0.2.1-draft → AP-0.3, insert-only, no app role reads it; the engine policy of both children is AP-0.3 while the catalogue keeps AP-0.2", async () => {
      expect(await q1("select catalogue_version, policy_version from english.engine_policy_map")).toEqual([{ catalogue_version: "0.2.1-draft", policy_version: "AP-0.3" }]);
      expect(await q1("select english.eng_engine_policy($1) as a, english.eng_engine_policy($2) as b", [s.learners.an, s.learners.nguyen])).toEqual([{ a: "AP-0.3", b: "AP-0.3" }]);
      expect(await q1("select assessment_policy_version as v from english.catalogue_versions")).toEqual([{ v: "AP-0.2-pilot" }]);
      expect((await q1<{ n: number }>("select cardinality(english.eng_engine_scope($1)) as n", [s.learners.an]))[0]?.n).toBe(12);
      for (const sql of ["update english.engine_policy_map set note = 'x'", "delete from english.engine_policy_map", "truncate english.engine_policy_map"]) {
        const err = await db.client.query(sql).then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e as { code?: string });
        expect(err?.code, sql).toBe("42501");
      }
      for (const role of ["authenticated", "service_role", "anon"]) {
        expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_table_privilege('${role}', 'english.engine_policy_map', 'select') as ok`))[0]?.ok, role).toBe(false);
      }
    });

    it("the book refuses every shape that would downgrade or skip a person; it is append-only; nobody (service_role included) writes it or the state directly", async () => {
      const base = { kind: "AUTO", from: "NOT_OBSERVED", to: "SUPPORTED", key: `1-${"b".repeat(16)}`, engine: "S7a-1", by: null as string | null, role: null as string | null, reason: null as string | null, responds: null as string | null, client: null as string | null };
      const ins = (o: Partial<typeof base>) => {
        const v = { ...base, ...o };
        return q1(
          `insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version,
                                                 decided_by_member, decided_role, reason, responds_to, client_id)
           values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'AP-0.3', $7, $8, $9, $10, $11, $12)`,
          [s.learners.an, SKILL, v.kind, v.from, v.to, v.key, v.engine, v.by, v.role, v.reason, v.responds, v.client],
        );
      };
      const tutor = { by: s.members.tutorAn, role: "tutor", key: null, engine: null };
      for (const bad of [
        { to: "INDEPENDENT" }, // AUTO above level 2
        { from: "DEVELOPING", to: "SUPPORTED" }, // AUTO downward
        { kind: "PROPOSED", to: "DEVELOPING" }, // a proposal below level 3
        { kind: "FLAG", from: "SUPPORTED", to: "SUPPORTED" }, // ⚑ below level 2
        { kind: "MANUAL", to: "INDEPENDENT", reason: "Lý do đủ dài", client: "client-0001", ...tutor }, // a person sets only 1–2 by hand
        { kind: "MANUAL", to: "DEVELOPING", client: "client-0002", ...tutor }, // and with a reason
        { kind: "CONFIRMED", to: "INDEPENDENT", client: "client-0003", ...tutor }, // a confirmation answers a proposal
        { key: "not-a-key" },
        { to: "NEEDS_CONFIRMATION" }, // ⚑ is a flag, never a state (D12)
        { by: s.members.parent, role: "parent" }, // engine rows have no actor; people are tutors
      ]) {
        await fail(ins(bad), "23514");
      }
      await ins({});
      for (const sql of ["update english.mastery_decisions set reason = 'x'", "delete from english.mastery_decisions", "truncate english.mastery_decisions"]) {
        const err = await db.client.query(sql).then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e as { code?: string });
        expect(err?.code, sql).toBe("42501");
      }
      const direct = (sql: string) => asCommit(db.client, "service_role", null, (q) => q(sql, [s.learners.an])).then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e as { code?: string });
      expect((await direct(`insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version)
                            values ($1, 'x', 'AUTO', 'NOT_OBSERVED', 'SUPPORTED', '1-${"c".repeat(16)}', 'AP-0.3', 'S7a-1')`))?.code).toBe("42501");
      expect((await direct("insert into english.competency_state(learner_id, target_key) values ($1, 'x')"))?.code).toBe("42501");
    });

    it("RLS: An's tutor and the parent read An's book and state; the student and Nguyên's tutor read nothing", async () => {
      await book(s.learners.an, { target: "A-T4-CAUSE" });
      await q1("select english.eng_project_skill_state($1, 'A-T4-CAUSE')", [s.learners.an]);
      const count = async (user: string, t: string) =>
        (await rowsAs<{ n: number }>(db.client, "authenticated", user, `select count(*)::int as n from english.${t} where learner_id = $1`, [s.learners.an]))[0]?.n;
      for (const t of ["mastery_decisions", "competency_state"]) {
        expect(await count(s.users.tutorAn, t), t).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(await count(s.users.parent, t), t).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(await count(s.users.studentAn, t), t).toBe(0);
        expect(await count(s.users.tutorNguyen, t), t).toBe(0);
        expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_table_privilege('anon', 'english.${t}', 'select') as ok`))[0]?.ok, t).toBe(false);
      }
      expect(await q1("select state, flag_decision_id, proposal_decision_id from english.competency_state where learner_id = $1 and target_key = 'A-T4-CAUSE'", [s.learners.an]))
        .toEqual([{ state: "SUPPORTED", flag_decision_id: null, proposal_decision_id: null }]);
    });

    it("score words (A): the database and the app agree on every shared sample; of the 0–4 labels only the two upper ones stay refused", async () => {
      for (const [text, hit] of SCORE_WORD_SAMPLES) {
        expect((await q1<{ r: boolean }>("select english.eng_has_score_words($1) as r", [text]))[0]?.r, text).toBe(hit);
      }
      expect((await q1("select english.eng_has_score_words('Chưa thể hiện') as a, english.eng_has_score_words('Còn thiếu nhiều') as b, english.eng_has_score_words('Được một phần') as c, english.eng_has_score_words('Đạt yêu cầu') as d, english.eng_has_score_words('Vững, rõ ràng') as e"))[0])
        .toEqual({ a: false, b: false, c: false, d: true, e: true });
    });

    it("the evidence helper refuses a learner that is not a test learner", async () => {
      await q1("update english.learners set is_test = false where id = $1", [s.learners.nguyen]);
      try {
        const n1 = await assign("nguyen", "B-W1-HOMEWORK");
        await expect(evidence("nguyen", { activityId: await step(n1, 7), daysAgo: 1, scores: { "B-T7-ARGUE": 3 } })).rejects.toThrow(/not a test learner/);
      } finally {
        await q1("update english.learners set is_test = true where id = $1", [s.learners.nguyen]);
      }
    });

    it("the purge removes both tables of one child (receipt and preview count them); the other children keep theirs", async () => {
      const t = await seedThrowawayLearner(db.client);
      await book(t.learnerId);
      await q1("select english.eng_project_skill_state($1, $2)", [t.learnerId, SKILL]);
      const before = await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.mastery_decisions where learner_id = $1", [s.learners.an]);
      const preview = await svc<{ counts: Record<string, number> }>("select english.eng_purge_preview($1) as r", [t.learnerId]);
      expect(preview.counts).toMatchObject({ mastery_decisions: 1, competency_state: 1 });
      const receipt = await svc<{ deleted: Record<string, number> }>("select english.eng_purge_learner($1, null) as r", [t.learnerId]);
      expect(receipt.deleted).toMatchObject({ mastery_decisions: 1, competency_state: 1 });
      expect(await q1("select 1 from english.competency_state where learner_id = $1", [t.learnerId])).toEqual([]);
      expect(await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.mastery_decisions where learner_id = $1", [s.learners.an])).toEqual(before);
    });

    it("running every migration again changes nothing", async () => {
      const snap = () => q1("select (select count(*) from english.engine_policy_map)::int as m, (select count(*) from english.mastery_decisions)::int as d, (select count(*) from english.assessment_policies)::int as p");
      const before = await snap();
      await migrate(db.client, true);
      expect(await snap()).toEqual(before);
    });
  });

  // (Tasks 5, 6 and 7 add their sections here)
});
```

Note: the `update english.learners set is_test` in "the evidence helper refuses…" runs as the owner. `learners` is not append-only, and the `finally` puts it back.

### Task 5: Migration 0023 part 2 — engine in / out: `eng_engine_inputs`, `eng_apply_engine_results`, `eng_reset_skill_states`

**Files:**
- Modify: `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql` (paste section 4 above `-- 8. Purge:`), `tests/unit/migration0023.test.ts`, `tests/db/skillStates.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 4: `eng_engine_policy`, `eng_engine_scope`, `eng_project_skill_state`, `eng_uuid_array`, `eng_attempt_assistance`, `eng_skill_level`, the tables;
  - `english.eng_current_skill_map()` (0019), `skill_leaves` / `skill_groups` (0019);
  - `attempt_auto_checks.checks.items{id: {kind, result}}` (0009 L240–299);
  - `inquiry_instances.snapshot.steps[].items[].target_id` (`lib/config/inquiries.ts` L186);
  - the current `validation_decisions` (not superseded, `AGREE` / `MODIFY`);
  - `exposure_log`.
- Produces (all `service_role` only; the server calls them after `requireAccess`, or after a write that already passed it):
  - `english.eng_engine_inputs(p_learner uuid, p_targets text[] default null) returns jsonb`, returning:
    - `learner_id`, `now`;
    - `policy` = `{version, config}` or null;
    - `targets` = `[{key, domain}]` in set order;
    - `episodes` = `[{episode_id, attempt_id, activity_id, at, context_id, is_first_attempt, mode, observed_directly, facets, declared_unknown, assistance: [{kind, verification, targets}], scores: [{target_key, decision_id, score, not_observed}]}]`;
    - `auto` = `[{attempt_id, episode_id, activity_id, at, is_first_attempt, mode, facets, declared_unknown, assistance, items: [{item_id, target_key, result}]}]`;
    - `exposures` = `[{target_key, at, attempt_id, context_id}]`;
    - `decisions` = `[{id, seq, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, evidence_key, responds_to, created_at}]`.
    - `p_targets` is intersected with the current set (D11).
  - `english.eng_apply_engine_results(p_learner uuid, p_results jsonb, p_engine_version text) returns jsonb` = `{auto, proposed, flagged, policy_version}`. Each element of `p_results` is `{target_key, auto, propose, raise_flag, flag_key, route, evidence_key, conditions, evidence: {auto, propose, flag}}`. Errors: `22023 (not_in_set)`, `22023 (bad_results)`, `P0002 (no_target_set)`.
  - `english.eng_reset_skill_states(p_learner uuid, p_by uuid) returns jsonb` = `{targets}`. The caller must be a tutor of the child or the family's parent, otherwise `42501 (staff_only)`. Audit action `skill_states.rebuilt`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Paste section 4** into `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql`, immediately above the line starting `-- 8. Purge:`:

```sql
-- 4. Engine: đọc bằng chứng, ghi kết quả (service_role; máy chủ gọi sau requireAccess, hoặc ngay sau một lần ghi đã qua requireAccess) -----
-- Bằng chứng của các kỹ năng trong bộ hiện tại của em (p_targets = chỉ một phần của bộ; mã ngoài bộ bị bỏ qua, D11):
--   episodes  = bài có quyết định duyệt hiện hành (AGREE / MODIFY chưa bị thay) cho ít nhất một kỹ năng đó (§2 "chỉ bằng chứng đã duyệt");
--   auto      = câu đóng app tự chấm (correct / incorrect / blank; needs_person không tính) gắn kỹ năng đó trong ảnh chụp bài giao (P3-b);
--   exposures = lần luyện (cho khoảng cách "≥ 14 ngày"); decisions = sổ quyết định kỹ năng.
-- Không đọc results / tag_marks / reading_reports / recheck_events (tín hiệu, D32).
create or replace function english.eng_engine_inputs(p_learner uuid, p_targets text[] default null) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_policy text;
  v_map text := english.eng_current_skill_map();
  v_keys text[];
begin
  if p_learner is null then
    raise exception 'eng_engine_inputs: learner id is required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_engine_inputs: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  v_policy := english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner);
  v_keys := array(
    select u.k from unnest(coalesce(english.eng_engine_scope(p_learner), '{}'::text[])) with ordinality as u(k, o)
    where p_targets is null or u.k = any (p_targets)
    order by u.o);

  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
    'learner_id', p_learner,
    'now', pg_catalog.clock_timestamp(),
    'policy', case when v_policy is null then null else pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
      'version', v_policy,
      'config', (select p.config from english.assessment_policies p where p.version = v_policy)) end,
    'targets', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'key', u.k,
               'domain', (select g.domain_key from english.skill_leaves sl
                          join english.skill_groups g on g.map_id = sl.map_id and g.key = sl.group_key
                          where sl.map_id = v_map and sl.kind = 'target' and sl.track_id = v_l.track_id and sl.ref_key = u.k))
             order by u.o)
      from unnest(v_keys) with ordinality as u(k, o)), '[]'::jsonb),
    'episodes', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'episode_id', e.id, 'attempt_id', t.id, 'activity_id', t.activity_instance_id, 'at', t.submitted_at,
               'context_id', e.context_id, 'is_first_attempt', e.is_first_attempt, 'mode', t.mode,
               'observed_directly', e.observed_directly, 'facets', e.facets,
               'declared_unknown', 'unknown' = any (t.declared_assistance),
               'assistance', english.eng_attempt_assistance(t.id),
               'scores', sc.scores)
             order by t.submitted_at, e.id)
      from english.episodes e
      join english.attempts t on t.id = e.attempt_id
      cross join lateral (
        select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                 'target_key', vd.target_key, 'decision_id', vd.id, 'score', vd.final_score, 'not_observed', vd.final_not_observed)
               order by vd.target_key) as scores
        from english.validation_decisions vd
        where vd.episode_id = e.id and vd.target_key = any (v_keys) and vd.decision in ('AGREE', 'MODIFY')
          and not exists (select 1 from english.validation_decisions s where s.supersedes_id = vd.id)) sc
      where e.learner_id = p_learner and sc.scores is not null), '[]'::jsonb),
    'auto', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'attempt_id', t.id, 'episode_id', e.id, 'activity_id', t.activity_instance_id, 'at', t.submitted_at,
               'is_first_attempt', e.is_first_attempt, 'mode', t.mode, 'facets', e.facets,
               'declared_unknown', 'unknown' = any (t.declared_assistance),
               'assistance', english.eng_attempt_assistance(t.id),
               'items', it.items)
             order by t.submitted_at, t.id)
      from english.attempt_auto_checks c
      join english.attempts t on t.id = c.attempt_id
      join english.episodes e on e.attempt_id = t.id
      join english.activity_instances a on a.id = t.activity_instance_id
      join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id
      cross join lateral (
        select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                 'item_id', x ->> 'id', 'target_key', x ->> 'target_id', 'result', c.checks -> 'items' -> (x ->> 'id') ->> 'result')
               order by x ->> 'id') as items
        from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(i.snapshot -> 'steps') as st
        cross join lateral pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(st -> 'items', '[]'::jsonb)) as x
        where (st ->> 'step_no')::int = a.step_no
          and x ->> 'target_id' = any (v_keys)
          and (c.checks -> 'items' -> (x ->> 'id') ->> 'result') in ('correct', 'incorrect', 'blank')) it
      where c.learner_id = p_learner and it.items is not null), '[]'::jsonb),
    'exposures', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'target_key', x.target_key, 'at', x.at, 'attempt_id', x.attempt_id, 'context_id', e.context_id)
             order by x.at, x.id)
      from english.exposure_log x
      left join english.episodes e on e.attempt_id = x.attempt_id
      where x.learner_id = p_learner and x.target_key = any (v_keys)), '[]'::jsonb),
    'decisions', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'id', d.id, 'seq', d.seq, 'target_key', d.target_key, 'kind', d.kind, 'from_state', d.from_state, 'to_state', d.to_state,
               'evidence_key', d.evidence_key, 'responds_to', d.responds_to, 'created_at', d.created_at)
             order by d.seq)
      from english.mastery_decisions d
      where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = any (v_keys)), '[]'::jsonb));
end
$$;

-- Ghi kết quả của engine cho một em (lib/engine/runner.ts). Hàm KIỂM LẠI mọi luật trước khi ghi (máy chủ có lỗi cũng không hạ bậc được):
--   AUTO     chỉ bậc 1–2, chỉ đi lên (§1 mục 1);
--   PROPOSED chỉ bậc 3–4, cao hơn bậc hiện tại, bậc 4 chỉ từ "Tự làm được", không có đề xuất đang mở, chưa từng đề xuất cùng khóa bằng chứng (D13);
--   FLAG     chỉ khi bậc ≥ 2, không có ⚑ đang mở, chưa từng gắn cờ cùng khóa bằng chứng (D12); bậc giữ nguyên.
-- Rồi cập nhật competency_state (bậc, ⚑, đề xuất từ sổ; điều kiện / đường / lúc tính từ engine). Khóa dòng em: hai lần tính cùng lúc xếp hàng.
create or replace function english.eng_apply_engine_results(p_learner uuid, p_results jsonb, p_engine_version text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_policy text;
  v_scope text[];
  v_r jsonb;
  v_key text;
  v_cur english.competency_state%rowtype;
  v_to text;
  v_route smallint;
  v_auto int := 0;
  v_proposed int := 0;
  v_flagged int := 0;
begin
  if p_learner is null or p_results is null or p_engine_version is null then
    raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: learner, results and engine version are required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  if pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(p_results) <> 'array' or p_engine_version !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9.-]{0,19}$' then
    raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: results must be an array and the engine version a short name (bad_results)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  perform 1 from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  v_policy := english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner);
  v_scope := english.eng_engine_scope(p_learner);
  if v_policy is null or v_scope is null then
    raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: this learner has no skill set yet (no_target_set)' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;

  for v_r in select x from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(p_results) as x loop
    v_key := v_r ->> 'target_key';
    if v_key is null or not (v_key = any (v_scope)) then
      raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: % is not a skill of this learner''s set (not_in_set)', coalesce(v_key, 'null') using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    if pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(coalesce(v_r -> 'conditions', 'null'::jsonb)) <> 'array'
       or coalesce(v_r ->> 'evidence_key', '') !~ '^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$'
       or coalesce(v_r ->> 'route', '1') not in ('1', '2') then
      raise exception 'eng_apply_engine_results: conditions must be an array, the evidence key well-formed and the route 1 or 2 (bad_results)' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_route := (v_r ->> 'route')::smallint;
    perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_key);
    select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = v_key;

    -- (1) Bậc 1–2: app tự cập nhật, chỉ đi lên.
    v_to := v_r ->> 'auto';
    if v_to is not null and english.eng_skill_level(v_to) between 1 and 2
       and english.eng_skill_level(v_to) > english.eng_skill_level(v_cur.state) then
      insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, route, conditions, evidence_episode_ids,
                                            evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version)
      values (p_learner, v_key, 'AUTO', v_cur.state, v_to, v_route, v_r -> 'conditions', english.eng_uuid_array(v_r -> 'evidence' -> 'auto'),
              v_r ->> 'evidence_key', v_policy, p_engine_version);
      v_auto := v_auto + 1;
      perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_key);
      select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = v_key;
    end if;

    -- (2) Bậc 3–4: chỉ đề xuất.
    v_to := v_r ->> 'propose';
    if v_to is not null and english.eng_skill_level(v_to) between 3 and 4
       and english.eng_skill_level(v_to) > english.eng_skill_level(v_cur.state)
       and (v_to <> 'TRANSFERABLE' or v_cur.state = 'INDEPENDENT')
       and v_cur.proposal_decision_id is null
       and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions d
                       where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = v_key and d.kind = 'PROPOSED' and d.evidence_key = v_r ->> 'evidence_key') then
      insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, route, conditions, evidence_episode_ids,
                                            evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version)
      values (p_learner, v_key, 'PROPOSED', v_cur.state, v_to, v_route, v_r -> 'conditions', english.eng_uuid_array(v_r -> 'evidence' -> 'propose'),
              v_r ->> 'evidence_key', v_policy, p_engine_version);
      v_proposed := v_proposed + 1;
      perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_key);
      select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = v_key;
    end if;

    -- (3) ⚑ "Cần thầy/cô xem lại": bậc giữ nguyên.
    if coalesce((v_r ->> 'raise_flag')::boolean, false)
       and coalesce(v_r ->> 'flag_key', '') ~ '^[0-9]{1,6}-[0-9a-f]{16}$'
       and english.eng_skill_level(v_cur.state) >= 2
       and v_cur.flag_decision_id is null
       and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions d
                       where d.learner_id = p_learner and d.target_key = v_key and d.kind = 'FLAG' and d.evidence_key = v_r ->> 'flag_key') then
      insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, route, conditions, evidence_episode_ids,
                                            evidence_key, policy_version, engine_version)
      values (p_learner, v_key, 'FLAG', v_cur.state, v_cur.state, null, v_r -> 'conditions', english.eng_uuid_array(v_r -> 'evidence' -> 'flag'),
              v_r ->> 'flag_key', v_policy, p_engine_version);
      v_flagged := v_flagged + 1;
      perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_key);
    end if;

    -- (4) Điều kiện mới nhất (cho "Còn cần"), đường, lúc tính.
    update english.competency_state c
    set conditions = v_r -> 'conditions', route = v_route, evaluated_at = pg_catalog.clock_timestamp(),
        policy_version = v_policy, engine_version = p_engine_version
    where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = v_key;
  end loop;

  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('auto', v_auto, 'proposed', v_proposed, 'flagged', v_flagged, 'policy_version', v_policy);
end
$$;

-- "Tính lại từ đầu" (§3 bước 6), phần cơ sở dữ liệu: xóa competency_state của em rồi dựng lại từ sổ (mọi kỹ năng của bộ + mọi kỹ năng có trong sổ).
-- Máy chủ chạy lại engine ngay sau (lib/engine/runner.ts rebuildLearner) để điền điều kiện; luật của eng_apply_engine_results làm cho việc này
-- không tạo đề xuất trùng. Gia sư của em hoặc phụ huynh của gia đình.
create or replace function english.eng_reset_skill_states(p_learner uuid, p_by uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_m english.members%rowtype;
  v_key text;
  v_n int := 0;
begin
  if p_learner is null or p_by is null then
    raise exception 'eng_reset_skill_states: learner and actor are required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_reset_skill_states: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select * into v_m from english.members m where m.id = p_by;
  if v_m.id is null or v_m.role not in ('tutor', 'parent') or not english.eng_actor_may_manage(p_by, p_learner) then
    raise exception 'eng_reset_skill_states: only a tutor of this learner or the family''s parent rebuilds (staff_only)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  delete from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner;
  for v_key in
    select k from unnest(coalesce(english.eng_engine_scope(p_learner), '{}'::text[])) as k
    union
    select d.target_key from english.mastery_decisions d where d.learner_id = p_learner
    order by 1
  loop
    perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_key);
    v_n := v_n + 1;
  end loop;

  insert into english.audit_log(actor_user_id, actor_role, action, family_id, learner_id, payload)
  values (v_m.user_id, v_m.role, 'skill_states.rebuilt', v_l.family_id, p_learner, pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('targets', v_n));
  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('targets', v_n);
end
$$;

revoke execute on function english.eng_engine_inputs(uuid, text[]) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_engine_inputs(uuid, text[]) to service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_apply_engine_results(uuid, jsonb, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_apply_engine_results(uuid, jsonb, text) to service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_reset_skill_states(uuid, uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_reset_skill_states(uuid, uuid) to service_role;

```

```ts
  // Shared by sections 2–4: the three validated checkpoints of An on "Viết vì sao – nên" (A-W1 11 / A-W2 12 / A-W3 13; 20 / 13 / 5 days ago).
  const proof: { episodeId: string; decisionId: string }[] = [];
  let quizAttempt = "";
  const inputs = (targets: string[] | null = null) =>
    svc<{
      policy: { version: string; config: { receptiveAuto?: { minItems: number } } } | null;
      targets: { key: string; domain: string | null }[];
      episodes: { episode_id: string; facets: Record<string, boolean>; declared_unknown: boolean; scores: { target_key: string; decision_id: string; score: number | null }[] }[];
      auto: { attempt_id: string; items: { item_id: string; target_key: string; result: string }[] }[];
      exposures: { target_key: string; attempt_id: string | null; context_id: string | null }[];
      decisions: unknown[];
    }>("select english.eng_engine_inputs($1, $2::text[]) as r", [s.learners.an, targets]);
  const K = (c: string) => `3-${c.repeat(16)}`;
  type Row = { target_key: string; auto?: string | null; propose?: string | null; raise_flag?: boolean; flag_key?: string | null; route?: number | null; evidence_key?: string; conditions?: unknown; evidence?: { auto: string[]; propose: string[]; flag: string[] } };
  const row = (o: Row) => ({ auto: null, propose: null, raise_flag: false, flag_key: null, route: 1, evidence_key: K("1"), conditions: [], evidence: { auto: [], propose: [], flag: [] }, ...o });
  const apply = (rows: unknown[], learner = s.learners.an, version = "S7a-1") =>
    svc<{ auto: number; proposed: number; flagged: number; policy_version: string }>("select english.eng_apply_engine_results($1, $2::jsonb, $3) as r", [learner, JSON.stringify(rows), version]);
  const stateOf = async (k: string, learner = s.learners.an) =>
    (await q1<{ state: string; flag: boolean; proposal: boolean }>(
      "select state, flag_decision_id is not null as flag, proposal_decision_id is not null as proposal from english.competency_state where learner_id = $1 and target_key = $2", [learner, k]))[0];
  const bookCount = async (learner = s.learners.an) => (await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.mastery_decisions where learner_id = $1", [learner]))[0]!.n;
  /** Answer a proposal / flag straight in the book, as the owner (section 3 tests the real functions). */
  const answer = (id: string, kind: "CONFIRMED" | "REJECTED", from: string, to: string) =>
    q1(`insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, policy_version, decided_by_member, decided_role, reason, responds_to, client_id)
        select learner_id, target_key, $2, $3, $4, 'AP-0.3', $5, 'tutor', 'Lý do thử', id, $6 from english.mastery_decisions where id = $1`,
      [id, kind, from, to, s.members.tutorAn, `answer-${randomUUID()}`]);
  const openOf = async (k: string, kind: "PROPOSED" | "FLAG") =>
    (await q1<{ id: string }>(`select d.id from english.mastery_decisions d where d.learner_id = $1 and d.target_key = $2 and d.kind = $3
                               and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = d.id)`, [s.learners.an, k, kind]))[0]?.id;

  describe("2. engine inputs, apply, reset", () => {
    beforeAll(async () => {
      for (const [inst, n, daysAgo, score] of [[w1, 11, 20, 3], [w2, 12, 13, 4], [w3, 13, 5, 3]] as const) {
        const e = await evidence("an", { activityId: await step(inst, n), daysAgo, scores: { [SKILL]: score }, facets: ["checkpoint"] });
        proof.push({ episodeId: e.episodeId, decisionId: e.decisionIds[SKILL]! });
      }
      // A real closed answer from the app: An answers W1-R1 (mcq, A-T1-TEXT-R) right on A-W1 step 3.
      const option = template("A-W1-VOLCANOES").steps[2]!.items[0]!.options[1]!;
      const receipt = await asCommit(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.studentAn, async (q) =>
        ((await q("select english.eng_submit_attempt($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4::text[], 'in_app') as r", [await step(w1, 3), randomUUID(), JSON.stringify({ answers: { "W1-R1": option } }), ["none"]])).rows[0] as { r: { attempt_id: string } }).r);
      quizAttempt = receipt.attempt_id;
    });

    it("inputs: AP-0.3 with receptiveAuto, the 12 skills of A-v1 with their domains, the validated episodes and the auto-checked items", async () => {
      const r = await inputs();
      expect(r.policy?.version).toBe("AP-0.3");
      expect(r.policy?.config.receptiveAuto?.minItems).toBe(8);
      expect(r.targets).toHaveLength(12);
      expect(r.targets.find((t) => t.key === "A-T1-TEXT-R")?.domain).toBe("reading");
      expect(r.targets.find((t) => t.key === SKILL)?.domain).toBe("writing");
      const mine = r.episodes.filter((e) => e.scores.some((x) => x.target_key === SKILL));
      expect(mine.map((e) => e.scores.find((x) => x.target_key === SKILL)?.decision_id)).toEqual(proof.map((p) => p.decisionId));
      expect(mine.every((e) => e.facets.checkpoint && !e.declared_unknown)).toBe(true);
      expect(r.auto.find((a) => a.attempt_id === quizAttempt)?.items).toEqual([{ item_id: "W1-R1", target_key: "A-T1-TEXT-R", result: "correct" }]);
      expect(r.episodes.some((e) => e.scores.length === 0)).toBe(false); // the quiz has no decision: it is not an "episode" input
      const practice = r.exposures.filter((x) => x.target_key === SKILL);
      expect(practice).toHaveLength(3);
      expect(practice.every((x) => x.context_id !== null && x.attempt_id !== null)).toBe(true);
    });

    it("inputs follow the CURRENT decision (a MODIFY replaces the AGREE) and keep only skills of the set (D11)", async () => {
      const old = proof[1]!.decisionId;
      const [m] = await q1<{ id: string }>(
        `insert into english.validation_decisions(episode_id, learner_id, target_key, decision, final_score, validator_member_id, validator_role, reason, supersedes_id)
         values ($1, $2, $3, 'MODIFY', 3, $4, 'tutor', 'Sửa lại sau khi đọc kỹ', $5) returning id`,
        [proof[1]!.episodeId, s.learners.an, SKILL, s.members.tutorAn, old]);
      proof[1]!.decisionId = m!.id;
      const r = await inputs([SKILL, "A-A3-REDRAFT"]);
      expect(r.targets.map((t) => t.key)).toEqual([SKILL]);
      const scores = r.episodes.flatMap((e) => e.scores);
      expect(scores.find((x) => x.decision_id === old)).toBeUndefined();
      expect(scores.find((x) => x.decision_id === m!.id)?.score).toBe(3);
      expect(r.auto).toEqual([]); // the quiz has no item of SKILL
    });

    it("apply: AUTO + PROPOSED once; the same results again write nothing; AUTO never goes down; one open proposal", async () => {
      const k = "A-L3-PARA-W";
      const ev = { auto: [proof[0]!.episodeId], propose: proof.map((p) => p.episodeId), flag: [] };
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence: ev })])).toMatchObject({ auto: 1, proposed: 1, flagged: 0, policy_version: "AP-0.3" });
      expect(await stateOf(k)).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: false, proposal: true });
      const n = await bookCount();
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence: ev })])).toMatchObject({ auto: 0, proposed: 0, flagged: 0 });
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, auto: "SUPPORTED" })])).toMatchObject({ auto: 0 });
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("2") })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 0 });
      expect(await bookCount()).toBe(n);
      const [p] = await q1<{ from_state: string; to_state: string; evidence_episode_ids: string[] }>(
        "select from_state, to_state, evidence_episode_ids from english.mastery_decisions where learner_id = $1 and target_key = $2 and kind = 'PROPOSED'", [s.learners.an, k]);
      expect(p).toEqual({ from_state: "DEVELOPING", to_state: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_episode_ids: [...ev.propose].sort() });
    });

    it("apply: after 'Chưa' the same evidence key is never proposed again; another key is (D13)", async () => {
      const k = "A-L3-PARA-W";
      await answer((await openOf(k, "PROPOSED"))!, "REJECTED", "DEVELOPING", "DEVELOPING");
      expect(await stateOf(k)).toMatchObject({ state: "DEVELOPING" }); // the projection runs on the next apply
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("1") })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 0 });
      expect(await stateOf(k)).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: false, proposal: false });
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("2") })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 1 });
    });

    it("apply: ⚑ keeps the level, only one open, never twice for the same evidence, never below level 2; level 4 only from Tự làm được", async () => {
      const k = "A-L3-PARA-W";
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("f") })])).toMatchObject({ flagged: 1 });
      expect(await stateOf(k)).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: true, proposal: true });
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("e") })])).toMatchObject({ flagged: 0 }); // one open ⚑
      await answer((await openOf(k, "FLAG"))!, "CONFIRMED", "DEVELOPING", "DEVELOPING");
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("f") })])).toMatchObject({ flagged: 0 }); // same evidence
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: k, raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("e") })])).toMatchObject({ flagged: 1 }); // new evidence
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: "A-L1-PAST-W", auto: "SUPPORTED" }), row({ target_key: "A-L1-PAST-W", raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("d") })])).toMatchObject({ auto: 1, flagged: 0 });
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: "A-L2-CHUNKS", propose: "TRANSFERABLE", evidence_key: K("4") })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 0 });
    });

    it("apply stores the latest conditions and route; refuses skills outside the set, bad keys, routes and versions; app roles cannot call it", async () => {
      const cond = [{ id: "l1_evidence", level: 1, route: 1, have: 1, need: 1, met: true, evidence: [] }];
      await apply([row({ target_key: "A-L2-COMPENSATE-S", conditions: cond, route: 2 })]);
      expect(await q1("select conditions, route, evaluated_at is not null as at from english.competency_state where learner_id = $1 and target_key = 'A-L2-COMPENSATE-S'", [s.learners.an]))
        .toEqual([{ conditions: cond, route: 2, at: true }]);
      await fail(apply([row({ target_key: "A-A3-REDRAFT" })]), "22023", /not_in_set/);
      await fail(apply([row({ target_key: SKILL, evidence_key: "x" })]), "22023", /bad_results/);
      await fail(apply([row({ target_key: SKILL, route: 3 })]), "22023", /bad_results/);
      await fail(apply([row({ target_key: SKILL, conditions: {} })]), "22023", /bad_results/);
      await fail(apply([], s.learners.an, "bad version!"), "22023", /bad_results/);
      for (const fn of ["eng_engine_inputs(uuid, text[])", "eng_apply_engine_results(uuid, jsonb, text)", "eng_reset_skill_states(uuid, uuid)"]) {
        expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'english.${fn}', 'execute') as ok`))[0]?.ok, fn).toBe(false);
        expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_function_privilege('service_role', 'english.${fn}', 'execute') as ok`))[0]?.ok, fn).toBe(true);
      }
    });

    it("reset: the parent or An's tutor rebuilds An's state from the book (same levels, ⚑ and proposals); Nguyên's tutor cannot", async () => {
      const snap = () => q1("select target_key, state, state_decision_id, flag_decision_id, proposal_decision_id from english.competency_state where learner_id = $1 order by target_key", [s.learners.an]);
      const before = await snap();
      const n = await bookCount();
      expect(await svc<{ targets: number }>("select english.eng_reset_skill_states($1, $2) as r", [s.learners.an, s.members.parent])).toMatchObject({ targets: expect.any(Number) });
      const first = await snap();
      expect(first).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(before)); // every row that existed comes back the same; the other skills of the set get a row
      expect(first.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(12);
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_reset_skill_states($1, $2) as r", [s.learners.an, s.members.tutorAn])).toMatchObject({ targets: first.length });
      expect(await snap()).toEqual(first); // twice → the same
      expect(await bookCount()).toBe(n);
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_reset_skill_states($1, $2) as r", [s.learners.an, s.members.tutorNguyen]), "42501", /staff_only/);
      expect((await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.audit_log where learner_id = $1 and action = 'skill_states.rebuilt'", [s.learners.an]))[0]?.n).toBe(2);
    });
  });
```

### Task 6: Migration 0023 part 3 — the tutor's decisions (Xác nhận / Chưa / Xác nhận tất cả / sửa tay) and the staff reads

**Files:**
- Modify: `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql` (paste sections 5–6 above `-- 8. Purge:`), `tests/unit/migration0023.test.ts`, `tests/db/skillStates.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 4–5; `english.eng_actor_may_manage` (0007); `english.targets` (0005); `english.skill_leaves` / `skill_groups` (0019); `english.audit_log`.
- Produces (all `service_role` only; called after `requireAccess` / `requireStaff`; the SQL checks the actor again):
  - `english.eng_decide_skill(p_decision uuid, p_action text, p_reason text, p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb` = `{id, target_key, replayed}`.
    - `p_action`: `confirm` | `reject`.
    - Errors: `42501 (tutor_only)`, `22023 (bad_action)`, `22023 (not_a_proposal)`, `22023 (flag_keep_or_edit)`, `23514 (reason_required)`, `22023 (reason_too_long)`, `23514 (stale_proposal)`, `23505 (already_answered)`, `P0002`.
    - Audit `skill.confirmed` / `skill.rejected`.
  - `english.eng_confirm_skill_proposals(p_learner uuid, p_ids uuid[], p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb` = `{ids, replayed}`.
    - Proposals only.
    - Errors: `22023 (bad_ids)`, `22023 (flag_not_bulk)`, `22023 (not_a_proposal)`, `23505 (already_answered)`, `23505 (client_reused)`, `P0002 (not_found)`, `42501 (tutor_only)`, `23514 (stale_proposal)`.
    - Audit `skill.confirmed_all`.
  - `english.eng_set_skill_state(p_learner uuid, p_target text, p_state text, p_reason text, p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb` = `{id, replayed}`.
    - `p_state`: `SUPPORTED` | `DEVELOPING`. Closes an open ⚑ (`responds_to`).
    - Errors: `22023 (manual_levels_1_2)`, `23514 (reason_required)`, `22023 (not_in_set)`, `23514 (same_state)`, `23505 (client_reused)`, `42501 (tutor_only)`.
    - Audit `skill.manual`.
  - `english.eng_skill_states(p_learner uuid) returns jsonb` = `{policy_version, set_id, rows: [{target_key, title_vi, domain_key, state, state_kind, state_since, flag: {id, at} | null, proposal: {id, to_state, route, at} | null, conditions, route, evaluated_at, updated_at}]}`, in set order.
  - `english.eng_skill_inbox(p_by uuid, p_allow_real boolean default false) returns jsonb` = `{open, decidable, items: [{id, kind, learner_id, alias, is_test, target_key, title_vi, from_state, to_state, current_state, route, conditions, evidence: [{episode_id, title_vi, step_no, at}], created_at, can_decide}]}`. Open `PROPOSED` / `FLAG` of every child in `p_by`'s scope. The caller must be staff, otherwise `42501 (staff_only)`.
