```ts
  it("Xác nhận tất cả never takes a ⚑, and a ⚑ is never rejected - it is kept or set by hand", () => {
    expect(fnText(sql, "eng_confirm_skill_proposals")).toContain("(flag_not_bulk)");
    expect(fnText(sql, "eng_decide_skill")).toContain("(flag_keep_or_edit)");
    expect(fnText(sql, "eng_set_skill_state")).toContain("p_state not in ('SUPPORTED', 'DEVELOPING')");
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them.** `npx vitest run tests/unit/migration0023.test.ts` → FAIL (`eng_decide_skill` not found).

- [ ] **Step 3: Paste sections 5–6** into `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql`, immediately above the line starting `-- 8. Purge:`:

```sql
-- 5. Gia sư của em trả lời (Xác nhận / Chưa / Xác nhận tất cả) hoặc sửa tay bậc 1–2 -------------------------------------------------------
-- Chỉ gia sư còn quan hệ hiệu lực với em (§1 mục 2, kiểu D15); phụ huynh xem. Lỗi có thẻ (tag) cuối câu để màn hình giải thích.
-- Một đề xuất / ⚑ chỉ được trả lời một lần (chỉ mục duy nhất responds_to). Gửi lại cùng client id + cùng việc = trả lại dòng cũ.
create or replace function english.eng_decide_skill(p_decision uuid, p_action text, p_reason text, p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_d english.mastery_decisions%rowtype;
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_m english.members%rowtype;
  v_prev english.mastery_decisions%rowtype;
  v_cur english.competency_state%rowtype;
  v_reason text := nullif(pg_catalog.btrim(coalesce(p_reason, '')), '');
  v_kind text;
  v_to text;
  v_id uuid;
begin
  if p_decision is null or p_action is null or p_by is null or p_client_id is null then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: decision, action, actor and client id are required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  if p_action not in ('confirm', 'reject') then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: action must be confirm or reject (bad_action)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  select * into v_d from english.mastery_decisions d where d.id = p_decision;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: proposal not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = v_d.learner_id for update;
  select * into v_m from english.members m where m.id = p_by;
  if v_m.id is null or v_m.role <> 'tutor' or not english.eng_actor_may_manage(p_by, v_d.learner_id) then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: only a tutor of this learner confirms a skill level (tutor_only)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_kind := case p_action when 'confirm' then 'CONFIRMED' else 'REJECTED' end;

  select * into v_prev from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = p_decision;
  if found then
    if v_prev.client_id = p_client_id and v_prev.kind = v_kind and v_prev.decided_by_member = p_by then
      return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', v_prev.id, 'target_key', v_prev.target_key, 'replayed', true);
    end if;
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: this proposal was already answered (already_answered)' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  if v_d.kind not in ('PROPOSED', 'FLAG') then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: only a proposal or a flag can be answered (not_a_proposal)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_d.kind = 'FLAG' and p_action = 'reject' then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: a flag is kept ("Giữ bậc") or the level is set by hand, never rejected (flag_keep_or_edit)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_reason is not null and pg_catalog.length(v_reason) > 300 then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: the reason is too long (reason_too_long)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_action = 'reject' and (v_reason is null or pg_catalog.length(v_reason) < 3) then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: "Chưa" needs a reason (reason_required)' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;

  perform english.eng_project_skill_state(v_d.learner_id, v_d.target_key);
  select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = v_d.learner_id and c.target_key = v_d.target_key;
  if v_d.kind = 'PROPOSED' and p_action = 'confirm' and v_d.to_state = 'TRANSFERABLE' and v_cur.state <> 'INDEPENDENT' then
    raise exception 'eng_decide_skill: the level changed since this proposal; reload the page (stale_proposal)' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  -- Xác nhận đề xuất → bậc mới; xác nhận ⚑ ("Giữ bậc") hoặc "Chưa" → bậc giữ nguyên.
  v_to := case when v_d.kind = 'PROPOSED' and p_action = 'confirm' then v_d.to_state else v_cur.state end;
  insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, route, conditions, evidence_episode_ids, policy_version,
                                        decided_by_member, decided_role, reason, responds_to, client_id)
  values (v_d.learner_id, v_d.target_key, v_kind, v_cur.state, v_to, v_d.route, v_d.conditions, v_d.evidence_episode_ids,
          coalesce(english.eng_engine_policy(v_d.learner_id), v_d.policy_version), p_by, 'tutor', v_reason, p_decision, p_client_id)
  returning id into v_id;
  perform english.eng_project_skill_state(v_d.learner_id, v_d.target_key);

  insert into english.audit_log(actor_user_id, actor_role, action, family_id, learner_id, payload)
  values (v_m.user_id, v_m.role, case v_kind when 'CONFIRMED' then 'skill.confirmed' else 'skill.rejected' end, v_l.family_id, v_d.learner_id,
          pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('decision', v_id, 'responds_to', p_decision, 'target_key', v_d.target_key, 'answered', v_d.kind, 'to_state', v_to));
  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', v_id, 'target_key', v_d.target_key, 'replayed', false);
end
$$;

-- "Xác nhận tất cả" (§3 bước 4): đúng các đề xuất lên bậc mà màn hình đã hiện (p_ids), cùng một lần; ⚑ không bao giờ đi cùng. Tất cả hoặc không.
create or replace function english.eng_confirm_skill_proposals(p_learner uuid, p_ids uuid[], p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_m english.members%rowtype;
  v_d english.mastery_decisions%rowtype;
  v_cur english.competency_state%rowtype;
  v_ids uuid[] := '{}';
  v_id uuid;
begin
  if p_learner is null or p_ids is null or p_by is null or p_client_id is null then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: learner, ids, actor and client id are required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  if cardinality(p_ids) not between 1 and 50 or cardinality(p_ids) <> (select pg_catalog.count(distinct x) from unnest(p_ids) as x) then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: 1 to 50 different proposals (bad_ids)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select * into v_m from english.members m where m.id = p_by;
  if v_m.id is null or v_m.role <> 'tutor' or not english.eng_actor_may_manage(p_by, p_learner) then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: only a tutor of this learner confirms skill levels (tutor_only)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  if exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.learner_id = p_learner and a.client_id = p_client_id) then
    if not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a
                   where a.learner_id = p_learner and a.client_id = p_client_id and (a.kind <> 'CONFIRMED' or a.decided_by_member <> p_by))
       and (select array_agg(a.responds_to order by a.responds_to) from english.mastery_decisions a where a.learner_id = p_learner and a.client_id = p_client_id)
           = (select array_agg(x order by x) from unnest(p_ids) as x) then
      return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
        'ids', (select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(a.id order by a.seq) from english.mastery_decisions a where a.learner_id = p_learner and a.client_id = p_client_id),
        'replayed', true);
    end if;
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: this client id was already used for other decisions (client_reused)' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from unnest(p_ids) as x where not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions d where d.id = x and d.learner_id = p_learner)) then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: a proposal of this learner was not found (not_found)' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions d where d.id = any (p_ids) and d.kind = 'FLAG') then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: a flag is looked at one by one, never confirmed in bulk (flag_not_bulk)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions d where d.id = any (p_ids) and d.kind <> 'PROPOSED') then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: only proposals can be confirmed (not_a_proposal)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = any (p_ids)) then
    raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: a proposal was already answered (already_answered)' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;

  for v_d in select d.* from english.mastery_decisions d where d.id = any (p_ids) order by d.seq loop
    perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_d.target_key);
    select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = v_d.target_key;
    if v_d.to_state = 'TRANSFERABLE' and v_cur.state <> 'INDEPENDENT' then
      raise exception 'eng_confirm_skill_proposals: a level changed since the proposal; reload the page (stale_proposal)' using errcode = '23514';
    end if;
    insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, route, conditions, evidence_episode_ids, policy_version,
                                          decided_by_member, decided_role, responds_to, client_id)
    values (p_learner, v_d.target_key, 'CONFIRMED', v_cur.state, v_d.to_state, v_d.route, v_d.conditions, v_d.evidence_episode_ids,
            coalesce(english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner), v_d.policy_version), p_by, 'tutor', v_d.id, p_client_id)
    returning id into v_id;
    v_ids := v_ids || v_id;
    perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, v_d.target_key);
  end loop;

  insert into english.audit_log(actor_user_id, actor_role, action, family_id, learner_id, payload)
  values (v_m.user_id, v_m.role, 'skill.confirmed_all', v_l.family_id, p_learner, pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('count', cardinality(v_ids)));
  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('ids', pg_catalog.to_jsonb(v_ids), 'replayed', false);
end
$$;

-- Gia sư sửa tay bậc 1–2 (§3 bước 5; G3): có lý do; đóng ⚑ đang mở của kỹ năng đó. Không bao giờ đặt bậc 3–4 bằng tay (chỉ qua đề xuất).
create or replace function english.eng_set_skill_state(p_learner uuid, p_target text, p_state text, p_reason text, p_by uuid, p_client_id text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_m english.members%rowtype;
  v_prev english.mastery_decisions%rowtype;
  v_cur english.competency_state%rowtype;
  v_reason text := nullif(pg_catalog.btrim(coalesce(p_reason, '')), '');
  v_id uuid;
begin
  if p_learner is null or p_target is null or p_state is null or p_by is null or p_client_id is null then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: learner, skill, state, actor and client id are required' using errcode = '22004';
  end if;
  if p_state not in ('SUPPORTED', 'DEVELOPING') then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: a level is set by hand only to 1 or 2 (manual_levels_1_2)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_reason is not null and pg_catalog.length(v_reason) > 300 then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: the reason is too long (reason_too_long)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_reason is null or pg_catalog.length(v_reason) < 3 then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: a level set by hand needs a reason (reason_required)' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select * into v_m from english.members m where m.id = p_by;
  if v_m.id is null or v_m.role <> 'tutor' or not english.eng_actor_may_manage(p_by, p_learner) then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: only a tutor of this learner sets a skill level (tutor_only)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not (p_target = any (coalesce(english.eng_engine_scope(p_learner), '{}'::text[]))) then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: this skill is not in the learner''s set (not_in_set)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  select * into v_prev from english.mastery_decisions a where a.learner_id = p_learner and a.client_id = p_client_id and a.target_key = p_target;
  if found then
    if v_prev.kind = 'MANUAL' and v_prev.to_state = p_state and v_prev.decided_by_member = p_by then
      return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', v_prev.id, 'replayed', true);
    end if;
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: this client id was already used (client_reused)' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, p_target);
  select * into v_cur from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = p_target;
  if v_cur.state = p_state then
    raise exception 'eng_set_skill_state: the new level is the current one (same_state)' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;

  insert into english.mastery_decisions(learner_id, target_key, kind, from_state, to_state, policy_version, decided_by_member, decided_role, reason,
                                        responds_to, client_id)
  values (p_learner, p_target, 'MANUAL', v_cur.state, p_state, coalesce(english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner), 'AP-0.3'), p_by, 'tutor', v_reason,
          v_cur.flag_decision_id, p_client_id)
  returning id into v_id;
  perform english.eng_project_skill_state(p_learner, p_target);

  insert into english.audit_log(actor_user_id, actor_role, action, family_id, learner_id, payload)
  values (v_m.user_id, v_m.role, 'skill.manual', v_l.family_id, p_learner,
          pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('decision', v_id, 'target_key', p_target, 'from_state', v_cur.state, 'to_state', p_state));
  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', v_id, 'replayed', false);
end
$$;

revoke execute on function english.eng_decide_skill(uuid, text, text, uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_decide_skill(uuid, text, text, uuid, text) to service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_confirm_skill_proposals(uuid, uuid[], uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_confirm_skill_proposals(uuid, uuid[], uuid, text) to service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_set_skill_state(uuid, text, text, text, uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_set_skill_state(uuid, text, text, text, uuid, text) to service_role;

-- 6. Đọc (phụ huynh / gia sư; máy chủ gọi sau requireAccess / requireStaff) -----------------------------------------------------------
-- Trạng thái mọi kỹ năng của bộ hiện tại, theo thứ tự của bộ (Kỹ năng đang theo dõi, Hồ sơ cấp). Kỹ năng chưa có dòng = "Chưa đủ bài".
create or replace function english.eng_skill_states(p_learner uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_l english.learners%rowtype;
  v_def english.target_set_definitions%rowtype;
  v_map text := english.eng_current_skill_map();
begin
  select * into v_l from english.learners l where l.id = p_learner;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'eng_skill_states: learner not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select d.* into v_def
  from english.target_sets ts join english.target_set_definitions d on d.id = ts.definition_id
  where ts.learner_id = p_learner and ts.effective_from <= pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()
  order by ts.effective_from desc, ts.seq desc limit 1;

  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
    'policy_version', english.eng_engine_policy(p_learner),
    'set_id', v_def.id,
    'rows', coalesce((
      select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
               'target_key', u.k,
               'title_vi', t.title_vi,
               'domain_key', (select g.domain_key from english.skill_leaves sl
                              join english.skill_groups g on g.map_id = sl.map_id and g.key = sl.group_key
                              where sl.map_id = v_map and sl.kind = 'target' and sl.track_id = v_l.track_id and sl.ref_key = u.k),
               'state', coalesce(c.state, 'NOT_OBSERVED'),
               'state_kind', sd.kind,
               'state_since', c.state_since,
               'flag', case when f.id is null then null else pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', f.id, 'at', f.created_at) end,
               'proposal', case when pr.id is null then null
                                else pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'to_state', pr.to_state, 'route', pr.route, 'at', pr.created_at) end,
               'conditions', coalesce(c.conditions, '[]'::jsonb),
               'route', c.route,
               'evaluated_at', c.evaluated_at,
               'updated_at', c.updated_at)
             order by u.o)
      from unnest(coalesce(v_def.target_ids, '{}'::text[])) with ordinality as u(k, o)
      left join english.targets t on t.id = u.k and t.descriptor_version = v_def.descriptor_version
      left join english.competency_state c on c.learner_id = p_learner and c.target_key = u.k
      left join english.mastery_decisions sd on sd.id = c.state_decision_id
      left join english.mastery_decisions f on f.id = c.flag_decision_id
      left join english.mastery_decisions pr on pr.id = c.proposal_decision_id), '[]'::jsonb));
end
$$;

-- Hộp duyệt → tab "Kỹ năng": đề xuất / ⚑ đang mở của mọi em trong phạm vi của p_by (phụ huynh: cả gia đình; gia sư: em còn quan hệ hiệu lực).
-- can_decide = p_by là gia sư của em đó. Không có điểm, không có tên người học thật khi p_allow_real = false (preview).
create or replace function english.eng_skill_inbox(p_by uuid, p_allow_real boolean default false) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_m english.members%rowtype;
begin
  select * into v_m from english.members m where m.id = p_by;
  if v_m.id is null or v_m.role not in ('tutor', 'parent') then
    raise exception 'eng_skill_inbox: staff only (staff_only)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return (
    with pending as (
      select d.*, l.alias, l.is_test
      from english.mastery_decisions d
      join english.learners l on l.id = d.learner_id
      where english.eng_actor_may_manage(p_by, l.id) and (p_allow_real or l.is_test)
        and d.kind in ('PROPOSED', 'FLAG')
        and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = d.id)
        and d.target_key = any (coalesce(english.eng_engine_scope(d.learner_id), '{}'::text[]))
    )
    select pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
      'open', (select pg_catalog.count(*) from pending)::int,
      'decidable', case when v_m.role = 'tutor' then (select pg_catalog.count(*) from pending)::int else 0 end,
      'items', coalesce((
        select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                 'id', p.id, 'kind', p.kind, 'learner_id', p.learner_id, 'alias', p.alias, 'is_test', p.is_test,
                 'target_key', p.target_key,
                 'title_vi', (select t.title_vi from english.targets t where t.id = p.target_key order by t.descriptor_version desc limit 1),
                 'from_state', p.from_state, 'to_state', p.to_state,
                 'current_state', coalesce((select c.state from english.competency_state c where c.learner_id = p.learner_id and c.target_key = p.target_key), 'NOT_OBSERVED'),
                 'route', p.route, 'conditions', p.conditions,
                 'evidence', coalesce((
                   select pg_catalog.jsonb_agg(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(
                            'episode_id', e.id, 'title_vi', i.snapshot -> 'template' ->> 'title_vi', 'step_no', a.step_no, 'at', t.submitted_at)
                          order by t.submitted_at, e.id)
                   from english.episodes e
                   join english.attempts t on t.id = e.attempt_id
                   join english.activity_instances a on a.id = t.activity_instance_id
                   join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id
                   where e.id = any (p.evidence_episode_ids)), '[]'::jsonb),
                 'created_at', p.created_at,
                 'can_decide', v_m.role = 'tutor')
               order by p.alias, p.seq)
        from pending p), '[]'::jsonb)));
end
$$;

revoke execute on function english.eng_skill_states(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_skill_states(uuid) to service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_skill_inbox(uuid, boolean) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function english.eng_skill_inbox(uuid, boolean) to service_role;

```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the unit test.** `npx vitest run tests/unit/migration0023.test.ts` → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the DB tests (section 3).** In `tests/db/skillStates.test.ts`, replace `  // (Tasks 6 and 7 add their sections here)` with the section below, followed by `  // (Task 7 adds its section here)`.

```ts
  describe("3. the tutor decides, the parent reads, the student gets nothing", () => {
    type Out = { id: string; target_key?: string; ids?: string[]; replayed: boolean };
    const decide = (decision: string, action: string, by: string, reason: string | null = null, clientId = `decide-${randomUUID()}`) =>
      svc<Out>("select english.eng_decide_skill($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) as r", [decision, action, reason, by, clientId]);
    const confirmAll = (ids: string[], by: string, clientId = `all-${randomUUID()}`, learner = s.learners.an) =>
      svc<Out>("select english.eng_confirm_skill_proposals($1, $2::uuid[], $3, $4) as r", [learner, ids, by, clientId]);
    const manual = (target: string, state: string, reason: string | null, by: string, clientId = `manual-${randomUUID()}`) =>
      svc<Out>("select english.eng_set_skill_state($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6) as r", [s.learners.an, target, state, reason, by, clientId]);
    const ev = () => ({ auto: [], propose: proof.map((p) => p.episodeId), flag: [proof[2]!.episodeId] });
    let p1 = ""; // A-T7-OPINION: DEVELOPING → INDEPENDENT (to reject, then confirm a new one)
    let p2 = ""; // A-X1-TURN: DEVELOPING → INDEPENDENT (confirm-all)
    let p3 = ""; // A-L4-PROCESS-S: DEVELOPING → INDEPENDENT (confirm-all)
    let f1 = ""; // A-L4-PROCESS-S ⚑
    beforeAll(async () => {
      await apply([
        row({ target_key: "A-T7-OPINION", auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("7"), evidence: ev() }),
        row({ target_key: "A-X1-TURN", auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("8"), evidence: ev() }),
        row({ target_key: "A-L4-PROCESS-S", auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("9"), evidence_key: K("9"), evidence: ev() }),
      ]);
      [p1, p2, p3, f1] = [await openOf("A-T7-OPINION", "PROPOSED"), await openOf("A-X1-TURN", "PROPOSED"), await openOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "PROPOSED"), await openOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "FLAG")] as string[];
    });

    it("only An's tutor answers: the parent and Nguyên's tutor get 42501 (tutor_only); no app role can execute the functions", async () => {
      await fail(decide(p1, "confirm", s.members.parent), "42501", /tutor_only/);
      await fail(decide(p1, "confirm", s.members.tutorNguyen), "42501", /tutor_only/);
      await fail(confirmAll([p2], s.members.parent), "42501", /tutor_only/);
      await fail(manual("A-T7-OPINION", "SUPPORTED", "Lý do thử", s.members.parent), "42501", /tutor_only/);
      for (const fn of ["eng_decide_skill(uuid, text, text, uuid, text)", "eng_confirm_skill_proposals(uuid, uuid[], uuid, text)", "eng_set_skill_state(uuid, text, text, text, uuid, text)", "eng_skill_states(uuid)", "eng_skill_inbox(uuid, boolean)"]) {
        expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'english.${fn}', 'execute') as ok`))[0]?.ok, fn).toBe(false);
      }
    });

    it("'Chưa' needs a reason, keeps the level and closes the proposal; a second answer is refused; the same request again is a replay", async () => {
      await fail(decide(p1, "reject", s.members.tutorAn, " x "), "23514", /reason_required/);
      const out = await decide(p1, "reject", s.members.tutorAn, "Bài thứ ba còn dựa vào câu mẫu", "decide-reject-0001");
      expect(out).toMatchObject({ target_key: "A-T7-OPINION", replayed: false });
      expect(await stateOf("A-T7-OPINION")).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: false, proposal: false });
      expect(await decide(p1, "reject", s.members.tutorAn, "Bài thứ ba còn dựa vào câu mẫu", "decide-reject-0001")).toMatchObject({ id: out.id, replayed: true });
      await fail(decide(p1, "confirm", s.members.tutorAn), "23505", /already_answered/);
      // the receipt names the decision, never the reason
      expect(await q1("select payload ? 'reason' as has_reason from english.audit_log where learner_id = $1 and action = 'skill.rejected'", [s.learners.an])).toEqual([{ has_reason: false }]);
    });

    it("'Xác nhận' moves the level; 'Giữ bậc' keeps it and closes ⚑; a ⚑ is never rejected", async () => {
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: "A-T7-OPINION", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("6"), evidence: ev() })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 1 });
      await decide((await openOf("A-T7-OPINION", "PROPOSED"))!, "confirm", s.members.tutorAn);
      expect(await stateOf("A-T7-OPINION")).toEqual({ state: "INDEPENDENT", flag: false, proposal: false });
      await fail(decide(f1, "reject", s.members.tutorAn, "Không đồng ý"), "22023", /flag_keep_or_edit/);
      await decide(f1, "confirm", s.members.tutorAn);
      expect(await stateOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S")).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: false, proposal: true });
    });

    it("'Xác nhận tất cả' confirms exactly the shown proposals, refuses a ⚑, is all-or-nothing and replayable", async () => {
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: "A-L4-PROCESS-S", raise_flag: true, flag_key: K("a") })])).toMatchObject({ flagged: 1 });
      const f2 = (await openOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "FLAG"))!;
      const n = await bookCount();
      await fail(confirmAll([p2, f2], s.members.tutorAn), "22023", /flag_not_bulk/);
      await fail(confirmAll([p2, p2], s.members.tutorAn), "22023", /bad_ids/);
      await fail(confirmAll([p2, randomUUID()], s.members.tutorAn), "P0002");
      expect(await bookCount()).toBe(n);
      const out = await confirmAll([p2, p3], s.members.tutorAn, "all-0000-0001");
      expect(out.ids).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(await confirmAll([p3, p2], s.members.tutorAn, "all-0000-0001")).toMatchObject({ replayed: true });
      expect([await stateOf("A-X1-TURN"), await stateOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S")]).toEqual([
        { state: "INDEPENDENT", flag: false, proposal: false },
        { state: "INDEPENDENT", flag: true, proposal: false },
      ]);
      await fail(confirmAll([p2], s.members.tutorAn), "23505", /already_answered/);
    });

    it("manual: only levels 1–2, a reason, a skill of the set, a different level; it closes an open ⚑ (G3)", async () => {
      await fail(manual("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "INDEPENDENT", "Lý do đủ dài", s.members.tutorAn), "22023", /manual_levels_1_2/);
      await fail(manual("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "DEVELOPING", " ", s.members.tutorAn), "23514", /reason_required/);
      await fail(manual("A-A3-REDRAFT", "DEVELOPING", "Lý do đủ dài", s.members.tutorAn), "22023", /not_in_set/);
      const out = await manual("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "DEVELOPING", "Hai bài gần đây đều cần câu mẫu", s.members.tutorAn, "manual-0000-0001");
      expect(await stateOf("A-L4-PROCESS-S")).toEqual({ state: "DEVELOPING", flag: false, proposal: false });
      expect(await manual("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "DEVELOPING", "Hai bài gần đây đều cần câu mẫu", s.members.tutorAn, "manual-0000-0001")).toMatchObject({ id: out.id, replayed: true });
      await fail(manual("A-L4-PROCESS-S", "DEVELOPING", "Lý do khác", s.members.tutorAn), "23514", /same_state/);
    });

    it("eng_skill_states: the 12 skills of A-v1 in set order with state, kind, ⚑, proposal, domain and title", async () => {
      const r = await svc<{ policy_version: string; set_id: string; rows: { target_key: string; title_vi: string; domain_key: string; state: string; state_kind: string | null; flag: unknown; proposal: unknown }[] }>(
        "select english.eng_skill_states($1) as r", [s.learners.an]);
      expect([r.policy_version, r.set_id]).toEqual(["AP-0.3", "A-v1"]);
      expect(r.rows.map((x) => x.target_key)).toEqual((await q1<{ t: string[] }>("select target_ids as t from english.target_set_definitions where id = 'A-v1'"))[0]!.t);
      expect(r.rows.find((x) => x.target_key === "A-X1-TURN")).toMatchObject({ state: "INDEPENDENT", state_kind: "CONFIRMED", flag: null, proposal: null, domain_key: "speaking" });
      expect(r.rows.find((x) => x.target_key === "A-L4-PROCESS-S")).toMatchObject({ state: "DEVELOPING", state_kind: "MANUAL" });
      expect(r.rows.find((x) => x.target_key === "A-T1-TEXT-R")).toMatchObject({ state: "NOT_OBSERVED", state_kind: null, domain_key: "reading" });
      expect(r.rows.every((x) => typeof x.title_vi === "string" && x.title_vi.length > 0)).toBe(true);
    });

    it("eng_skill_inbox: An's tutor can decide An's open items (with evidence links); the parent sees them read-only; Nguyên's tutor sees none; a student is refused", async () => {
      expect(await apply([row({ target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-S", auto: "DEVELOPING", propose: "INDEPENDENT", evidence_key: K("b"), evidence: ev() })])).toMatchObject({ proposed: 1 });
      type Inbox = { open: number; decidable: number; items: { kind: string; learner_id: string; target_key: string; can_decide: boolean; evidence: { episode_id: string; step_no: number }[] }[] };
      const tutor = await svc<Inbox>("select english.eng_skill_inbox($1, false) as r", [s.members.tutorAn]);
      const mine = tutor.items.find((i) => i.target_key === "A-L3-CAUSE-S")!;
      expect(mine).toMatchObject({ kind: "PROPOSED", learner_id: s.learners.an, can_decide: true });
      expect(mine.evidence.map((e) => e.episode_id)).toEqual(proof.map((p) => p.episodeId));
      expect(mine.evidence.map((e) => e.step_no)).toEqual([11, 12, 13]);
      expect(tutor.decidable).toBe(tutor.open);
      const parent = await svc<Inbox>("select english.eng_skill_inbox($1, false) as r", [s.members.parent]);
      expect(parent.items.map((i) => i.target_key)).toEqual(tutor.items.map((i) => i.target_key));
      expect(parent.items.every((i) => !i.can_decide)).toBe(true);
      expect(parent.decidable).toBe(0);
      expect((await svc<Inbox>("select english.eng_skill_inbox($1, false) as r", [s.members.tutorNguyen])).items.filter((i) => i.learner_id === s.learners.an)).toEqual([]);
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_skill_inbox($1, false) as r", [s.members.studentAn]), "42501", /staff_only/);
    });
  });
```

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/engine/shape.ts`, `lib/engine/runner.ts`, `lib/engine/supabaseStore.ts`, `lib/engine/hook.ts`, `scripts/lib/enginePg.ts`, `tests/unit/engineRunner.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/db/skillStates.test.ts` (section 4)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 2–3: `computeTargetState`, `projectDecisions`, the types;
  - Task 5 functions: `eng_engine_inputs`, `eng_apply_engine_results`, `eng_reset_skill_states`;
  - `ReviewDbError` (`lib/review/handlers.ts`), `logAppError` (`lib/ops/logAppError.ts`), `createAdminClient`.
- Produces:
  - `lib/engine/shape.ts`:
    - `type EngineInputs`, `parseEngineInputs(raw: unknown): EngineInputs`;
    - `sliceTarget(inputs: EngineInputs, key: string): EngineInput`;
    - `type ApplyRow = { target_key; auto; propose; raise_flag; flag_key; route; evidence_key; conditions; evidence: {auto; propose; flag} }`, `toApplyRow(r: EngineResult): ApplyRow`;
    - `type Applied = { auto: number; proposed: number; flagged: number; policyVersion: string | null }`, `parseApplied(raw: unknown): Applied`;
    - `MAX_EVIDENCE = 200`.
  - `lib/engine/runner.ts`:
    - `interface EngineStore { loadInputs(learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null): Promise<unknown>; applyResults(learnerId: string, rows: ApplyRow[], engineVersion: string): Promise<unknown>; resetStates(learnerId: string, byMemberId: string): Promise<unknown> }`;
    - `type RecomputeSummary = { learnerId: string; results: EngineResult[]; applied: Applied }`;
    - `recomputeLearner(store: EngineStore, learnerId: string, targets?: string[] | null): Promise<RecomputeSummary>`;
    - `rebuildLearner(store: EngineStore, learnerId: string, byMemberId: string): Promise<RecomputeSummary>`;
    - `type EngineHook = (learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null) => Promise<void>`;
    - `makeEngineHook(store: () => EngineStore, log: (err: unknown) => Promise<void>): EngineHook`;
    - `isMissingFunction(err: unknown): boolean`.
  - `lib/engine/supabaseStore.ts`: `createSupabaseEngineStore(): EngineStore` (server-only).
  - `lib/engine/hook.ts`: `engineHook(route: string, userId: string | null): EngineHook` (server-only).
  - `scripts/lib/enginePg.ts`: `pgEngineStore(client: ClientBase): EngineStore`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { isMissingFunction, makeEngineHook, rebuildLearner, recomputeLearner, type EngineStore } from "@/lib/engine/runner";
import { MAX_EVIDENCE, parseApplied, parseEngineInputs, sliceTarget, toApplyRow } from "@/lib/engine/shape";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, type EngineResult } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { AP03 } from "./fixtures/engine";

/** What english.eng_engine_inputs returns (timestamps as Postgres writes them in jsonb: offset + microseconds). */
const raw = (o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  learner_id: "L1",
  now: "2026-10-23T12:00:00.123456+07:00",
  policy: { version: "AP-0.3", config: AP03.config },
  targets: [{ key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", domain: "writing" }, { key: "A-T1-TEXT-R", domain: "reading" }],
  episodes: [{
    episode_id: "ep1", attempt_id: "at1", activity_id: "act1", at: "2026-10-20T12:00:00.654321+07:00", context_id: "A-W1-VOLCANOES",
    is_first_attempt: true, mode: "INDEPENDENT_CHECK", observed_directly: false,
    facets: { baseline: false, checkpoint: true, delayed_check: false, transfer_check: false }, declared_unknown: false,
    assistance: [{ kind: "notes", verification: "declared", targets: ["A-T1-TEXT-R"] }],
    scores: [
      { target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", decision_id: "vd1", score: 3, not_observed: false },
      { target_key: "A-T1-TEXT-R", decision_id: "vd2", score: null, not_observed: true },
    ],
  }],
  auto: [{
    attempt_id: "aa1", episode_id: "ae1", activity_id: "act2", at: "2026-10-21T09:00:00+07:00", is_first_attempt: true, mode: "INDEPENDENT_CHECK",
    facets: {}, declared_unknown: false, assistance: [],
    items: [{ item_id: "q1", target_key: "A-T1-TEXT-R", result: "correct" }, { item_id: "q2", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", result: "incorrect" }],
  }],
  exposures: [
    { target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", at: "2026-10-20T12:00:00+07:00", attempt_id: "at1", context_id: "A-W1-VOLCANOES" },
    { target_key: "A-T1-TEXT-R", at: "2026-10-19T12:00:00+07:00", attempt_id: null, context_id: null },
  ],
  decisions: [{
    id: "md1", seq: 5, target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", kind: "AUTO", from_state: "NOT_OBSERVED", to_state: "SUPPORTED",
    evidence_key: "1-0000000000000000", responds_to: null, created_at: "2026-10-20T13:00:00.5+07:00",
  }],
  ...o,
});
const store = (over: Partial<EngineStore> = {}) => ({
  loadInputs: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => raw()),
  applyResults: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ auto: 1, proposed: 0, flagged: 0, policy_version: "AP-0.3" })),
  resetStates: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ targets: 2 })),
  ...over,
}) satisfies EngineStore;

describe("parseEngineInputs + sliceTarget", () => {
  it("one skill sees its own score of each episode, only the help that lists it, its own items, exposures and decisions", () => {
    const inputs = parseEngineInputs(raw());
    const w = sliceTarget(inputs, "A-L3-CAUSE-W");
    expect(w).toMatchObject({ targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", domain: "writing", policy: { version: "AP-0.3" } });
    expect(w.episodes).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ episodeId: "ep1", decisionId: "vd1", score: 3, assistance: [], facets: { baseline: false, checkpoint: true, delayedCheck: false, transferCheck: false, revision: false } })]);
    expect(w.autoAttempts.map((a) => a.items)).toEqual([[{ itemId: "q2", result: "incorrect" }]]);
    expect(w.exposures).toEqual([{ at: expect.any(String), contextId: "A-W1-VOLCANOES", attemptId: "at1" }]);
    expect(w.decisions).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id: "md1", seq: 5, kind: "AUTO", toState: "SUPPORTED" })]);
    const r = sliceTarget(inputs, "A-T1-TEXT-R");
    expect(r.episodes[0]).toMatchObject({ decisionId: "vd2", score: null, assistance: [{ kind: "notes", verification: "declared" }] });
    expect(r.autoAttempts[0]?.items).toEqual([{ itemId: "q1", result: "correct" }]);
    expect(r.decisions).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("reads Postgres timestamps (offset + microseconds) as dates; refuses unknown states and item results", () => {
    const w = sliceTarget(parseEngineInputs(raw()), "A-L3-CAUSE-W");
    expect(w.now.toISOString()).toBe("2026-10-23T05:00:00.123Z");
    expect(Date.parse(w.episodes[0]!.at)).toBe(Date.parse("2026-10-20T05:00:00.654Z"));
    expect(() => parseEngineInputs(raw({ decisions: [{ ...raw().decisions[0], to_state: "NEEDS_CONFIRMATION" }] }))).toThrow();
    expect(() => parseEngineInputs(raw({ auto: [{ ...raw().auto[0], items: [{ item_id: "q", target_key: "x", result: "needs_person" }] }] }))).toThrow();
  });
});

describe("recomputeLearner / rebuildLearner", () => {
  it("loads once, runs the engine for every skill of the inputs and applies one row per skill with the engine version", async () => {
    const s = store();
    const out = await recomputeLearner(s, "L1", null);
    expect(s.loadInputs).toHaveBeenCalledWith("L1", null);
    const [learner, rows, version] = s.applyResults.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, { target_key: string }[], string];
    expect([learner, rows.map((r) => r.target_key), version]).toEqual(["L1", ["A-L3-CAUSE-W", "A-T1-TEXT-R"], ENGINE_VERSION]);
    expect(out.results.map((r) => r.targetKey)).toEqual(["A-L3-CAUSE-W", "A-T1-TEXT-R"]);
    expect(out.applied).toEqual({ auto: 1, proposed: 0, flagged: 0, policyVersion: "AP-0.3" });
  });

  it("writes nothing for a child without a skill set (no policy) or without skills", async () => {
    for (const r of [raw({ policy: null }), raw({ targets: [] })]) {
      const s = store({ loadInputs: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => r) });
      expect((await recomputeLearner(s, "L1")).applied).toEqual({ auto: 0, proposed: 0, flagged: 0, policyVersion: null });
      expect(s.applyResults).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    }
  });

  it("rebuild re-projects first, then runs the engine on every skill", async () => {
    const s = store();
    await rebuildLearner(s, "L1", "m-tutor");
    expect(s.resetStates).toHaveBeenCalledWith("L1", "m-tutor");
    expect(s.loadInputs).toHaveBeenCalledWith("L1", null);
    expect(s.resetStates.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]).toBeLessThan(s.loadInputs.mock.invocationCallOrder[0] as number);
  });

  it("toApplyRow keeps at most 200 evidence ids per list; parseApplied reads the SQL answer", () => {
    const many = Array.from({ length: 250 }, (_, i) => `e${i}`);
    const r = { targetKey: "K", auto: null, propose: "INDEPENDENT", raiseFlag: false, flag: { conflict: false, key: null, evidence: [] }, route: 1, evidenceKey: "1-0000000000000000", conditions: [], evidence: { auto: [], propose: many, flag: [] } } as unknown as EngineResult;
    expect(toApplyRow(r).evidence.propose).toHaveLength(MAX_EVIDENCE);
    expect(toApplyRow(r)).toMatchObject({ target_key: "K", propose: "INDEPENDENT", raise_flag: false, flag_key: null, route: 1 });
    expect(() => parseApplied({ auto: "1" })).toThrow();
  });
});

describe("makeEngineHook (D16: never fails the write)", () => {
  it("logs an engine error and resolves; skips silently when the functions are not there yet (0023 not applied)", async () => {
    const log = vi.fn(async () => {});
    const broken = store({ loadInputs: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => { throw new Error("boom"); }) });
    await expect(makeEngineHook(() => broken, log)("L1", null)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const missing = store({ loadInputs: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => { throw Object.assign(new Error("fn"), { code: "PGRST202" }); }) });
    await makeEngineHook(() => missing, log)("L1", ["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]);
    expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect([isMissingFunction({ code: "42883" }), isMissingFunction({ code: "42501" }), isMissingFunction(null)]).toEqual([true, false, false]);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Parse english.eng_engine_inputs (migration 0023) and cut it into one engine input per skill. Relative imports only (Node scripts use it). */
import { z } from "zod";
import { assessmentPolicySchema } from "../config/schemas";
import { DOMAIN_KEYS } from "../skillmap/types";
import { DECISION_KINDS, SKILL_STATES, type AssistanceMark, type EngineInput, type EngineResult, type Facets, type MasteryDecision } from "./types";

/** Postgres writes microseconds; JavaScript dates keep milliseconds. Cut the extra digits so every engine sees the same moment. */
const at = z.string().min(1).transform((s) => s.replace(/(\.\d{3})\d+/, "$1"));
const id = z.string().min(1);
const mode = z.enum(["PRACTICE", "INDEPENDENT_CHECK", "REVISION"]);
const facets = z
  .object({
    baseline: z.boolean().optional(),
    checkpoint: z.boolean().optional(),
    delayed_check: z.boolean().optional(),
    transfer_check: z.boolean().optional(),
    revision: z.boolean().optional(),
  })
  .transform((f): Facets => ({
    baseline: f.baseline ?? false,
    checkpoint: f.checkpoint ?? false,
    delayedCheck: f.delayed_check ?? false,
    transferCheck: f.transfer_check ?? false,
    revision: f.revision ?? false,
  }));
const assistance = z.array(z.object({ kind: z.string(), verification: z.enum(["observed", "declared", "unknown"]), targets: z.array(z.string()) }));
const episodeRow = z.object({
  episode_id: id, attempt_id: id, activity_id: id, at, context_id: z.string(), is_first_attempt: z.boolean(), mode,
  observed_directly: z.boolean(), facets, declared_unknown: z.boolean(), assistance,
  scores: z.array(z.object({ target_key: z.string(), decision_id: id, score: z.number().int().min(0).max(4).nullable(), not_observed: z.boolean() })),
});
const autoRow = z.object({
  attempt_id: id, episode_id: id, activity_id: id, at, is_first_attempt: z.boolean(), mode, facets, declared_unknown: z.boolean(), assistance,
  items: z.array(z.object({ item_id: z.string(), target_key: z.string(), result: z.enum(["correct", "incorrect", "blank"]) })),
});
const exposureRow = z.object({ target_key: z.string(), at, attempt_id: z.string().nullable(), context_id: z.string().nullable() });
const decisionRow = z.object({
  id, seq: z.number().int(), target_key: z.string(), kind: z.enum(DECISION_KINDS), from_state: z.enum(SKILL_STATES), to_state: z.enum(SKILL_STATES),
  evidence_key: z.string().nullable(), responds_to: z.string().nullable(), created_at: at,
});
const inputsRow = z.object({
  learner_id: id,
  now: at,
  policy: z.object({ version: z.string(), config: assessmentPolicySchema }).nullable(),
  targets: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), domain: z.enum(DOMAIN_KEYS).nullable() })),
  episodes: z.array(episodeRow),
  auto: z.array(autoRow),
  exposures: z.array(exposureRow),
  decisions: z.array(decisionRow),
});
export type EngineInputs = z.infer<typeof inputsRow>;

export function parseEngineInputs(raw: unknown): EngineInputs {
  return inputsRow.parse(raw);
}

const marks = (rows: z.infer<typeof assistance>, key: string): AssistanceMark[] =>
  rows.filter((r) => r.targets.includes(key)).map((r) => ({ kind: r.kind, verification: r.verification }));

/** The evidence of ONE skill: its own score of each validated episode (one per episode: counting is per episode), its items, exposures, decisions. */
export function sliceTarget(inputs: EngineInputs, key: string): EngineInput {
  if (inputs.policy === null) throw new Error("sliceTarget: this learner has no engine policy");
  return {
    targetKey: key,
    domain: inputs.targets.find((t) => t.key === key)?.domain ?? null,
    episodes: inputs.episodes.flatMap((e) => {
      const sc = e.scores.find((x) => x.target_key === key);
      if (!sc) return [];
      const score = sc.not_observed ? null : (sc.score as 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | null);
      return [{
        episodeId: e.episode_id, attemptId: e.attempt_id, activityId: e.activity_id, at: e.at, contextId: e.context_id,
        isFirstAttempt: e.is_first_attempt, mode: e.mode, observedDirectly: e.observed_directly, facets: e.facets,
        assistance: marks(e.assistance, key), declaredUnknown: e.declared_unknown, decisionId: sc.decision_id, score,
      }];
    }),
    autoAttempts: inputs.auto.flatMap((a) => {
      const items = a.items.filter((i) => i.target_key === key).map((i) => ({ itemId: i.item_id, result: i.result }));
      if (items.length === 0) return [];
      return [{
        attemptId: a.attempt_id, episodeId: a.episode_id, activityId: a.activity_id, at: a.at, isFirstAttempt: a.is_first_attempt, mode: a.mode,
        facets: a.facets, assistance: marks(a.assistance, key), declaredUnknown: a.declared_unknown, items,
      }];
    }),
    exposures: inputs.exposures.filter((x) => x.target_key === key).map((x) => ({ at: x.at, contextId: x.context_id, attemptId: x.attempt_id })),
    decisions: inputs.decisions
      .filter((d) => d.target_key === key)
      .map((d): MasteryDecision => ({
        id: d.id, seq: d.seq, kind: d.kind, fromState: d.from_state, toState: d.to_state, evidenceKey: d.evidence_key, respondsTo: d.responds_to, createdAt: d.created_at,
      })),
    policy: { version: inputs.policy.version, config: inputs.policy.config },
    now: new Date(inputs.now),
  };
}

/** Same cap as the CHECK on mastery_decisions.evidence_episode_ids. */
export const MAX_EVIDENCE = 200;
export type ApplyRow = {
  target_key: string;
  auto: string | null;
  propose: string | null;
  raise_flag: boolean;
  flag_key: string | null;
  route: 1 | 2 | null;
  evidence_key: string;
  conditions: EngineResult["conditions"];
  evidence: { auto: string[]; propose: string[]; flag: string[] };
};
const cap = (xs: readonly string[]) => xs.slice(0, MAX_EVIDENCE);
export function toApplyRow(r: EngineResult): ApplyRow {
  return {
    target_key: r.targetKey,
    auto: r.auto,
    propose: r.propose,
    raise_flag: r.raiseFlag,
    flag_key: r.flag.key,
    route: r.route,
    evidence_key: r.evidenceKey,
    conditions: r.conditions.map((c) => ({ ...c, evidence: cap(c.evidence) })),
    evidence: { auto: cap(r.evidence.auto), propose: cap(r.evidence.propose), flag: cap(r.evidence.flag) },
  };
}

export type Applied = { auto: number; proposed: number; flagged: number; policyVersion: string | null };
const appliedRow = z.object({ auto: z.number().int(), proposed: z.number().int(), flagged: z.number().int(), policy_version: z.string().nullable() });
export function parseApplied(raw: unknown): Applied {
  const a = appliedRow.parse(raw);
  return { auto: a.auto, proposed: a.proposed, flagged: a.flagged, policyVersion: a.policy_version };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `lib/engine/runner.ts`**

```ts
/** The server side of the S7a engine (spec §3): load → compute every skill → apply. Pure of any framework; the stores do the I/O. */
import { computeTargetState } from "./computeTargetState";
import { parseApplied, parseEngineInputs, sliceTarget, toApplyRow, type Applied, type ApplyRow } from "./shape";
import { ENGINE_VERSION, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/** The engine functions of migration 0023 (service role; always after requireAccess or after a write that passed it). */
export interface EngineStore {
  loadInputs(learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null): Promise<unknown>;
  applyResults(learnerId: string, rows: ApplyRow[], engineVersion: string): Promise<unknown>;
  resetStates(learnerId: string, byMemberId: string): Promise<unknown>;
}

export type RecomputeSummary = { learnerId: string; results: EngineResult[]; applied: Applied };
const NOTHING: Applied = { auto: 0, proposed: 0, flagged: 0, policyVersion: null };

/** Recompute the skills of one child (null = every skill of the current set; keys outside the set are ignored, D11) and write the outcome. */
export async function recomputeLearner(store: EngineStore, learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null = null): Promise<RecomputeSummary> {
  const inputs = parseEngineInputs(await store.loadInputs(learnerId, targets));
  if (inputs.policy === null || inputs.targets.length === 0) return { learnerId, results: [], applied: NOTHING };
  const results = inputs.targets.map((t) => computeTargetState(sliceTarget(inputs, t.key)));
  const applied = parseApplied(await store.applyResults(learnerId, results.map(toApplyRow), ENGINE_VERSION));
  return { learnerId, results, applied };
}

/** "Tính lại từ đầu" (§3 step 6): re-project the state from the book, then run the engine on every skill. Twice in a row → the same state, no new row. */
export async function rebuildLearner(store: EngineStore, learnerId: string, byMemberId: string): Promise<RecomputeSummary> {
  await store.resetStates(learnerId, byMemberId);
  return recomputeLearner(store, learnerId, null);
}

/** PostgREST "function not found" (PGRST202) or Postgres undefined_function (42883): migration 0023 is not applied yet. */
export function isMissingFunction(err: unknown): boolean {
  const code = (err as { code?: unknown } | null)?.code;
  return code === "PGRST202" || code === "42883";
}

/** Run after a successful write (D16). Never throws: the user's write already succeeded. */
export type EngineHook = (learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null) => Promise<void>;
export function makeEngineHook(store: () => EngineStore, log: (err: unknown) => Promise<void>): EngineHook {
  return async (learnerId, targets) => {
    try {
      await recomputeLearner(store(), learnerId, targets);
    } catch (err) {
      if (isMissingFunction(err)) return;
      await log(err);
    }
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `lib/engine/supabaseStore.ts`**

```ts
import "server-only";
import { ReviewDbError } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import type { EngineStore } from "./runner";

/** The engine functions of migration 0023 through the service role. Call only after requireAccess (or after a write that passed it). */
export function createSupabaseEngineStore(): EngineStore {
  const call = async (fn: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<unknown> => {
    const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc(fn, args);
    if (error) throw new ReviewDbError(error.code ?? "unknown", `${fn}: ${error.message ?? error.code ?? "error"}`);
    return data;
  };
  return {
    loadInputs: (learnerId, targets) => call("eng_engine_inputs", { p_learner: learnerId, p_targets: targets }),
    applyResults: (learnerId, rows, engineVersion) => call("eng_apply_engine_results", { p_learner: learnerId, p_results: rows, p_engine_version: engineVersion }),
    resetStates: (learnerId, byMemberId) => call("eng_reset_skill_states", { p_learner: learnerId, p_by: byMemberId }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `lib/engine/hook.ts`**

```ts
import "server-only";
import { logAppError } from "@/lib/ops/logAppError";
import { makeEngineHook, type EngineHook } from "./runner";
import { createSupabaseEngineStore } from "./supabaseStore";

/** The hook the route glue hands to write handlers: recompute the child's skills after the write; errors go to app_errors (engine_recompute). */
export function engineHook(route: string, userId: string | null): EngineHook {
  return makeEngineHook(createSupabaseEngineStore, (error) => logAppError({ route, code: "engine_recompute", error, userId }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `scripts/lib/enginePg.ts`**

```ts
/** EngineStore over a pg connection (DB tests, CI): each call runs as service_role in its own committed transaction, like the app's admin client. */
import type { ClientBase } from "pg";
import type { EngineStore } from "../../lib/engine/runner";

export function pgEngineStore(client: ClientBase): EngineStore {
  const call = async (sql: string, params: unknown[]): Promise<unknown> => {
    await client.query("begin");
    try {
      await client.query("set local role service_role");
      const r = await client.query<{ r: unknown }>(sql, params);
      await client.query("commit");
      return r.rows[0]?.r;
    } catch (err) {
      await client.query("rollback");
      throw err;
    }
  };
  return {
    loadInputs: (learnerId, targets) => call("select english.eng_engine_inputs($1, $2::text[]) as r", [learnerId, targets]),
    applyResults: (learnerId, rows, engineVersion) => call("select english.eng_apply_engine_results($1, $2::jsonb, $3) as r", [learnerId, JSON.stringify(rows), engineVersion]),
    resetStates: (learnerId, byMemberId) => call("select english.eng_reset_skill_states($1, $2) as r", [learnerId, byMemberId]),
  };
}
```

```ts
const FAMILY = "f0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const AN = "a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const NGUYEN = "b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DECISION = "d0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const P1 = "d0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002";
const NOW = new Date("2026-10-23T08:00:00Z");
const mk = (id: string, role: Member["role"]): Member => ({ id: `m-${id}`, userId: `u-${id}`, familyId: FAMILY, role, displayName: id });
const members = [mk("parent", "parent"), mk("tutorAn", "tutor"), mk("tutorNguyen", "tutor"), mk("studentAn", "student")];
const learners: Learner[] = [
  { id: AN, familyId: FAMILY, memberId: "m-studentAn", alias: "An", trackId: "A", isTest: true, status: "active" },
  { id: NGUYEN, familyId: FAMILY, memberId: null, alias: "Nguyên", trackId: "B", isTest: true, status: "active" },
];
const rels: Relationship[] = [
  { memberId: "m-tutorAn", learnerId: AN, relation: "tutor", validFrom: new Date("2026-01-01"), validTo: null },
  { memberId: "m-tutorNguyen", learnerId: NGUYEN, relation: "tutor", validFrom: new Date("2026-01-01"), validTo: null },
];
const access: AccessStore = {
  getMemberByUserId: async (u) => members.find((m) => m.userId === u) ?? null,
  getLearner: async (id) => learners.find((l) => l.id === id) ?? null,
  getRelationships: async (m, l) => rels.filter((r) => r.memberId === m && r.learnerId === l),
};
const accessDeps = { store: access, allowRealLearners: false, now: NOW };
const STATES = {
  policy_version: "AP-0.3", set_id: "A-v1",
  rows: [{
    target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", title_vi: "Viết nguyên nhân – kết quả", domain_key: "writing", state: "DEVELOPING", state_kind: "AUTO",
    state_since: "2026-10-20T12:00:00+07:00", flag: null, proposal: { id: P1, to_state: "INDEPENDENT", route: 1, at: "2026-10-20T12:00:00+07:00" },
    conditions: [{ id: "r1_first_attempts", level: 3, route: 1, have: 3, need: 3, met: true, evidence: [] }], route: 1, evaluated_at: null, updated_at: null,
  }],
};
const INBOX = {
  open: 1, decidable: 1,
  items: [{
    id: P1, kind: "PROPOSED", learner_id: AN, alias: "An", is_test: true, target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", title_vi: "x", from_state: "DEVELOPING", to_state: "INDEPENDENT",
    current_state: "DEVELOPING", route: 1, conditions: [], evidence: [{ episode_id: "e1", title_vi: "Núi lửa", step_no: 11, at: "2026-10-03T12:00:00+07:00" }],
    created_at: "2026-10-20T12:00:00+07:00", can_decide: true,
  }],
};
const skillStore = (over: Partial<SkillStore> = {}) =>
  ({
    getDecisionLearnerId: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (id === DECISION || id === P1 ? AN : null)),
    states: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => STATES),
    inbox: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => INBOX),
    decide: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ id: "c1", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", replayed: false })),
    confirmAll: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ ids: ["c1", "c2"], replayed: false })),
    setManual: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ id: "m1", replayed: false })),
    ...over,
  }) satisfies SkillStore;
const engine = () =>
  ({
    loadInputs: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ learner_id: AN, now: "2026-10-23T12:00:00+07:00", policy: null, targets: [], episodes: [], auto: [], exposures: [], decisions: [] })),
    applyResults: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ auto: 0, proposed: 0, flagged: 0, policy_version: "AP-0.3" })),
    resetStates: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ targets: 12 })),
  }) satisfies EngineStore;
const deps = (user: string | null, store: SkillStore = skillStore(), eng: EngineStore = engine()): SkillDeps => ({
  user: user ? { id: `u-${user}` } : null, store, engine: eng, access: accessDeps, log: vi.fn(async () => {}),
});
const decide = { client_id: "decide-0001", action: "confirm" };
const reject = { client_id: "decide-0002", action: "reject", reason: "Bài thứ ba còn dựa vào câu mẫu" };

describe("request bodies", () => {
  it("decide: confirm needs nothing more, 'Chưa' needs a reason of 3+ characters; manual only to 1–2 with a reason; confirm-all 1..50 different ids", () => {
    expect(decideBodySchema.safeParse(decide).success).toBe(true);
    expect(decideBodySchema.safeParse(reject).success).toBe(true);
    expect(decideBodySchema.safeParse({ ...reject, reason: " x " }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(decideBodySchema.safeParse({ ...decide, action: "maybe" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(decideBodySchema.safeParse({ ...decide, score: 3 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(manualBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "manual-0001", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", state: "DEVELOPING", reason: "Cần câu mẫu" }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(manualBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "manual-0001", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", state: "INDEPENDENT", reason: "Cần câu mẫu" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(manualBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "manual-0001", target_key: "a-l3", state: "DEVELOPING", reason: "Cần câu mẫu" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(confirmAllBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "all-00001", ids: [P1, DECISION] }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(confirmAllBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "all-00001", ids: [P1, P1] }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(confirmAllBodySchema.safeParse({ client_id: "all-00001", ids: [] }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("skill handlers: requireAccess first, then the body, then the store; the SQL checks the actor again", () => {
  it("GET states / inbox: tutor and parent read; the student is 403; outsiders 404; the inbox needs staff", async () => {
    expect((await handleSkillStates(deps("tutorAn"), AN)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await handleSkillStates(deps("parent"), AN)).body).toEqual({ skills: parseSkillStates(STATES) });
    expect((await handleSkillStates(deps("studentAn"), AN)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await handleSkillStates(deps("tutorNguyen"), AN)).status).toBe(404);
    const s = skillStore();
    expect((await handleSkillInbox(deps("parent", s))).body).toEqual({ inbox: parseSkillInbox(INBOX) });
    expect(s.inbox).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ byMemberId: "m-parent", allowReal: false });
    expect((await handleSkillInbox(deps("studentAn"))).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await handleSkillInbox(deps(null))).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("decide: only the child's tutor (parent 403 before the store); unknown or foreign proposal 404; after a new answer the skill is recomputed", async () => {
    const s = skillStore();
    const e = engine();
    expect((await handleDecideSkill(deps("parent", s, e), P1, decide)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorNguyen", s, e), P1, decide)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorAn", s, e), "d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000ff", decide)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorAn", s, e), "not-a-uuid", decide)).status).toBe(404);
    expect(s.decide).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect((await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorAn", s, e), P1, { ...reject, reason: "" })).status).toBe(400);
    const ok = await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorAn", s, e), P1, reject);
    expect(ok).toEqual({ status: 201, body: { id: "c1", replayed: false } });
    expect(s.decide).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ decisionId: P1, action: "reject", reason: "Bài thứ ba còn dựa vào câu mẫu", byMemberId: "m-tutorAn", clientId: "decide-0002" });
    expect(e.loadInputs).toHaveBeenCalledWith(AN, ["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]);
  });

  it("database refusals become words the screen can show", async () => {
    const refuse = (code: string, tag: string) => skillStore({ decide: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ReviewDbError(code, `eng_decide_skill: x (${tag})`); }) });
    const cases: [string, string, number, string][] = [
      ["42501", "tutor_only", 403, "skill_tutor_only"],
      ["23505", "already_answered", 409, "already_answered"],
      ["23514", "stale_proposal", 409, "stale_proposal"],
      ["22023", "flag_keep_or_edit", 400, "flag_keep_or_edit"],
      ["23514", "reason_required", 400, "skill_reason_required"],
    ];
    for (const [code, tag, status, error] of cases) {
      const r = await handleDecideSkill(deps("tutorAn", refuse(code, tag)), P1, decide);
      expect([r.status, (r.body as { error: string }).error], tag).toEqual([status, error]);
      expect(failText({ ok: false, status, error })).not.toBe(tutorWorkErrors.generic);
    }
  });

  it("confirm-all and manual: tutor only; a replay does not recompute; manual refuses levels 3–4 in the body", async () => {
    const e = engine();
    expect((await handleConfirmAll(deps("parent", skillStore(), e), AN, { client_id: "all-00001", ids: [P1] })).status).toBe(403);
    const replay = skillStore({ confirmAll: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ ids: ["c1"], replayed: true })) });
    expect((await handleConfirmAll(deps("tutorAn", replay, e), AN, { client_id: "all-00001", ids: [P1] })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(e.loadInputs).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect((await handleConfirmAll(deps("tutorAn", skillStore(), e), AN, { client_id: "all-00001", ids: [P1] })).status).toBe(201);
    expect(e.loadInputs).toHaveBeenCalledWith(AN, null);
    expect((await handleManualSkill(deps("tutorAn"), AN, { client_id: "manual-0001", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", state: "INDEPENDENT", reason: "x y z" })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await handleManualSkill(deps("tutorAn"), AN, { client_id: "manual-0001", target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", state: "SUPPORTED", reason: "Cần câu mẫu" })).status).toBe(201);
  });

  it("rebuild: tutor and parent; it resets, recomputes every skill and reports what was written", async () => {
    const e = engine();
    const r = await handleRebuild(deps("parent", skillStore(), e), AN);
    expect(r).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { targets: 0, auto: 0, proposed: 0, flagged: 0 } });
    expect(e.resetStates).toHaveBeenCalledWith(AN, "m-parent");
    expect((await handleRebuild(deps("studentAn"), AN)).status).toBe(403);
  });
});

describe("the engine runs after every write that changes evidence (D16), never after a replay or a refusal", () => {
  const hook = () => vi.fn(async () => {});
  const reviewStore = (over: Partial<ReviewStore> = {}) =>
    ({
      getEpisodeLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN), getDecisionLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN), getAttemptLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN), getActivityLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN),
      recordReviews: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => [{ target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", decision_id: "v1", observation_id: "o1", replayed: false }]),
      modifyDecision: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ decision_id: "v2", replayed: false })),
      recordSupport: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ id: 1, replayed: false })),
      ...over,
    }) as unknown as ReviewStore;
  const E = "e0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";

  it("S5 decisions, corrections and observed help call the hook with the child and the skills; a replay does not", async () => {
    const h = hook();
    const d = { user: { id: "u-tutorAn" }, store: reviewStore(), access: accessDeps, afterWrite: h };
    await handleDecisions(d, E, { client_id: "review-0001", items: [{ target_id: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", score: 3, not_observed: false }] });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(AN, ["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]);
    await handleModify(d, E, { client_id: "modify-0001", score: 2, not_observed: false, reason: "Đọc lại thấy thiếu" });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(AN, null);
    await handleSupport(d, E, { client_id: "support-0001", target_ids: ["A-L3-CAUSE-W"], kind: "frame" });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(AN, ["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]);
    expect(h).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    const replayed = { ...d, store: reviewStore({ recordReviews: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => [{ target_key: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", decision_id: "v1", observation_id: "o1", replayed: true }]) }) };
    await handleDecisions(replayed, E, { client_id: "review-0001", items: [{ target_id: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", score: 3, not_observed: false }] });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  });

  it("a student's submission and the reviewer's 'Xong' call it too", async () => {
    const h = hook();
    const work = {
      getActivityLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN),
      submit: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ attempt_id: "t1", attempt_no: 1, submitted_at: "2026-10-23T08:00:00Z", hash: "e".repeat(64), replayed: false })),
    } as unknown as WorkStore;
    await handleSubmit({ user: { id: "u-studentAn" }, store: work, access: accessDeps, afterWrite: h }, { activity_id: E, client_submission_id: E, content: { text: "Volcanoes erupt because the gas is trapped." }, assistance: ["none"], provenance: "in_app" });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenCalledWith(AN, null);
    const rev = {
      getAttemptLearnerId: vi.fn(async () => AN),
      finishRevision: vi.fn(async (): Promise<unknown> => ({ closing_id: "c1", feedback_id: null, revision_activity_id: null, replayed: false })),
    } as unknown as RevisionStore;
    await handleFinishRevision({ user: { id: "u-tutorAn" }, store: rev, access: accessDeps, afterWrite: h }, E, { client_id: "finish-0001", action: "close", checks: [{ n: 1, verdict: "improved" }] });
    expect(h).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

The bodies used for `handleModify`, `handleSupport`, `handleSubmit` and `handleFinishRevision` must be valid for their existing schemas: `modifyBodySchema`, `supportBodySchema`, `attemptBodySchema` and `finishBodySchema` in `lib/review/schemas.ts`, `lib/work/schemas.ts` and `lib/revision/schemas.ts`. If a field name differs, copy a valid body from `tests/unit/reviewHandlers.test.ts`, `tests/unit/workHandlers.test.ts` and `tests/unit/revisionApi.test.ts`. The assertions stay as written.
