```ts
/** S7a staff views of skill states (camelCase, parsed by shape.ts). Students never get any of these. */
import type { Condition, Route, SkillState } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import type { DomainKey } from "@/lib/skillmap/types";

/** Same limits as migration 0023. */
export const REASON_MIN = 3;
export const REASON_MAX = 300;
export const CONFIRM_ALL_MAX = 50;
/** A person sets a level by hand only to 1 or 2 (spec §3 step 5); 3–4 only through a confirmed proposal. */
export const MANUAL_STATES = ["SUPPORTED", "DEVELOPING"] as const;

export type SkillStateRow = {
  targetKey: string;
  titleVi: string | null;
  domainKey: DomainKey | null;
  state: SkillState;
  /** The level came from the app (AUTO): "App tự cập nhật". */
  auto: boolean;
  stateSince: string | null;
  flag: { id: string; at: string } | null;
  proposal: { id: string; toState: SkillState; route: Route | null; at: string } | null;
  conditions: Condition[];
  route: Route | null;
  evaluatedAt: string | null;
};
export type SkillStates = { policyVersion: string | null; setId: string | null; rows: SkillStateRow[] };

export type SkillEvidence = { episodeId: string; titleVi: string | null; stepNo: number; at: string };
export type SkillInboxItem = {
  id: string;
  kind: "PROPOSED" | "FLAG";
  learnerId: string;
  alias: string;
  isTest: boolean;
  targetKey: string;
  titleVi: string | null;
  fromState: SkillState;
  toState: SkillState;
  currentState: SkillState;
  route: Route | null;
  conditions: Condition[];
  evidence: SkillEvidence[];
  createdAt: string;
  canDecide: boolean;
};
export type SkillInbox = { open: number; decidable: number; items: SkillInboxItem[] };
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `lib/skills/shape.ts`**

```ts
/** Parse eng_skill_states / eng_skill_inbox (migration 0023). zod strips unknown keys; anything unexpected throws (→ 500, logged). */
import { z } from "zod";
import { CONDITION_IDS, SKILL_STATES, type Condition, type Route } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { DOMAIN_KEYS } from "@/lib/skillmap/types";
import type { SkillInbox, SkillStates } from "./types";

const state = z.enum(SKILL_STATES);
const route = z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2)]).nullable();
const condition = z.object({
  id: z.enum(CONDITION_IDS), level: z.number().int().min(1).max(4), route: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2)]),
  have: z.number(), need: z.number(), met: z.boolean(), evidence: z.array(z.string()),
});
const toConditions = (cs: z.infer<typeof condition>[]): Condition[] => cs.map((c) => ({ ...c, level: c.level as Condition["level"] }));

const statesRow = z.object({
  policy_version: z.string().nullable(),
  set_id: z.string().nullable(),
  rows: z.array(z.object({
    target_key: z.string(), title_vi: z.string().nullable(), domain_key: z.enum(DOMAIN_KEYS).nullable(), state,
    state_kind: z.enum(["AUTO", "CONFIRMED", "MANUAL"]).nullable(), state_since: z.string().nullable(),
    flag: z.object({ id: z.string(), at: z.string() }).nullable(),
    proposal: z.object({ id: z.string(), to_state: state, route, at: z.string() }).nullable(),
    conditions: z.array(condition), route, evaluated_at: z.string().nullable(), updated_at: z.string().nullable(),
  })),
});

export function parseSkillStates(raw: unknown): SkillStates {
  const s = statesRow.parse(raw);
  return {
    policyVersion: s.policy_version,
    setId: s.set_id,
    rows: s.rows.map((r) => ({
      targetKey: r.target_key, titleVi: r.title_vi, domainKey: r.domain_key, state: r.state, auto: r.state_kind === "AUTO", stateSince: r.state_since,
      flag: r.flag, proposal: r.proposal ? { id: r.proposal.id, toState: r.proposal.to_state, route: r.proposal.route as Route | null, at: r.proposal.at } : null,
      conditions: toConditions(r.conditions), route: r.route as Route | null, evaluatedAt: r.evaluated_at,
    })),
  };
}

const inboxRow = z.object({
  open: z.number().int(),
  decidable: z.number().int(),
  items: z.array(z.object({
    id: z.string(), kind: z.enum(["PROPOSED", "FLAG"]), learner_id: z.string(), alias: z.string(), is_test: z.boolean(), target_key: z.string(),
    title_vi: z.string().nullable(), from_state: state, to_state: state, current_state: state, route, conditions: z.array(condition),
    evidence: z.array(z.object({ episode_id: z.string(), title_vi: z.string().nullable(), step_no: z.number().int(), at: z.string() })),
    created_at: z.string(), can_decide: z.boolean(),
  })),
});

export function parseSkillInbox(raw: unknown): SkillInbox {
  const b = inboxRow.parse(raw);
  return {
    open: b.open,
    decidable: b.decidable,
    items: b.items.map((i) => ({
      id: i.id, kind: i.kind, learnerId: i.learner_id, alias: i.alias, isTest: i.is_test, targetKey: i.target_key, titleVi: i.title_vi,
      fromState: i.from_state, toState: i.to_state, currentState: i.current_state, route: i.route as Route | null, conditions: toConditions(i.conditions),
      evidence: i.evidence.map((e) => ({ episodeId: e.episode_id, titleVi: e.title_vi, stepNo: e.step_no, at: e.at })),
      createdAt: i.created_at, canDecide: i.can_decide,
    })),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `lib/skills/schemas.ts`**

```ts
/** Request bodies of the S7a routes (zod). Parsed AFTER requireAccess, so outsiders always get 404 first. */
import { z } from "zod";
import { uuid } from "@/lib/practice/schemas";
import { CONFIRM_ALL_MAX, MANUAL_STATES, REASON_MAX, REASON_MIN } from "./types";

const clientId = z.string().min(8).max(100);
const reason = z.string().trim().max(REASON_MAX);

export const decideBodySchema = z
  .strictObject({ client_id: clientId, action: z.enum(["confirm", "reject"]), reason: reason.optional() })
  .refine((b) => b.action === "confirm" || (b.reason ?? "").length >= REASON_MIN, { path: ["reason"], message: "'Chưa' needs a reason" });
export type DecideBody = z.infer<typeof decideBodySchema>;

export const confirmAllBodySchema = z
  .strictObject({ client_id: clientId, ids: z.array(uuid).min(1).max(CONFIRM_ALL_MAX) })
  .refine((b) => new Set(b.ids).size === b.ids.length, { path: ["ids"], message: "each proposal once" });

export const manualBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  client_id: clientId,
  target_key: z.string().max(100).regex(/^[A-Z]-[A-Z0-9]+(?:-[A-Z0-9]+)*$/),
  state: z.enum(MANUAL_STATES),
  reason: reason.min(REASON_MIN),
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write `lib/skills/handlers.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Handlers of the S7a routes. Same order as lib/levels/handlers.ts: requireAccess / requireStaff first (401 / 404 outside scope / 403), then the
 * body (400), then the service-role store. The SQL checks the actor again (only the child's tutor decides; D15-style). After a new decision the
 * skill is recomputed, fail-soft (D16).
 */
import { AccessError, requireAccess, requireStaff, type AccessDeps, type AccessGrant, type AccessUser, type Action } from "@/lib/auth/requireAccess";
import { apiErrors } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { allowRealLearners } from "@/lib/env";
import { isMissingFunction, rebuildLearner, recomputeLearner, type EngineStore } from "@/lib/engine/runner";
import { fromDbError, ReviewDbError, type HandlerResult } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { confirmAllBodySchema, decideBodySchema, manualBodySchema } from "./schemas";
import { parseSkillInbox, parseSkillStates } from "./shape";

export interface SkillStore {
  /** Which learner a mastery decision belongs to (service role, read-only), or null. */
  getDecisionLearnerId(decisionId: string): Promise<string | null>;
  /** english.eng_skill_states / eng_skill_inbox (service role, after requireAccess / requireStaff). */
  states(learnerId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  inbox(a: { byMemberId: string; allowReal: boolean }): Promise<unknown>;
  /** english.eng_decide_skill / eng_confirm_skill_proposals / eng_set_skill_state (service role). */
  decide(a: { decisionId: string; action: "confirm" | "reject"; reason: string | null; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  confirmAll(a: { learnerId: string; ids: string[]; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  setManual(a: { learnerId: string; targetKey: string; state: "SUPPORTED" | "DEVELOPING"; reason: string; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
}

export type SkillDeps = {
  user: AccessUser;
  store: SkillStore;
  engine: EngineStore;
  access?: Partial<AccessDeps>;
  /** Where an engine error after a decision goes (app_errors); the decision itself already succeeded. */
  log?: (err: unknown) => Promise<void>;
};

type FailStatus = 400 | 401 | 403 | 404 | 409;
const fail = (status: FailStatus, error: string): HandlerResult => ({ status, body: { error, message: apiErrors[status] } });
const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

async function guard(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string, action: Action): Promise<{ ok: true; grant: AccessGrant } | { ok: false; result: HandlerResult }> {
  try {
    return { ok: true, grant: await requireAccess(deps.user, learnerId, action, deps.access) };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof AccessError) return { ok: false, result: fail(err.status, err.message) };
    throw err;
  }
}

/** The tags migration 0023 puts at the end of its messages, and what the screens are told. */
const DB_ANSWERS: { code: string; tag: string; status: FailStatus; error: string }[] = [
  { code: "42501", tag: "tutor_only", status: 403, error: "skill_tutor_only" },
  { code: "42501", tag: "staff_only", status: 403, error: "forbidden" },
  { code: "23505", tag: "already_answered", status: 409, error: "already_answered" },
  { code: "23505", tag: "client_reused", status: 409, error: "conflict" },
  { code: "23514", tag: "reason_required", status: 400, error: "skill_reason_required" },
  { code: "23514", tag: "same_state", status: 400, error: "same_state" },
  { code: "23514", tag: "stale_proposal", status: 409, error: "stale_proposal" },
  { code: "22023", tag: "flag_not_bulk", status: 400, error: "flag_not_bulk" },
  { code: "22023", tag: "flag_keep_or_edit", status: 400, error: "flag_keep_or_edit" },
  { code: "22023", tag: "not_a_proposal", status: 400, error: "not_a_proposal" },
  { code: "22023", tag: "manual_levels_1_2", status: 400, error: "manual_levels_1_2" },
  { code: "22023", tag: "not_in_set", status: 400, error: "not_in_set" },
];

function mapDbError(err: unknown): HandlerResult {
  if (err instanceof ReviewDbError) {
    const hit = DB_ANSWERS.find((a) => a.code === err.code && err.detail.includes(`(${a.tag})`));
    if (hit) return fail(hit.status, hit.error);
  }
  return fromDbError(err);
}

/** Recompute after a new decision (a confirmed level can open level 4; a closed ⚑ restarts the window). Never fails the decision. */
async function settle(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string, targets: string[] | null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await recomputeLearner(deps.engine, learnerId, targets);
  } catch (err) {
    if (!isMissingFunction(err)) await deps.log?.(err);
  }
}

export async function handleSkillStates(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "mastery:read");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  return { status: 200, body: { skills: parseSkillStates(await deps.store.states(g.grant.learner.id)) } };
}

export async function handleSkillInbox(deps: SkillDeps): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  let memberId: string;
  try {
    memberId = (await requireStaff(deps.user, deps.access)).id;
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof AccessError) return fail(err.status, err.message);
    throw err;
  }
  const allowReal = deps.access?.allowRealLearners ?? allowRealLearners();
  return { status: 200, body: { inbox: parseSkillInbox(await deps.store.inbox({ byMemberId: memberId, allowReal })) } };
}

export async function handleDecideSkill(deps: SkillDeps, decisionId: string, rawBody: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  if (!deps.user?.id) return fail(401, "unauthenticated");
  if (!UUID_RE.test(decisionId)) return fail(404, "not_found");
  const learnerId = await deps.store.getDecisionLearnerId(decisionId);
  if (!learnerId) return fail(404, "not_found");
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "mastery:decide");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const parsed = decideBodySchema.safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) return fail(400, parsed.error.issues.some((i) => i.path[0] === "reason") ? "skill_reason_required" : "invalid_body");
  const b = parsed.data;
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.decide({
      decisionId, action: b.action, reason: b.reason && b.reason.length > 0 ? b.reason : null, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: b.client_id,
    })) as { id: string; target_key: string; replayed: boolean };
    if (!r.replayed) await settle(deps, g.grant.learner.id, [r.target_key]);
    return { status: r.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: { id: r.id, replayed: r.replayed } };
  } catch (err) {
    return mapDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleConfirmAll(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string, rawBody: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "mastery:decide");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const parsed = confirmAllBodySchema.safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.confirmAll({ learnerId: g.grant.learner.id, ids: parsed.data.ids, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: parsed.data.client_id })) as {
      ids: string[]; replayed: boolean;
    };
    if (!r.replayed) await settle(deps, g.grant.learner.id, null);
    return { status: r.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: { ids: r.ids, replayed: r.replayed } };
  } catch (err) {
    return mapDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleManualSkill(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string, rawBody: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "mastery:decide");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const parsed = manualBodySchema.safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) return fail(400, parsed.error.issues.some((i) => i.path[0] === "reason") ? "skill_reason_required" : "invalid_body");
  const b = parsed.data;
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.setManual({
      learnerId: g.grant.learner.id, targetKey: b.target_key, state: b.state, reason: b.reason, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: b.client_id,
    })) as { id: string; replayed: boolean };
    if (!r.replayed) await settle(deps, g.grant.learner.id, [b.target_key]);
    return { status: r.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: { id: r.id, replayed: r.replayed } };
  } catch (err) {
    return mapDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleRebuild(deps: SkillDeps, learnerId: string): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "mastery:rebuild");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  try {
    const s = await rebuildLearner(deps.engine, g.grant.learner.id, g.grant.member.id);
    return { status: 200, body: { targets: s.results.length, auto: s.applied.auto, proposed: s.applied.proposed, flagged: s.applied.flagged } };
  } catch (err) {
    return mapDbError(err);
  }
}
```

```ts
// lib/skills/supabaseStore.ts
import "server-only";
import { ReviewDbError } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import type { SkillStore } from "./handlers";

/** The S7a staff functions of migration 0023 through the service role; only after the handler ran requireAccess / requireStaff. */
export function createSupabaseSkillStore(): SkillStore {
  const call = async (fn: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<unknown> => {
    const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc(fn, args);
    if (error) throw new ReviewDbError(error.code ?? "unknown", `${fn}: ${error.message ?? error.code ?? "error"}`);
    return data;
  };
  return {
    async getDecisionLearnerId(id) {
      const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().from("mastery_decisions").select("learner_id").eq("id", id).maybeSingle<{ learner_id: string }>();
      if (error) throw new ReviewDbError(error.code ?? "unknown", `mastery_decisions lookup: ${error.message ?? error.code ?? "error"}`);
      return data?.learner_id ?? null;
    },
    states: (learnerId) => call("eng_skill_states", { p_learner: learnerId }),
    inbox: (a) => call("eng_skill_inbox", { p_by: a.byMemberId, p_allow_real: a.allowReal }),
    decide: (a) => call("eng_decide_skill", { p_decision: a.decisionId, p_action: a.action, p_reason: a.reason, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
    confirmAll: (a) => call("eng_confirm_skill_proposals", { p_learner: a.learnerId, p_ids: a.ids, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
    setManual: (a) =>
      call("eng_set_skill_state", { p_learner: a.learnerId, p_target: a.targetKey, p_state: a.state, p_reason: a.reason, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
  };
}
```

```ts
// lib/skills/route.ts
import "server-only";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { apiErrors } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { createSupabaseEngineStore } from "@/lib/engine/supabaseStore";
import { logAppError } from "@/lib/ops/logAppError";
import type { HandlerResult } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import type { SkillDeps } from "./handlers";
import { createSupabaseSkillStore } from "./supabaseStore";

/** Session → handler → JSON (no-store); 500 logged. Same glue as lib/levels/route.ts, plus the engine store. */
export async function runSkillRoute(routeName: string, run: (deps: SkillDeps) => Promise<HandlerResult>): Promise<NextResponse> {
  let userId: string | null = null;
  try {
    const supabase = await createUserClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    userId = user?.id ?? null;
    const result = await run({
      user,
      store: createSupabaseSkillStore(),
      engine: createSupabaseEngineStore(),
      log: (error) => logAppError({ route: routeName, code: "engine_recompute", error, userId }),
    });
    return NextResponse.json(result.body, { status: result.status, headers: { "Cache-Control": "private, no-store" } });
  } catch (err) {
    await logAppError({ route: routeName, code: "internal", error: err, userId });
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "internal", message: apiErrors[500] }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

```ts
// lib/skills/load.ts
import "server-only";
import { isMissingFunction } from "@/lib/engine/runner";
import { allowRealLearners } from "@/lib/env";
import { parseSkillInbox, parseSkillStates } from "./shape";
import { createSupabaseSkillStore } from "./supabaseStore";
import type { SkillInbox, SkillStates } from "./types";

/** Skill states of one child (service role). Call only after requireLearnerPage / requireAccess. null = migration 0023 not applied yet. */
export async function loadSkillStates(learnerId: string): Promise<SkillStates | null> {
  try {
    return parseSkillStates(await createSupabaseSkillStore().states(learnerId));
  } catch (err) {
    if (isMissingFunction(err)) return null;
    throw err;
  }
}

/** Open proposals / ⚑ for this staff member. Call only after authorizeStaffPage. null = migration 0023 not applied yet. */
export async function loadSkillInbox(memberId: string): Promise<SkillInbox | null> {
  try {
    return parseSkillInbox(await createSupabaseSkillStore().inbox({ byMemberId: memberId, allowReal: allowRealLearners() }));
  } catch (err) {
    if (isMissingFunction(err)) return null;
    throw err;
  }
}
```

```ts
// app/api/learners/[id]/skills/route.ts
import { handleSkillStates } from "@/lib/skills/handlers";
import { runSkillRoute } from "@/lib/skills/route";

/** GET /api/learners/:id/skills - the level, ⚑ and open proposal of every skill of the child's set (tutor, parent). */
export async function GET(_req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {

}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { levelCopy, levelLabels, skillNames, skillStateCopy, targetsPage, tutorHonorific } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { Condition } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import type { SkillInboxItem, SkillStateRow } from "@/lib/skills/types";
import {
  conditionText, conditionsFor, domainSkills, groupInbox, isPractising, isSolid, nextNeed, proposalTitle, routeLabel, scaleCells, skillTabCount, skillTitle, solidByDomain,
} from "@/lib/skills/view";

const c = (id: Condition["id"], level: Condition["level"], route: 1 | 2, have: number, need: number): Condition => ({ id, level, route, have, need, met: have >= need, evidence: [] });
const row = (o: Partial<SkillStateRow> = {}): SkillStateRow => ({
  targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", titleVi: "Viết nguyên nhân", domainKey: "writing", state: "NOT_OBSERVED", auto: false, stateSince: null, flag: null,
  proposal: null, conditions: [], route: null, evaluatedAt: null, ...o,
});
const item = (o: Partial<SkillInboxItem> = {}): SkillInboxItem => ({
  id: "p1", kind: "PROPOSED", learnerId: "L1", alias: "An", isTest: true, targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", titleVi: "x", fromState: "DEVELOPING", toState: "INDEPENDENT",
  currentState: "DEVELOPING", route: 1, conditions: [], evidence: [], createdAt: "2026-10-20T12:00:00+07:00", canDecide: true, ...o,
});
const NO_DAT = /đạt/iu;
const STANDALONE_CO = /(^|[^/\p{L}])cô([^/\p{L}]|$)/iu;

describe("copy (UX_COPY_GUIDE §3.2 with tutorHonorific, D21)", () => {
  it("⚑ is 'Cần thầy/cô xem lại'; the five level names do not change; no level-page copy says 'đạt' or a standalone 'cô'", () => {
    expect(levelLabels.NEEDS_CONFIRMATION.label).toBe(`Cần ${tutorHonorific} xem lại`);
    expect(levelLabels.SUPPORTED.explain).toContain(tutorHonorific);
    expect(levelLabels.INDEPENDENT.explain).toContain(tutorHonorific);
    const texts = [...Object.values(skillStateCopy.conditions), levelCopy.solidCount(2, 5), levelCopy.pendingLine("Kỹ năng"), levelCopy.flagLine("Kỹ năng"), levelCopy.noSkillsInDomain];
    for (const t of texts) {
      expect(t).not.toMatch(NO_DAT);
      expect(t).not.toMatch(STANDALONE_CO);
    }
    expect(targetsPage.intro).toContain(tutorHonorific);
  });
});

describe("scale and lines", () => {
  it("4 labelled cells filled up to the level; none for 'Chưa đủ bài'", () => {
    expect(scaleCells("DEVELOPING").map((x) => [x.label, x.filled, x.current])).toEqual([
      [levelLabels.SUPPORTED.label, true, false],
      [levelLabels.DEVELOPING.label, true, true],
      [levelLabels.INDEPENDENT.label, false, false],
      [levelLabels.TRANSFERABLE.label, false, false],
    ]);
    expect(scaleCells("NOT_OBSERVED").some((x) => x.filled)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("titles use the staff name of UX §3.4; conditions show have/need (accuracy in %); routes and proposals read as sentences", () => {
    expect(skillTitle({ targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", titleVi: "x" })).toBe(skillNames["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]!.staff);
    expect(skillTitle({ targetKey: "B-T7-ARGUE", titleVi: "Lập luận" })).toBe("Lập luận");
    expect(conditionText(c("r1_contexts", 3, 1, 1, 2))).toBe(skillStateCopy.conditionLine(skillStateCopy.conditions.r1_contexts!, 1, 2));
    expect(conditionText(c("r2_accuracy", 3, 2, 69, 75))).toBe(skillStateCopy.percentLine(skillStateCopy.conditions.r2_accuracy!, 69, 75));
    expect([routeLabel(1), routeLabel(2), routeLabel(null)]).toEqual([skillStateCopy.routeLabels[1], skillStateCopy.routeLabels[2], null]);
    expect(proposalTitle(item())).toBe(skillStateCopy.proposalLine(skillNames["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]!.staff, levelLabels.DEVELOPING.label, levelLabels.INDEPENDENT.label));
    expect(proposalTitle(item({ kind: "FLAG", toState: "INDEPENDENT", currentState: "INDEPENDENT" }))).toBe(skillStateCopy.flagLine(skillNames["A-L3-CAUSE-W"]!.staff, levelLabels.INDEPENDENT.label));
  });

  it("a proposal shows the conditions of its level and route; a ⚑ shows none (its evidence is the low work)", () => {
    const conditions = [c("l2_unaided_first", 2, 1, 1, 1), c("r1_contexts", 3, 1, 2, 2), c("r2_items", 3, 2, 3, 8), c("l4_delayed", 4, 1, 0, 1)];
    expect(conditionsFor(item({ conditions })).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(["r1_contexts"]);
    expect(conditionsFor(item({ conditions, route: 2 })).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(["r2_items"]);
    expect(conditionsFor(item({ conditions, toState: "TRANSFERABLE" })).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(["l4_delayed"]);
    expect(conditionsFor(item({ conditions, kind: "FLAG" }))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("Hồ sơ cấp boxes (D20)", () => {
  it("Đã vững = 3–4 without ⚑; Đang luyện = 1–2 or any ⚑", () => {
    expect([isSolid(row({ state: "INDEPENDENT" })), isSolid(row({ state: "INDEPENDENT", flag: { id: "f", at: "x" } })), isSolid(row({ state: "DEVELOPING" }))]).toEqual([true, false, false]);
    expect([isPractising(row({ state: "SUPPORTED" })), isPractising(row({ state: "TRANSFERABLE", flag: { id: "f", at: "x" } })), isPractising(row())]).toEqual([true, true, false]);
  });

  it("'Còn cần' = the nearest unmet condition of the next level; at level 2 the route with more conditions met wins", () => {
    expect(nextNeed(row({ conditions: [c("l1_evidence", 1, 1, 0, 1)] }))?.id).toBe("l1_evidence");
    const level3 = [c("r1_first_attempts", 3, 1, 2, 3), c("r1_contexts", 3, 1, 1, 2), c("r1_span_days", 3, 1, 3, 7), c("r1_direct_checkpoint", 3, 1, 1, 1),
      c("r2_items", 3, 2, 2, 8), c("r2_activities", 3, 2, 1, 2), c("r2_accuracy", 3, 2, 50, 75), c("r2_review", 3, 2, 0, 1)];
    expect(nextNeed(row({ state: "DEVELOPING", conditions: level3 }))?.id).toBe("r1_first_attempts"); // route 1: 1 met vs 0; 2/3 is nearest
    expect(nextNeed(row({ state: "TRANSFERABLE" }))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("domainSkills: n/N over the set's skills of that domain; a pending proposal is shown instead of a need", () => {
    const rows = [
      row({ targetKey: "A-L3-CAUSE-W", state: "INDEPENDENT" }),
      row({ targetKey: "A-L3-PARA-W", state: "DEVELOPING", proposal: { id: "p", toState: "INDEPENDENT", route: 1, at: "x" } }),
      row({ targetKey: "A-L1-PAST-W", domainKey: "grammar", state: "SUPPORTED", conditions: [c("l2_unaided_first", 2, 1, 0, 1)] }),
    ];
    const w = domainSkills(rows, "writing");
    expect([w.n, w.total, w.solid.length, w.practising.length]).toEqual([1, 2, 1, 1]);
    expect(w.next).toEqual([{ row: rows[1], need: null, pending: true }]);
    expect(domainSkills(rows, "grammar").next[0]?.need?.id).toBe("l2_unaided_first");
    expect(domainSkills(rows, "listening")).toMatchObject({ n: 0, total: 0 });
    expect(solidByDomain(rows)).toEqual({ writing: { n: 1, total: 2 }, grammar: { n: 0, total: 1 } });
  });
});

describe("the tab", () => {
  it("groups items per child in order; only proposals the person can decide go to 'Xác nhận tất cả'; the count is decidable (tutor) or open (parent)", () => {
    const items = [item({ id: "a" }), item({ id: "b", kind: "FLAG" }), item({ id: "c", learnerId: "L2", alias: "Nguyên", canDecide: false })];
    const g = groupInbox(items);
    expect(g.map((x) => [x.learnerId, x.items.map((i) => i.id), x.proposalIds, x.canDecide])).toEqual([["L1", ["a", "b"], ["a"], true], ["L2", ["c"], [], false]]);
    const inbox = { open: 3, decidable: 2, items };
    expect([skillTabCount(inbox, "tutor"), skillTabCount(inbox, "parent"), skillTabCount(null, "tutor")]).toEqual([2, 3, null]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `lib/skills/view.ts`**

```ts
/** Pure helpers of the S7a screens (no I/O). Copy comes from lib/copy/vi.ts only. */
import type { Role } from "@/lib/auth/requireAccess";
import { levelCopy, levelLabels, skillNames, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { STATE_LEVEL, type Condition, type Route, type SkillState } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import type { DomainKey } from "@/lib/skillmap/types";
import type { SkillInbox, SkillInboxItem, SkillStateRow } from "./types";

/** The 4 labelled cells of UX §3.2 ("thang 4 ô có chữ, không dùng màu đơn thuần"). */
export const SCALE_STATES = ["SUPPORTED", "DEVELOPING", "INDEPENDENT", "TRANSFERABLE"] as const;
export function scaleCells(state: SkillState): { state: (typeof SCALE_STATES)[number]; label: string; filled: boolean; current: boolean }[] {
  const level = STATE_LEVEL[state];
  return SCALE_STATES.map((s, i) => ({ state: s, label: levelLabels[s].label, filled: level >= i + 1, current: level === i + 1 }));
}
export const stateLabel = (s: SkillState): string => levelLabels[s].label;

/** Staff name of UX §3.4 when it has one, else the catalogue's Vietnamese title, else the code. */
export function skillTitle(r: { targetKey: string; titleVi: string | null }): string {
  return skillNames[r.targetKey]?.staff ?? r.titleVi ?? r.targetKey;
}

export function conditionText(c: Condition): string {
  const label = skillStateCopy.conditions[c.id] ?? c.id;
  return c.id === "r2_accuracy" ? skillStateCopy.percentLine(label, c.have, c.need) : skillStateCopy.conditionLine(label, c.have, c.need);
}

export const routeLabel = (route: Route | null): string | null => (route === null ? null : (skillStateCopy.routeLabels[route] ?? null));

export function proposalTitle(i: SkillInboxItem): string {
  const skill = skillTitle(i);
  return i.kind === "FLAG" ? skillStateCopy.flagLine(skill, stateLabel(i.currentState)) : skillStateCopy.proposalLine(skill, stateLabel(i.fromState), stateLabel(i.toState));
}

/** The conditions shown under a proposal: those of its level (and, at level 3, of its route). A ⚑ shows its evidence only. */
export function conditionsFor(i: SkillInboxItem): Condition[] {
  if (i.kind === "FLAG") return [];
  const level = STATE_LEVEL[i.toState];
  return i.conditions.filter((c) => c.level === level && (level !== 3 || c.route === (i.route ?? 1)));
}

export const isSolid = (r: SkillStateRow): boolean => STATE_LEVEL[r.state] >= 3 && r.flag === null;
export const isPractising = (r: SkillStateRow): boolean => (STATE_LEVEL[r.state] >= 1 && STATE_LEVEL[r.state] <= 2) || r.flag !== null;

const ratio = (c: Condition) => (c.need > 0 ? c.have / c.need : 1);
/** D20 "Còn cần": the nearest unmet condition of the next level; for level 3, inside the route with more conditions met (ties: route 1). */
export function nextNeed(r: SkillStateRow): Condition | null {
  const next = STATE_LEVEL[r.state] + 1;
  if (next > 4) return null;
  let pool = r.conditions.filter((c) => c.level === next);
  if (next === 3) {
    const routes = ([1, 2] as const).map((rt) => pool.filter((c) => c.route === rt)).filter((cs) => cs.length > 0);
    const metCount = (cs: Condition[]) => cs.filter((c) => c.met).length;
    pool = routes.reduce((best, cs) => (metCount(cs) > metCount(best) ? cs : best), routes[0] ?? []);
  }
  const unmet = pool.filter((c) => !c.met);
  if (unmet.length === 0) return null;
  return unmet.reduce((best, c) => (ratio(c) > ratio(best) ? c : best), unmet[0]!);
}

export type DomainSkills = {
  solid: SkillStateRow[];
  practising: SkillStateRow[];
  next: { row: SkillStateRow; need: Condition | null; pending: boolean }[];
  n: number;
  total: number;
};
export function domainSkills(rows: readonly SkillStateRow[], domain: DomainKey): DomainSkills {
  const mine = rows.filter((r) => r.domainKey === domain);
  const solid = mine.filter(isSolid);
  return {
    solid,
    practising: mine.filter(isPractising),
    next: mine.filter((r) => STATE_LEVEL[r.state] < 3).map((r) => ({ row: r, need: r.proposal ? null : nextNeed(r), pending: r.proposal !== null })),
    n: solid.length,
    total: mine.length,
  };
}
export function solidByDomain(rows: readonly SkillStateRow[]): Partial<Record<DomainKey, { n: number; total: number }>> {
  const out: Partial<Record<DomainKey, { n: number; total: number }>> = {};
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.domainKey === null) continue;
    const cur = out[r.domainKey] ?? { n: 0, total: 0 };
    out[r.domainKey] = { n: cur.n + (isSolid(r) ? 1 : 0), total: cur.total + 1 };
  }
  return out;
}

/** "n/N điểm đã vững" for one domain, or null when the set has no skill there. */
export const solidLine = (s: { n: number; total: number } | undefined): string | null => (s && s.total > 0 ? levelCopy.solidCount(s.n, s.total) : null);

export type InboxGroup = { learnerId: string; alias: string; isTest: boolean; canDecide: boolean; items: SkillInboxItem[]; proposalIds: string[] };
export function groupInbox(items: readonly SkillInboxItem[]): InboxGroup[] {
  const groups: InboxGroup[] = [];
  for (const i of items) {
    let g = groups.find((x) => x.learnerId === i.learnerId);
    if (!g) {
      g = { learnerId: i.learnerId, alias: i.alias, isTest: i.isTest, canDecide: false, items: [], proposalIds: [] };
      groups.push(g);
    }
    g.items.push(i);
    g.canDecide = g.canDecide || i.canDecide;
    if (i.kind === "PROPOSED" && i.canDecide) g.proposalIds.push(i.id);
  }
  return groups;
}

/** The number on the "Kỹ năng" tab: what a tutor can decide; what is open for a parent; null before migration 0023. */
export function skillTabCount(inbox: SkillInbox | null, role: Role): number | null {
  if (inbox === null) return null;
  return role === "tutor" ? inbox.decidable : inbox.open;
}
```

```tsx
// components/review/ReviewTabs.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";

/** Hộp duyệt has two parts (D19): "Bài làm" (/review, S5) and "Kỹ năng" (/review/skills, S7a). The count is null before migration 0023. */
export function ReviewTabs({ active, skillsCount }: { active: "work" | "skills"; skillsCount: number | null }) {
  const tab = (key: "work" | "skills", href: string, label: string) => (
    <Link
      href={href}
      aria-current={active === key ? "page" : undefined}
      data-testid={`review-tab-${key}`}
      className={`inline-flex min-h-11 items-center border-b-2 px-3 text-sm ${active === key ? "border-accent font-semibold text-ink" : "border-transparent text-muted hover:text-ink"}`}
    >
      {label}
    </Link>
  );
  return (
    <nav aria-label={skillStateCopy.tabsLabel} className="mt-4 flex gap-2 border-b border-line">
      {tab("work", "/review", skillStateCopy.tabWork)}
      {tab("skills", "/review/skills", skillsCount === null ? skillStateCopy.tabSkills : `${skillStateCopy.tabSkills} ${skillStateCopy.tabCount(skillsCount)}`)}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/ConditionList.tsx
import { skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { Condition } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { conditionText } from "@/lib/skills/view";

/** Conditions of one level, ✓ / ✗ plus the word (never colour alone, UX §4.3). */
export function ConditionList({ conditions }: { conditions: Condition[] }) {
  if (conditions.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <ul className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm" data-testid="condition-list">
      {conditions.map((c) => (
        <li key={c.id} data-testid={`condition-${c.id}`} data-met={c.met ? "true" : "false"}>
          <span aria-hidden="true" className={c.met ? "font-semibold text-accent" : "font-semibold text-[#7a2a0c]"}>
            {c.met ? skillStateCopy.met : skillStateCopy.notMet}
          </span>{" "}
          <span className="sr-only">{c.met ? skillStateCopy.metLabel : skillStateCopy.notMetLabel}: </span>
          {conditionText(c)}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/SkillProposalCard.tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { fallbackLabels, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { clientIdAfter, describedBy } from "@/lib/levels/form";
import { dayLabel } from "@/lib/levels/view";
import { REASON_MAX, REASON_MIN, type SkillInboxItem } from "@/lib/skills/types";
import { conditionsFor, proposalTitle, routeLabel } from "@/lib/skills/view";
import { failText, postJson } from "@/lib/tutorwork/client";
import { ConditionList } from "./ConditionList";

const button = "min-h-11 rounded-lg px-4 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-60";

/**
 * One open proposal or ⚑ of the "Kỹ năng" tab (spec §5). The child's tutor: Xác nhận / Chưa (reason) for a level-up; Giữ bậc / Sửa bậc for a ⚑.
 * Everyone else (the parent) reads it with "Chờ thầy/cô xác nhận". The server and the database check the actor again.
 */
export function SkillProposalCard({ item }: { item: SkillInboxItem }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [rejecting, setRejecting] = useState(false);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [clientId, setClientId] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());
  const flag = item.kind === "FLAG";
  const msgId = `skill-msg-${item.id}`;
  const meta = [routeLabel(item.route), skillStateCopy.createdOn(dayLabel(item.createdAt))].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");

  async function send(action: "confirm" | "reject") {
    setMsg(null);
    if (action === "reject" && reason.trim().length < REASON_MIN) return setMsg(skillStateCopy.reasonRequired);
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await postJson(`/api/skills/decisions/${item.id}`, { client_id: clientId, action, ...(action === "reject" ? { reason: reason.trim() } : {}) });
    setBusy(false);
    setClientId(clientIdAfter(r, clientId));
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(failText(r));
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <li className="rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4" data-testid={`skill-proposal-${item.targetKey}`} data-kind={item.kind}>
      <p className="font-semibold" data-testid="skill-proposal-title">
        {proposalTitle(item)}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{meta}</p>
      <details className="mt-2" data-testid="skill-conditions">
        <summary className="min-h-11 cursor-pointer py-2 text-sm text-accent underline">{skillStateCopy.conditionsToggle}</summary>
        <ConditionList conditions={conditionsFor(item)} />
        <h3 className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold">{skillStateCopy.evidenceTitle}</h3>
        {item.evidence.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">{skillStateCopy.evidenceNone}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
            {item.evidence.map((e) => (
              <li key={e.episodeId}>
                <Link href={`/review/${e.episodeId}`} data-testid="skill-evidence-link" className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-accent underline">
                  {skillStateCopy.evidenceLine(e.titleVi ?? fallbackLabels.activity, e.stepNo, dayLabel(e.at))}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </details>

      {!item.canDecide ? (
        <p className="mt-3 inline-block rounded-full border border-line px-3 py-1 text-xs" data-testid="skill-waiting">
          {skillStateCopy.waitingTutor}
        </p>
      ) : flag ? (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => send("confirm")} data-testid="skill-keep" aria-describedby={describedBy(msg && msgId)} className={`${button} bg-accent text-white`}>
            {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.keep}
          </button>
          <Link href={`/learners/${item.learnerId}/targets#skill-${item.targetKey}`} data-testid="skill-edit-level" className={`${button} inline-flex items-center border border-accent text-accent`}>
            {skillStateCopy.editLevel}
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : rejecting ? (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
            <span className="text-muted">{skillStateCopy.rejectReason}</span>
            <textarea
              data-testid="skill-reject-reason"
              value={reason}
              maxLength={REASON_MAX}
              rows={2}
              placeholder={skillStateCopy.rejectHint}
              aria-describedby={describedBy(msg && msgId)}
              onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-3 py-2"
            />
          </label>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => send("reject")} data-testid="skill-reject-send" className={`${button} bg-accent text-white`}>
              {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.rejectSend}
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setRejecting(false)} className={`${button} border border-line`}>
              {skillStateCopy.cancel}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => send("confirm")} data-testid="skill-confirm" aria-describedby={describedBy(msg && msgId)} className={`${button} bg-accent text-white`}>
            {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.confirm}
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setRejecting(true)} data-testid="skill-reject-open" className={`${button} border border-accent text-accent`}>
            {skillStateCopy.reject}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {msg ? (
        <p id={msgId} role="alert" data-testid="skill-msg" className="mt-2 font-medium text-[#7a2a0c]">
          {msg}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/ConfirmAllButton.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { clientIdAfter } from "@/lib/levels/form";
import { failText, postJson } from "@/lib/tutorwork/client";

/** "Xác nhận tất cả" for one child (§3 step 4): exactly the level-up proposals on screen, never a ⚑ (the database refuses one too). */
export function ConfirmAllButton({ learnerId, ids }: { learnerId: string; ids: string[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [clientId, setClientId] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());

  async function confirmAll() {
    setMsg(null);
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await postJson(`/api/learners/${learnerId}/skills/confirm-all`, { client_id: clientId, ids });
    setBusy(false);
    setClientId(clientIdAfter(r, clientId));
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(failText(r));
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
      <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={confirmAll} data-testid={`skill-confirm-all-${learnerId}`} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-accent px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60">
        {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.confirmAll(ids.length)}
      </button>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">{skillStateCopy.confirmAllHint}</p>
      {msg ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-[#7a2a0c]">
          {msg}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the page** — `app/review/skills/page.tsx`

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { ReviewTabs } from "@/components/review/ReviewTabs";
import { ConfirmAllButton } from "@/components/skills/ConfirmAllButton";
import { SkillProposalCard } from "@/components/skills/SkillProposalCard";
import { reviewPage, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { authorizeStaffPage } from "@/lib/review/access";
import { loadSkillInbox } from "@/lib/skills/load";
import { groupInbox, skillTabCount } from "@/lib/skills/view";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Hộp duyệt → tab "Kỹ năng" (spec §5): open proposals and ⚑ per child. The child's tutor decides; the parent reads. Students: 404. */
export default async function ReviewSkillsPage() {
  const supabase = await createUserClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const access = await authorizeStaffPage(user);
  if (access.kind === "login") redirect("/login");
  if (access.kind === "not-found") notFound();

  const inbox = await loadSkillInbox(access.member.id);
  const groups = inbox ? groupInbox(inbox.items) : [];

  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader name={access.member.displayName} showTestBadge={groups.some((g) => g.isTest)} />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-10" data-testid="skills-inbox">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm text-accent underline">
          {reviewPage.back}
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{skillStateCopy.title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{skillStateCopy.intro}</p>
        {access.member.role === "parent" ? <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{skillStateCopy.parentNote}</p> : null}
        <ReviewTabs active="skills" skillsCount={skillTabCount(inbox, access.member.role)} />

        {inbox === null ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4" data-testid="skills-not-ready">
            {skillStateCopy.notReady}
          </p>
        ) : groups.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4" data-testid="skills-empty">
            {skillStateCopy.empty}
          </p>
        ) : (
          groups.map((g) => (
            <section key={g.learnerId} className="mt-6" data-testid={`skills-group-${g.learnerId}`} aria-labelledby={`skills-group-title-${g.learnerId}`}>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <h2 id={`skills-group-title-${g.learnerId}`} className="text-lg font-semibold">
                  {skillStateCopy.groupTitle(g.alias, g.items.length)}
                </h2>
                {g.proposalIds.length > 1 ? <ConfirmAllButton learnerId={g.learnerId} ids={g.proposalIds} /> : null}
              </div>
              <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
                {g.items.map((i) => (
                  <SkillProposalCard key={i.id} item={i} />
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          ))
        )}
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/SkillScale.tsx
import { levelLabels, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { STATE_LEVEL, type SkillState } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { scaleCells, stateLabel } from "@/lib/skills/view";

/** UX §3.2: the 4-cell scale with words (never colour alone); "Chưa đủ bài" fills no cell and says so. */
export function SkillScale({ state }: { state: SkillState }) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-2" data-testid="skill-scale" data-level={STATE_LEVEL[state]}>
      <ol className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1" aria-label={skillStateCopy.scaleLabel(stateLabel(state))}>
        {scaleCells(state).map((cell) => (
          <li
            key={cell.state}
            aria-current={cell.current ? "step" : undefined}
            className={`flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-md border px-1 text-center text-[11px] leading-tight ${
              cell.filled ? "border-accent bg-[#E3ECE8] font-semibold text-ink" : "border-dashed border-line text-muted"
            }`}
          >
            {cell.label}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      {STATE_LEVEL[state] === 0 ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">{levelLabels.NOT_OBSERVED.label}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/SkillRow.tsx
import { skillStateCopy, targetsPage } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { dayLabel } from "@/lib/levels/view";
import type { SkillStateRow } from "@/lib/skills/types";
import { stateLabel } from "@/lib/skills/view";
import { ManualForm } from "./ManualForm";
import { SkillScale } from "./SkillScale";

/** One tracked skill: name, group, the 4-cell scale, ⚑, the waiting proposal, "App tự cập nhật", the day; the child's tutor can set 1–2 by hand. */
export function SkillRow({ id, title, dimensionName, state, learnerId, canEdit, showCode }: {
  id: string; title: string; dimensionName: string; state: SkillStateRow | null; learnerId: string; canEdit: boolean; showCode: boolean;
}) {
  const current = state?.state ?? "NOT_OBSERVED";
  return (
    <li id={`skill-${id}`} data-testid={`skill-row-${id}`} className="scroll-mt-6 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4">
      <p className="font-semibold">{title}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">
        {targetsPage.dimension}: {dimensionName}
      </p>
      <SkillScale state={current} />
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        {state?.flag ? (
          <span data-testid="skill-flag" className="rounded-full border border-[#B4690E] bg-[#FBF1DC] px-2 py-0.5 text-[#6B4600]">
            {skillStateCopy.flagChip}
          </span>
        ) : null}
        {state?.proposal ? (
          <span data-testid="skill-pending" className="rounded-full border border-accent px-2 py-0.5">
            {skillStateCopy.pending(stateLabel(state.proposal.toState))}
          </span>
        ) : null}
        {state?.auto ? (
          <span data-testid="skill-auto" className="text-muted">
            {skillStateCopy.autoNote}
          </span>
        ) : null}
        {state?.stateSince ? <span className="text-muted">{skillStateCopy.updatedOn(dayLabel(state.stateSince))}</span> : null}
      </div>
      {canEdit ? <ManualForm learnerId={learnerId} targetKey={id} current={current} /> : null}
      {showCode ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted">
          {targetsPage.codeLabel}: {id}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/ManualForm.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { levelLabels, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { SkillState } from "@/lib/engine/types";
import { clientIdAfter, describedBy } from "@/lib/levels/form";
import { MANUAL_STATES, REASON_MAX, REASON_MIN } from "@/lib/skills/types";
import { failText, postJson } from "@/lib/tutorwork/client";

/** "Sửa tay bậc 1–2" (§3 step 5, G3): the child's tutor only (the server and the database check it), always with a reason. */
export function ManualForm({ learnerId, targetKey, current }: { learnerId: string; targetKey: string; current: SkillState }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const choices = MANUAL_STATES.filter((s) => s !== current);
  const [state, setState] = useState<(typeof MANUAL_STATES)[number]>(choices[0] ?? "SUPPORTED");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [clientId, setClientId] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());
  const msgId = `skill-manual-msg-${targetKey}`;

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMsg(null);
    if (reason.trim().length < REASON_MIN) return setMsg(skillStateCopy.reasonRequired);
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await postJson(`/api/learners/${learnerId}/skills/manual`, { client_id: clientId, target_key: targetKey, state, reason: reason.trim() });
    setBusy(false);
    setClientId(clientIdAfter(r, clientId));
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(failText(r));
    setReason("");
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <details className="mt-3 text-sm" data-testid={`skill-manual-${targetKey}`}>
      <summary className="min-h-11 cursor-pointer py-2 text-accent underline">{skillStateCopy.manualOpen}</summary>
      <form onSubmit={save} className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <span className="text-muted">{skillStateCopy.manualState}</span>
          <select value={state} onChange={(e) => setState(e.target.value as (typeof MANUAL_STATES)[number])} data-testid="skill-manual-state" className="min-h-11 max-w-60 rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-2">
            {choices.map((s) => (
              <option key={s} value={s}>
                {levelLabels[s].label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <span className="text-muted">{skillStateCopy.manualReason}</span>
          <textarea
            required
            data-testid="skill-manual-reason"
            value={reason}
            maxLength={REASON_MAX}
            rows={2}
            placeholder={skillStateCopy.manualHint}
            aria-describedby={describedBy(msg && msgId)}
            onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
            className="rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-3 py-2"
          />
        </label>
        {msg ? (
          <p id={msgId} role="alert" className="font-medium text-[#7a2a0c]">
            {msg}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy} data-testid="skill-manual-save" className="min-h-11 self-start rounded-lg bg-accent px-4 font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60">
          {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.manualSave}
        </button>
      </form>
    </details>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/skills/RebuildButton.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { failText, postJson } from "@/lib/tutorwork/client";

/** "Tính lại từ đầu" (§3 step 6): tutor or parent. Idempotent on the server, so no client id. */
export function RebuildButton({ learnerId }: { learnerId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function rebuild() {
    setMsg(null);
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await postJson<{ proposed: number }>(`/api/learners/${learnerId}/skills/rebuild`, {});
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(failText(r));
    setMsg(skillStateCopy.rebuildDone(r.body.proposed));
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-1">
      <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={rebuild} data-testid="skills-rebuild" className="min-h-11 self-start rounded-lg border border-accent px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-accent disabled:opacity-60">
        {busy ? skillStateCopy.saving : skillStateCopy.rebuild}
      </button>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">{skillStateCopy.rebuildHint}</p>
      {msg ? (
        <p role="status" data-testid="skills-rebuild-msg" className="text-sm">
          {msg}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The e2e seed.** In `scripts/lib/skillEvidence.ts`, add `import { devDueOn, seedThrowawayLearner, TEST_LEARNER_AN_ID } from "./seed";` below the existing imports, then append:

```ts
/** The two writing skills of A-v1 that the e2e suite follows (both are on the checkpoint writing steps of A-W1 11, A-W2 12, A-W3 13). */
export const E2E_SKILLS = ["A-L3-CAUSE-W", "A-L3-PARA-W"] as const;

export type SkillEvidenceLearner = {
  learnerId: string;
  alias: string;
  tutorMemberId: string;
  /** activity_instances ids: the three checkpoints and one more INDEPENDENT_CHECK step (A-W2 11) for "new evidence after Chưa". */
  steps: { w1: string; w2: string; w3: string; extra: string };
  /** The three validated episodes, oldest first. */
  episodes: string[];
};

/**
 * S7a e2e (D23): a throwaway TEST learner (seedThrowawayLearner) that An (thử)'s tutor also teaches, with A-W1 / A-W2 / A-W3 assigned and
 * three validated checkpoints 20 / 13 / 5 days ago scored 3 / 4 / 3 on both E2E_SKILLS - enough for "Đang tiến bộ" + a proposal
 * "Tự làm được" (route 1) once the engine runs. The caller purges it (english.eng_purge_learner). Owner connection only (CI).
 */
export async function seedSkillEvidenceLearner(client: ClientBase): Promise<SkillEvidenceLearner> {
  const t = await seedThrowawayLearner(client);
  const tutor = (
    await client.query<{ id: string }>("select member_id as id from english.relationships where learner_id = $1 and relation = 'tutor' order by member_id limit 1", [TEST_LEARNER_AN_ID])
  ).rows[0]?.id;
  if (!tutor) throw new Error("An (thử) has no tutor - run the dev seed first");
  const parent = (
    await client.query<{ id: string }>("select member_id as id from english.relationships where learner_id = $1 and relation = 'parent' limit 1", [t.learnerId])
  ).rows[0]!.id;
  await client.query("insert into english.relationships(member_id, learner_id, relation) values ($1, $2, 'tutor')", [tutor, t.learnerId]);

  const inst: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const tpl of ["A-W1-VOLCANOES", "A-W2-WATERCYCLE", "A-W3-EARTHQUAKES"]) {
    const row = (
      await client.query<{ date_from: string; date_to: string }>(
        "select date_from::text as date_from, date_to::text as date_to from english.inquiry_templates where id = $1 and version = 1 and published_at is not null",
        [tpl],
      )
    ).rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new Error(`${tpl} is not published (apply 0008)`);
    inst[tpl] = (
      await client.query<{ id: string }>("select english.eng_assign_inquiry($1, $2, 1, $3, $4, $5) as id", [
        t.learnerId, tpl, devDueOn(row.date_from, row.date_to), parent, `s7a-e2e:${t.learnerId}:${tpl}`,
      ])
    ).rows[0]!.id;
  }
  const step = async (tpl: string, n: number) =>
    (await client.query<{ id: string }>("select id from english.activity_instances where inquiry_instance_id = $1 and step_no = $2", [inst[tpl], n])).rows[0]!.id;
  const steps = {
    w1: await step("A-W1-VOLCANOES", 11),
    w2: await step("A-W2-WATERCYCLE", 12),
    w3: await step("A-W3-EARTHQUAKES", 13),
    extra: await step("A-W2-WATERCYCLE", 11),
  };
  const episodes: string[] = [];
  for (const [activityId, daysAgo, score] of [[steps.w1, 20, 3], [steps.w2, 13, 4], [steps.w3, 5, 3]] as const) {
    const e = await insertValidatedEpisode(client, t.learnerId, tutor, {
      activityId, daysAgo, scores: Object.fromEntries(E2E_SKILLS.map((k) => [k, score])), facets: ["checkpoint"],
    });
    episodes.push(e.episodeId);
  }
  return { learnerId: t.learnerId, alias: t.alias, tutorMemberId: tutor, steps, episodes };
}
```

  Then run `npm run typecheck` → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 2: The S7a e2e suite.** In `tests/e2e/purge.spec.ts`:
  - Replace `import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";` with `import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";`.
  - Replace `import { purgePage } from "@/lib/copy/vi";` with `import { levelLabels, purgePage, skillStateCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";`.

  - Append at the end of the file:

```ts
// S7a (D23): skill states on a throwaway TEST learner with three backdated validated checkpoints (level 3 needs ≥ 7 days of work,
// which one run cannot produce live). One learner for the whole serial suite; purged at the end.
test.describe.serial("S7a skill states", () => {
  let t: SkillEvidenceLearner;
  const [CAUSE, PARA] = E2E_SKILLS;
  const targets = () => `/learners/${t.learnerId}/targets`;
  const row = (page: Page, k: string) => page.getByTestId(`skill-row-${k}`);
  const card = (page: Page, k: string) => page.getByTestId(`skills-group-${t.learnerId}`).getByTestId(`skill-proposal-${k}`);
  const openProposals = () =>
    withDb(async (db) =>
      (
        await db.query<{ id: string }>(
          `select d.id from english.mastery_decisions d where d.learner_id = $1 and d.kind = 'PROPOSED'
             and not exists (select 1 from english.mastery_decisions a where a.responds_to = d.id) order by d.target_key`,
          [t.learnerId],
        )
      ).rows.map((r) => r.id),
    );
  async function rebuild(page: Page, proposed: number) {
    await page.getByTestId("skills-rebuild").click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("skills-rebuild-msg")).toHaveText(skillStateCopy.rebuildDone(proposed));
  }

  test.beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await withDb((db) => seedSkillEvidenceLearner(db));
  });
  test.afterAll(async () => {
    if (t) await withDb((db) => db.query("select english.eng_purge_learner($1, null)", [t.learnerId]));
  });

  test("An's tutor: 'Tính lại từ đầu' turns three checkpoints into 'Đang tiến bộ' + two proposals 'Tự làm được'; a second run adds none", async ({ page }) => {
    await loginAs(page, "tutorAn");
    await page.goto(targets());
    await expect(page.getByTestId(/^skill-row-/)).toHaveCount(12);
    await rebuild(page, 2);
    for (const k of E2E_SKILLS) {
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-scale")).toHaveAttribute("data-level", "2");
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-pending")).toHaveText(skillStateCopy.pending(levelLabels.INDEPENDENT.label));
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-auto")).toBeVisible();
    }
    await rebuild(page, 0);
  });

  test("the parent: tab 'Kỹ năng' shows both proposals read-only; her confirm is refused (403); no manual form for her", async ({ page }) => {
    await loginAs(page, "parent");
    await page.goto("/review");
    await page.getByTestId("review-tab-skills").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/review\/skills$/);
    const group = page.getByTestId(`skills-group-${t.learnerId}`);
    await expect(group.getByTestId(/^skill-proposal-[A-Z]/)).toHaveCount(2); // the cards, not "skill-proposal-title"
    await expect(group.getByTestId("skill-waiting")).toHaveCount(2);
    await expect(group.getByTestId("skill-confirm")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId(`skill-confirm-all-${t.learnerId}`)).toHaveCount(0);
    const ids = await openProposals();
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(2);
    expect((await page.request.post(`/api/learners/${t.learnerId}/skills/confirm-all`, { data: { client_id: randomUUID(), ids } })).status()).toBe(403);
    expect((await page.request.post(`/api/skills/decisions/${ids[0]}`, { data: { client_id: randomUUID(), action: "confirm" } })).status()).toBe(403);
    expect(await openProposals()).toEqual(ids);
    await page.goto(targets());
    await expect(page.getByTestId(/^skill-manual-/)).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByTestId("skills-rebuild")).toBeVisible(); // the parent may rebuild (D18)
  });

  test("Nguyên's tutor sees nothing of this child: page and API 404, no group in the tab", async ({ page }) => {
    await loginAs(page, "tutorNguyen");
    expect((await page.goto(targets()))?.status()).toBe(404);
    expect((await page.request.get(`/api/learners/${t.learnerId}/skills`)).status()).toBe(404);
    expect((await page.request.post(`/api/learners/${t.learnerId}/skills/rebuild`, { data: {} })).status()).toBe(404);
    await page.goto("/review/skills");
    await expect(page.getByTestId(`skills-group-${t.learnerId}`)).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("An's tutor: conditions and the three pieces of work open; 'Chưa' needs a reason; a rejected proposal does not come back on a rebuild", async ({ page }) => {
    await loginAs(page, "tutorAn");
    await page.goto("/review/skills");
    const cause = card(page, CAUSE);
    await cause.getByTestId("skill-conditions").locator("summary").click();
    await expect(cause.getByTestId("condition-r1_first_attempts")).toBeVisible();
    const links = cause.getByTestId("skill-evidence-link");
    await expect(links).toHaveCount(3);
    expect((await links.evaluateAll((as) => as.map((a) => a.getAttribute("href")))).sort()).toEqual(t.episodes.map((e) => `/review/${e}`).sort());

    await cause.getByTestId("skill-reject-open").click();
    await cause.getByTestId("skill-reject-send").click();
    await expect(cause.getByTestId("skill-msg")).toHaveText(skillStateCopy.reasonRequired);
    await cause.getByTestId("skill-reject-reason").fill("Bài thứ ba vẫn dựa nhiều vào câu mẫu.");
    await cause.getByTestId("skill-reject-send").click();
    await expect(card(page, CAUSE)).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(card(page, PARA)).toHaveCount(1);

    // a piece of work the proposal cites opens for the tutor
    expect((await page.goto(`/review/${t.episodes[0]}`))?.status()).toBe(200);

    await page.goto(targets());
    await expect(row(page, CAUSE).getByTestId("skill-pending")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(row(page, CAUSE).getByTestId("skill-scale")).toHaveAttribute("data-level", "2"); // "Chưa" never lowers the level
    await rebuild(page, 0);
    await expect(row(page, CAUSE).getByTestId("skill-pending")).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("new work after 'Chưa' brings the proposal back; 'Xác nhận tất cả (2)' makes both 'Tự làm được'; Hồ sơ cấp counts them", async ({ page }) => {
    await withDb((db) => insertValidatedEpisode(db, t.learnerId, t.tutorMemberId, { activityId: t.steps.extra, daysAgo: 2, scores: { [CAUSE]: 3 }, facets: ["checkpoint"] }));
    await loginAs(page, "tutorAn");
    await page.goto(targets());
    await rebuild(page, 1);

    await page.goto("/review/skills");
    const all = page.getByTestId(`skill-confirm-all-${t.learnerId}`);
    await expect(all).toHaveText(skillStateCopy.confirmAll(2));
    await all.click();
    await expect(page.getByTestId(`skills-group-${t.learnerId}`)).toHaveCount(0);

    await page.goto(targets());
    for (const k of E2E_SKILLS) {
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-scale")).toHaveAttribute("data-level", "3");
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-pending")).toHaveCount(0);
      await expect(row(page, k).getByTestId("skill-auto")).toHaveCount(0); // level 3 came from a person, not the app
    }
    await page.goto(`/learners/${t.learnerId}/levels?domain=writing`);
    await expect(page.getByTestId("level-solid-writing")).toHaveText(/^2\/\d+ điểm đã vững$/);
    await expect(page.getByTestId("level-solid")).toContainText(/2\/\d+ điểm đã vững/);
  });
});
```

```markdown
### 19.10. Trạng thái từng kỹ năng (S7a, migration 0022 + 0023)

- **Ngưỡng:** `AP-0.3` (`content/assessment_policy_AP-0.3.json`, sinh `0022_seed_ap_0_3.sql` bằng `gen:config -- --new`) = AP-0.2 + `receptiveAuto` (cách 2, P3-b) + `levelGate: null` (để dành S7b) + `developing.minScore = 3`. Danh mục đã khóa vẫn trỏ AP-0.2; engine đọc `engine_policy_map` (chỉ thêm, `0.2.1-draft → AP-0.3`).
- **Engine:** `lib/engine/computeTargetState.ts` — hàm thuần, `ENGINE_VERSION = "S7a-1"`, chỉ import tương đối. Vào: chính sách, bài đã duyệt (điểm hiện hành), câu tự chấm, lần luyện, sổ quyết định. Ra: `auto` (1–2), `propose` (3–4), `raiseFlag`, điều kiện từng bậc, bằng chứng, `evidenceKey` (FNV-1a 64 trên id đã sắp xếp, `<số>-<16 hex>`). Ngày = ngày lịch Việt Nam. Không bao giờ hạ bậc.
- **Sổ `mastery_decisions`** (chỉ thêm, `eng_forbid_mutation`): CHECK ép AUTO chỉ lên 1–2, PROPOSED chỉ lên 3–4, ⚑ giữ bậc (≥ 2), MANUAL chỉ về 1–2 có lý do, CONFIRMED / REJECTED trả lời đúng 1 dòng (`responds_to` unique). Một đề xuất mở cho mỗi (em, kỹ năng); sau REJECTED không đề xuất lại cho tới khi `evidence_key` đổi.
- **`competency_state`** = dẫn xuất: bậc theo dòng AUTO / CONFIRMED / MANUAL mới nhất (`seq`), ⚑ và đề xuất đang mở, điều kiện lần tính gần nhất. "Tính lại từ đầu" (`eng_reset_skill_states`) dựng lại từ sổ + bài làm, kết quả giống hệt; không tạo đề xuất trùng.
- **Chạy:** sau mỗi lần ghi thành công (duyệt S5, sửa quyết định, hỗ trợ quan sát, bài ngoài app, em nộp bài, S6 Xong / Vòng 2) — đồng bộ, fail-soft (`app_errors` `engine_recompute`), chưa áp 0023 thì bỏ qua.
- **Hàm (service_role sau `requireAccess`):** `eng_engine_inputs`, `eng_apply_engine_results`, `eng_decide_skill` (Xác nhận / Chưa / Giữ bậc — chỉ thầy/cô của em), `eng_confirm_skill_proposals` (Xác nhận tất cả, không bao giờ ⚑), `eng_set_skill_state` (sửa tay 1–2), `eng_reset_skill_states` (thầy/cô hoặc phụ huynh), đọc `eng_skill_states`, `eng_skill_inbox`. Không có hàm nào cho học sinh. Xóa dữ liệu một em xóa cả hai bảng.
- **App:** `lib/engine/` (engine, runner, hook), `lib/skills/` (API, view), Hộp duyệt → tab "Kỹ năng" (`/review/skills`), Kỹ năng đang theo dõi (thang 4 ô, ⚑, đang chờ xác nhận, sửa tay, tính lại), Hồ sơ cấp (Đã vững · n/N, Đang luyện, Còn cần).
```

```markdown
## S7a — Trạng thái từng kỹ năng (23/10/2026)

App bắt đầu **tự tính bậc từng kỹ năng** từ bài đã duyệt. Bậc 1–2 app tự cập nhật; bậc 3–4 app chỉ **đề xuất**, thầy/cô của em bấm **Xác nhận** hoặc **Chưa** (bắt buộc lý do). Không bao giờ tự hạ bậc: bài gần đây kém hơn thì hiện ⚑ "Cần thầy/cô xem lại". Học sinh không thấy gì mới.

### Phụ huynh / gia sư làm được gì bây giờ

| Việc | Ở đâu |
|---|---|
| Xem đề xuất lên bậc "Tự làm được" / "Vận dụng được" và kỹ năng có ⚑; mở "Xem điều kiện" và từng bài làm được tính | Hộp duyệt → tab **Kỹ năng** |
| Thầy/cô: **Xác nhận** / **Chưa** (lý do) / **Xác nhận tất cả (n)**; với ⚑: **Giữ bậc** / **Sửa bậc** | Hộp duyệt → tab Kỹ năng |
| Thang 4 ô có chữ cho 12 kỹ năng, ⚑, "Đang chờ xác nhận: …", "App tự cập nhật"; thầy/cô **Sửa tay bậc 1–2** (lý do); **Tính lại từ đầu** (thầy/cô hoặc phụ huynh) | Kỹ năng đang theo dõi |
| "Đã vững · n/N điểm đã vững", "Đang luyện", "Để lên cấp tiếp, còn cần" theo từng mảng | Hồ sơ cấp |

Phụ huynh xem được hết nhưng **không** xác nhận bậc 3–4 (chỉ thầy/cô của em).

### SQL cần áp (đúng thứ tự)

1. **`db/migrations/0022_seed_ap_0_3.sql`** — ngưỡng AP-0.3 (sinh tự động bằng `gen:config`).
2. **`db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql`** — sổ quyết định + trạng thái + các hàm + bản mới của hàm xóa dữ liệu một em (giữ mọi dòng cũ) + luật chữ "điểm" thu hẹp (sửa đổi A).

Actions → **Apply database migrations** (gõ APPLY) hoặc dán vào SQL Editor. **Áp cả hai TRƯỚC khi thử preview hoặc gộp PR**; cần `0019`–`0021` đã áp. Chỉ thêm; chạy lại an toàn. Nên bấm chạy tay *Weekly backup* trước. Chưa áp thì tab Kỹ năng báo "chưa sẵn sàng", các trang khác như cũ. Migration kế tiếp: **0024**.

### Cách thử trên preview (gia đình thử)

1. `thu-giasu-an` → Hộp duyệt → mở bài viết của An (thử) → chấm `Viết "vì sao – nên"` 3/4 → lưu.
2. Vẫn tài khoản đó → thẻ An (thử) → **Kỹ năng đang theo dõi**: kỹ năng đó có ít nhất 1 ô tô và dòng "App tự cập nhật".
3. Bấm **Tính lại từ đầu** → thấy "Đã tính lại. Không có đề xuất mới." (bấm lại bao nhiêu lần cũng không tạo trùng).
4. **Sửa tay bậc 1–2** → chọn bậc, để trống lý do → thấy lời nhắc; ghi lý do → lưu → thang đổi.
5. Phụ huynh → Hộp duyệt → tab **Kỹ năng**: thấy "Phụ huynh xem; chỉ thầy/cô của em xác nhận."; không có nút Xác nhận.
6. `thu-an` → trang chủ: không có thang, bậc, ⚑ hay chữ "Đang chờ xác nhận".

Đề xuất lên "Tự làm được" cần ≥ 3 bài tự làm lần đầu ở ≥ 2 chủ đề trải ≥ 7 ngày, nên trên preview mới chưa thấy; CI kiểm luồng đó với một em thử có bài lùi ngày (e2e "S7a skill states").

### Sửa đổi A (luật chữ "điểm", phụ huynh duyệt trong spec 23/10)

Góp ý cho con giờ chỉ từ chối **hai** nhãn trên của thang 0–4: "Đạt yêu cầu", "Vững, rõ ràng". "Chưa thể hiện", "Còn thiếu nhiều", "Được một phần" là chữ thường lại ("con làm được một phần" được phép). Bốn loại kia (phân số kiểu điểm, "6 điểm", "8 trên 10", "80%") giữ nguyên.

### Quyết định trong kế hoạch (phụ huynh xem lại)

D1–D23 trong `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states.md` (bảng "Decisions"). Các mục đánh dấu §17 (cách đếm chi tiết: D4–D10, D12) là **Claude đề xuất, phụ huynh xác nhận**.

### Câu hỏi cho phụ huynh (G1–G4)

Xem mục "Spec gaps" của kế hoạch; ghi câu trả lời ở đây khi có.

### Tiêu chí đạt

`typecheck`, `lint`, `test` (gồm test database: ép không hạ bậc, một đề xuất mở, Chưa → không đề xuất lại tới khi có bài mới, tính lại giống hệt, ai được làm gì, xóa dữ liệu), `gen:config` xanh; CI (E2E + áp migration hai lần) xanh; review Opus; phụ huynh áp `0022` rồi `0023` và làm 6 bước thử ở trên.
```

## Amendments — parent decisions 23/10/2026 ("ok mockup S7a", then "ok r" to G1–G4 and D4–D12)

Task 0 passed: the parent approved mockup board 18 (Hộp duyệt → tab "Kỹ năng"). D4, D5, D6, D8, D9, D10 and D12 are confirmed under DESIGN §17 exactly as written. The answers below override the tasks wherever they conflict. Task 12 must record them in PROGRESS and in the PR.

- **G1 (keep as planned).** Level 2 needs an unaided first attempt ≥ 3/4 (`developing.minScore = 3` in AP-0.3). An unaided 2/4 stays level 1.

- **G2 (change).** Auto-checked items can lift a skill to **level 2** before route 2 is complete.
  - **Config:** add to AP-0.3 `receptiveAuto` the keys `developingMinItems: 4` and `developingMinAccuracyPct: 75`. Update the zod schema and the generated `0022` seed, and regenerate it with `gen:config -- --new` before anything is committed. 0022 is not applied anywhere yet.
  - **Rule:** a skill in the reading, listening, grammar or vocab domain reaches level 2 (kind `AUTO`, no confirmation) when it has ≥ 4 counted items (D9 counting) at ≥ 75 % accuracy. Fewer items, or lower accuracy with ≥ 1 correct, gives level 1.
  - **Scope:** route 1 and the tutor-scored rules are unchanged. Route 2 is still needed for level 3.
  - **Tests:** add engine unit tests for both boundaries (3 items vs 4; 74 % vs 75 %).

- **G3 (keep as planned).** ⚑ cards offer "Giữ bậc" and "Sửa bậc". "Sửa bậc" is the manual form, can lower a skill, needs a reason, and is tutor only. The app itself never lowers a level.

- **G4 (change).** Let the tutor mark transfer work so level 4 can happen now.
  - **UI:** on the S5 review page of an episode (`/review/[episodeId]`), add a checkbox "Bài này ở chủ đề / nguồn mới (vận dụng)". The copy goes in `vi.ts`. Only a reviewer allowed by D15 for that episode sees it, and only on first attempts that are not revisions.
  - **DB:** a new security-definer function in 0023 records it **append-only**. Do not update `episodes.facets`, since episodes are append-only. Either:
    - write a row to a new append-only table `episode_marks(episode_id, mark 'transfer_check', set_by, reason null, created_at)`, or
    - write a `validation_decisions` facet, if that table's design allows it without touching `first_once`.

    Pick the simplest correct option and note it.
  - **Engine:** treat an episode as `transfer_check` when the template facet says so **or** such a mark exists. C7 still applies: a new source counts as transfer, not as delayed.
  - **Purge/preview:** include the new table.
  - **Tests:** a DB test (only an allowed reviewer can mark; the student and the other tutor are refused) and an engine test (a mark plus a delayed check reaches level 4).

## Amendments — Opus review fixes (23/10/2026)

These override the tasks above where they conflict. `0023` is not applied anywhere, so it was edited in place; `0022` is unchanged (AP-0.3 did not change).

- **G4 narrowed (S1).** The "chủ đề / nguồn mới" mark is for the child's **tutor only** (parent's intent, spec item 4, D18): `eng_mark_episode_transfer` requires `eng_review_role = 'tutor'` (active relationship); the checkbox is shown only to that tutor. A template step that already has `transfer_check` shows it ticked and read-only (N4).
- **Proposal keys (B1, S3).** "Already proposed" is `(to_state, evidence_key)`. The key is the evidence the level's conditions use: route 1 = the counted pool's validation decisions; route 2 = the counted attempts; level 4 = delayed + transfer decisions + the effective transfer-mark ids. `EngineResult.proposeKey` / `ApplyRow.propose_key`; unique index per `to_state`, ⚑ has its own index.
- **Hand-set levels hold (B2).** `MANUAL` rows store the engine's global evidence key (all validated decisions + auto-checked attempts + transfer-mark ids). While the latest state row is a `MANUAL` with the same key, the engine writes no AUTO / PROPOSED (TS `held`, SQL `v_held`). `eng_set_skill_state(..., p_evidence_key)`; the manual handler computes it with `evidenceKeyFor`.
- **Smaller fixes.** S2 flag window from `max(submitted, validated)`; S4 manual form follows the level; S5 route-2 review always needs the 14-day gap; N2 `competency_state.inputs_at`; N3 soft state loading on the two pages; N5 `auto_route`; N7 guard for 0022; N8 `server-only` first.
- **Deferred (PROGRESS "Để sau / Hỏi phụ huynh"):** N1 (⚑ on one short quiz), N6 (a re-assigned template counts as a new activity).
